Thinking in Blender: Staged Executable Inverse Graphics with Vision-Language Models
Authors: Guangzhao He, Rundong Luo, Wei-Chiu Ma, Hadar Averbuch-Elor
First: 2026-06-01T17:59:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:59:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inverse graphics is a longstanding and highly underconstrained problem that seeks to reconstruct images as editable 3D scenes which can be rendered, relit, and manipulated. In this work, we investigate whether pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) can perform executable inverse graphics directly from a single image by reconstructing a scene as an editable Blender program, without relying on specialized 2D or 3D foundation models, differentiable rendering, or multi-view supervision. We introduce Staged Executable Inverse Graphics (SEIG), an agentic framework that reconstructs a 3D scene from a single image by progressively refining scene factors including geometry, materials, composition, and lighting directly in executable Blender code space. We evaluate our framework across diverse scenes using a range of reconstruction metrics spanning pixel-level, perceptual, and semantic fidelity. Our experiments show that staged reconstruction substantially improves reconstruction fidelity, highlighting the importance of task decomposition for executable inverse graphics with general-purpose VLMs. Finally, we showcase various downstream applications enabled by the reconstructed editable Blender scenes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在Blender中思考：基于视觉-语言模型的分阶段可执行逆图形
逆图形是一个长期存在且高度欠约束的问题，旨在将图像重建为可编辑的3D场景，以便进行渲染、重光照和操作。本研究探讨预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）是否能直接从单张图像重建场景为可编辑的Blender程序，而无需依赖专门的2D或3D基础模型、可微渲染或多视角监督。我们提出了分阶段可执行逆图形（SEIG），一种代理框架，通过在可执行的Blender代码空间中逐步优化几何、材质、构图和光照等场景因素，从单张图像重建3D场景。我们在多种场景中评估了该框架，使用涵盖像素级、感知和语义保真度的多种重建指标。实验表明，分阶段重建显著提升了重建保真度，突显了任务分解对通用VLMs执行逆图形的重要性。最后，我们展示了由重建的可编辑Blender场景所支持的各种下游应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of reconstructing editable 3D scenes from single images without specialized models or multi-view data, this work explores whether pretrained vision-language models can directly generate executable Blender code. The method, called Staged Executable Inverse Graphics (SEIG), progressively refines scene elements—geometry, materials, composition, and lighting—in Blender’s code space to produce a fully renderable and manipulable 3D scene. Experimental results demonstrate that staged reconstruction significantly improves fidelity across pixel-level, perceptual, and semantic metrics, underscoring the value of task decomposition for general-purpose VLMs in executable inverse graphics.
为了解决从单张图像重建可编辑3D场景的难题，且无需专用模型或多视角监督，本研究探索预训练视觉-语言模型是否能直接生成可执行的Blender代码。方法名为“分阶段可执行逆向图形”（SEIG），通过逐步优化场景要素——几何、材质、构图与光照——在Blender代码空间中生成可渲染和操作的3D场景。实验结果表明，分阶段重建显著提升了像素级、感知与语义层面的重建精度，凸显了任务分解对通用视觉-语言模型执行逆向图形任务的重要性。
Mitigating Perceptual Judgment Bias in Multimodal LLM-as-a-Judge via Perceptual Perturbation and Reward Modeling
Authors: Seojeong Park, Jiho Choi, Junyong Kang, Seonho Lee, Jaeyo Shin, Hyunjung Shim
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-01T17:59:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:59:46+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent multimodal large language models have demonstrated strong reasoning ability, yet their reliability as automated evaluators remains limited by a critical weakness: when visual evidence conflicts with textual cues, MLLM judges tend to reward plausible narratives over perceptually correct answers. We identify and systematically analyze this phenomenon, which we term Perceptual Judgment Bias. Through controlled visual perturbations, existing multimodal judges frequently anchor on the response text instead of their own visual perception, leading to inconsistent and non-verifiable evaluations. To address this issue, we introduce the Perceptually Perturbed Judgment Dataset, which constructs minimally edited counterfactual responses that isolate perceptual errors and enable verifiable supervision. Building on this dataset, we develop a unified training framework that combines a structured GRPO-based reward with a batch-ranking objective, achieving coherent global ordering without explicit pairwise labels. Experiments across diverse MLLM-as-a-Judge benchmarks show that our approach substantially improves perceptual fidelity, ranking coherence, and alignment with human evaluation. Our results establish a scalable and generalizable pathway for training multimodal judges that are perceptually grounded, interpretable, and robust to visual-reasoning conflicts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过感知扰动与奖励建模缓解多模态大语言模型作为裁判时的感知判断偏差
近期多模态大语言模型展现了强大的推理能力，但其作为自动化评估者的可靠性受限于一个关键弱点：当视觉证据与文本线索冲突时，多模态大语言模型裁判倾向于奖励合乎逻辑的叙述而非感知上正确的答案。我们识别并系统分析了这一现象，称之为“感知判断偏差”。通过受控的视觉扰动，现有多模态裁判常依赖响应文本而非自身视觉感知，导致评估结果不一致且不可验证。为解决此问题，我们引入了“感知扰动判断数据集”，该数据集构建了最小编辑的反事实响应，以隔离感知错误并实现可验证监督。基于该数据集，我们开发了一种统一训练框架，结合基于结构化GRPO的奖励与批量排序目标，在无需显式成对标签的情况下实现全局排序的一致性。在多个多模态大语言模型作为裁判的基准测试中，我们的方法显著提升了感知保真度、排序一致性及与人类评估的一致性。我们的成果为训练感知基础、可解释且对视觉推理冲突鲁棒的多模态裁判提供了一条可扩展且通用的路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the tendency of multimodal LLMs to favor plausible text narratives over perceptually accurate visual evidence, this work introduces Perceptual Judgment Bias and proposes a solution via perceptual perturbation and reward modeling. The method constructs a counterfactual dataset with minimally altered responses to isolate perceptual errors, then trains a unified reward model using GRPO-based structured rewards and batch-ranking objectives to enforce global coherence without pairwise labels. Experimental results across multiple MLLM-as-a-Judge benchmarks demonstrate significant improvements in perceptual fidelity, ranking consistency, and alignment with human judgments, establishing a scalable framework for perceptually grounded multimodal evaluators.
为解决多模态大语言模型在视觉与文本信息冲突时倾向于选择合理叙事而非感知正确答案的问题，本文提出“感知判断偏差”概念，并通过感知扰动与奖励建模方法进行缓解。方法构建了最小编辑的反事实响应数据集，以隔离感知错误，并结合基于GRPO的结构化奖励与批排名目标，实现全局排序的一致性。实验表明，该方法在多个多模态判断基准上显著提升了感知准确性、排序一致性及与人类评价的一致性，为训练感知可靠、可解释且抗视觉推理冲突的多模态评估器提供了可扩展的通用路径。
RoboDream: Compositional World Models for Scalable Robot Data Synthesis
Authors: Junjie Ye, Rong Xue, Basile Van Hoorick, Runhao Li, Harshitha Rajaprakash, Pavel Tokmakov, Muhammad Zubair Irshad, Vitor Guizilini, Yue Wang
First: 2026-06-01T17:59:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:59:38+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://junjieye.com/RoboDream/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scaling robot learning requires large-scale, diverse demonstrations, yet real-world data collection via teleoperation remains prohibitively expensive and time-consuming. While video diffusion models offer a promising avenue for data scaling, existing generative approaches are often limited to superficial visual augmentation, or suffer from embodiment hallucinations that yield physically infeasible motions. We present a generalizable embodiment-centric world model that achieves scalable data generation by synthesizing photorealistic demonstrations with novel objects, in novel scenes, and from novel viewpoints. Our approach anchors generation to rendered robot motion while conditioning on explicit scene and object priors, effectively decoupling trajectory execution from environment synthesis. This formulation has the potential to unlock two powerful data scaling capabilities: (1) retrieval and rebirth, which repurposes existing trajectories into entirely new contexts without new motion data; and (2) prop-free teleoperation, where operators manipulate empty air and the model hallucinates the target objects and scene afterwards, eliminating reset time. We demonstrate with real-world experiments that our generated data consistently improves downstream policy performance and significantly reduces real-world data requirements across diverse manipulation tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoboDream：用于可扩展机器人数据合成的组合式世界模型
扩展机器人学习需要大规模、多样化的演示数据，但通过远程操作收集真实世界数据仍过于昂贵且耗时。尽管视频扩散模型为数据扩展提供了有前景的途径，现有生成方法常局限于表面视觉增强，或存在具身幻觉问题，导致产生物理上不可行的动作。我们提出一种通用的具身中心世界模型，通过合成逼真的演示数据，涵盖新物体、新场景和新视角，实现可扩展的数据生成。该方法将生成锚定在渲染的机器人运动上，同时结合显式的场景与物体先验，有效解耦轨迹执行与环境合成。该框架有望解锁两种强大的数据扩展能力：（1）检索与重生，即无需新运动数据，即可将现有轨迹重用于全新上下文；（2）无道具远程操作，操作者仅需操控空域，模型随后幻觉生成目标物体与场景，从而消除重置时间。我们在真实实验中证明，所生成数据持续提升下游策略性能，并显著降低各类操作任务所需的真实数据量。
Summary / 总结
To address the high cost and inefficiency of real-world robot data collection, this work introduces RoboDream, a compositional world model that synthesizes photorealistic robot demonstrations with novel objects, scenes, and viewpoints by grounding generation in rendered robot motion and conditioning on explicit scene and object priors. This decoupling enables two key capabilities: 'retrieval and rebirth,' which reuses existing trajectories in new contexts without new motion data, and 'prop-free teleoperation,' where operators manipulate empty space and the model hallucinates objects and scenes, eliminating reset time. Experiments show that generated data consistently improves downstream policy performance and reduces real-world data needs across diverse manipulation tasks.
为解决真实世界机器人数据采集成本高、效率低的问题，本文提出RoboDream，一种基于渲染机器人运动并结合显式场景与物体先验条件的组合式世界模型，用于合成逼真的机器人演示。该方法通过解耦轨迹执行与环境合成，实现两项关键数据扩展能力：‘检索与重生’可复用已有轨迹于新场景无需新运动数据，‘无道具遥操作’允许操作者在空无一物的空间中交互，模型随后生成目标物体与场景，消除重置时间。实验表明，生成数据在多种操作任务中持续提升下游策略性能，并显著降低对真实世界数据的需求。
ProtoAda: Prototype-Guided Adaptive Adapter Expansion and Geometric Consolidation for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning
Authors: Yu-Cheng Shi, Zhen-Hao Xie, Jun-Tao Tang, Da-Wei Zhou
First: 2026-06-01T17:59:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:59:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance through instruction tuning, but real-world deployment requires them to continually acquire new vision-language capabilities, making Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) essential. To reduce inter-task interference and promote collaboration, recent methods often employ sparse architectures like Mixture of LoRA Experts with image-text similarity routing. However, tasks with distinct response structures could share highly similar visual-linguistic semantics and thus be wrongly routed to the same expert; image-text similarity alone is insufficient for reliable task assignment. For example, an expert in a grounding task requiring coordinate prediction may be biased toward producing short textual answers after learning semantically similar VQA tasks. This format-blind task assignment integrates heterogeneous response types into shared parameters, inducing gradient interference and ineffective expert collaboration. To address this problem, we propose ProtoAda, a prototype-guided adaptive tuning framework. ProtoAda introduces format-aware task prototypes to align task assignment and routing with both task semantics and output structure, and further consolidates format-compatible updates in a geometry-aware manner to effectively reuse and progressively refine existing parameters. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that ProtoAda achieves superior performance, especially on tasks whose answer structures are easily corrupted by sequential tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ProtoAda：基于原型引导的自适应适配器扩展与几何整合的多模态持续指令调优
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通过指令调优实现强大性能，但在实际部署中需持续获取新的视觉-语言能力，因此多模态持续指令调优（MCIT）至关重要。为减少任务间干扰并促进协作，近期方法常采用稀疏架构（如基于图像-文本相似度路由的LoRA专家混合）。然而，具有不同响应结构的任务可能共享高度相似的视觉-语言语义，从而被错误路由至同一专家；仅凭图像-文本相似度不足以可靠分配任务。例如，一个需坐标预测的定位任务专家，在学习语义相似的视觉问答任务后，可能偏向生成简短文本答案。这种格式盲的分配将异构响应类型整合至共享参数，导致梯度干扰与专家协作无效。为解决此问题，我们提出ProtoAda，一种原型引导的自适应调优框架。ProtoAda引入格式感知任务原型，使任务分配与路由同时契合任务语义与输出结构，并进一步以几何感知方式整合格式兼容的更新，有效复用并逐步优化现有参数。在多个基准上的广泛实验表明，ProtoAda表现卓越，尤其在答案结构易受序列调优破坏的任务中。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of task interference in multimodal continual instruction tuning, ProtoAda introduces format-aware task prototypes to guide adaptive routing and parameter consolidation, addressing the limitation of image-text similarity alone in assigning tasks to experts. The method dynamically aligns task assignment with both semantic content and output structure, then consolidates compatible updates geometrically to preserve and refine existing knowledge. Experiments show ProtoAda outperforms baseline methods, particularly on tasks where sequential tuning corrupts answer formats.
为减少梯度干扰并提升多模态持续指令调优中的专家协作，ProtoAda引入格式感知的任务原型，依据语义与输出结构引导自适应路由，避免因格式盲导致的参数污染。它进一步以几何方式整合兼容更新，逐步优化共享参数。实验表明，ProtoAda在任务输出格式易受顺序调优破坏的场景中表现显著优于现有方法。
From Zero to Hero: Training-Free Custom Concept Spawning in World Models
Authors: Kiymet Akdemir, Pinar Yanardag
First: 2026-06-01T17:59:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:59:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autoregressive world models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for interactive video generation, allowing users to navigate dynamically generated environments through actions. These models are typically conditioned on a text prompt and/or a single reference frame, from which the entire world is generated. Yet the moment the user navigates beyond what is visible in that frame, the unseen regions are populated by the base model's priors, with no mechanism for the user to specify what should appear and where. This is a fundamental limitation for applications such as gaming, interactive storytelling, and simulation, where controllable scene composition is essential. We refer to this missing capability as concept spawning; introducing a user-specified visual concept into a world model, analogous to spawning in a game engine. We introduce SPAWN (Swapping Pinned Anchor with Windowed iNjection), a training-free method for concept spawning. SPAWN exploits a structural property of image-to-video backbones: the first slot of the context memory is pinned to the reference frame and acts as a foundational anchor for every generated chunk. By swapping this anchor with an external concept latent over a short injection window and letting the original anchor return, we cause the concept to propagate naturally through the rollout via the model's own memory. SPAWN supports concepts from fine-grained entities such as characters and props to large-scale elements such as buildings and landmarks, and accepts either a concept image or a text description as input. Experiments show that SPAWN integrates concepts with consistent lighting, scale, and perspective while preserving identity and temporal coherence, demonstrating that controllable concept spawning is achievable in existing autoregressive world models without any training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从零到英雄：世界模型中无需训练的自定义概念生成
自回归世界模型已成为交互式视频生成的强大范式，允许用户通过动作在动态生成的环境中导航。这些模型通常以文本提示和/或单帧参考图像为条件，从而生成整个世界。然而，当用户导航超出该帧可见范围时，不可见区域将由基础模型的先验填充，用户无法指定应出现什么内容及位置。这在游戏、互动叙事和仿真等需要可控场景构成的应用中是根本性限制。我们将此缺失能力称为概念生成，即向世界模型中引入用户指定的视觉概念，类似于游戏引擎中的“生成”。我们提出SPAWN（交换固定锚点与窗口注入），一种无需训练的概念生成方法。SPAWN利用图像到视频骨干网络的结构特性：上下文记忆的第一个槽位固定为参考帧，作为每个生成块的基础锚点。通过在短暂注入窗口内将该锚点替换为外部概念潜变量，再让原锚点恢复，使概念自然通过模型自身记忆传播。SPAWN支持从细粒度实体（如角色和道具）到大规模元素（如建筑和地标）的概念，输入可为概念图像或文本描述。实验表明，SPAWN在保持身份与时间一致性的同时，实现光照、尺度和视角的一致性，证明无需训练即可在现有自回归世界模型中实现可控概念生成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for user-controlled scene composition in interactive video generation, this paper addresses the limitation that existing autoregressive world models cannot introduce new visual concepts beyond the initial reference frame. The authors propose SPAWN, a training-free method that leverages the pinned reference frame in image-to-video backbones as a memory anchor. By temporarily replacing this anchor with a user-provided concept latent over a short window and allowing it to revert, SPAWN enables natural propagation of the concept through the generated sequence. Experiments demonstrate that SPAWN successfully integrates diverse concepts—from characters to landmarks—while preserving lighting, scale, perspective, identity, and temporal coherence, proving controllable concept spawning is feasible without retraining.
为解决自回归世界模型在交互式视频生成中无法在初始参考帧之外引入用户指定视觉概念的问题，本文提出SPAWN方法，该方法利用图像到视频骨干网络中固定锚定的参考帧，通过短暂替换为用户提供的概念潜在表示，实现概念注入。实验表明，SPAWN可成功将从角色到地标等不同尺度概念融入生成视频，同时保持光照、比例、透视、身份与时间一致性，证明无需训练即可实现可控概念生成。
Paradoxical noise preference in RNNs
Authors: Noah Eckstein, Manoj Srinivasan
Venue: Transactions on Machine Learning Research, 2026
First: 2026-01-08T03:11:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:58:45+00:00
Comments: Published in Transactions on Machine Learning Research (TMLR), 2026 21 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In recurrent neural networks (RNNs) used to model biological neural networks, noise is typically introduced during training to emulate biological variability and regularize learning. The expectation is that removing the noise at test time should preserve or improve performance. Contrary to this intuition, we find that continuous-time RNNs (CTRNNs) often perform best at or near the training noise level. This noise preference typically arises when noise is injected inside the neural activation function; networks trained with noise injected outside the activation function perform best with zero noise. The phenomenon arises robustly in diverse tasks for large enough training noise; we also show the phenomenon arising in feedforward neural networks, not just in RNNs. Our analyses show that the phenomenon stems from noise-induced shifts of fixed points (stationary distributions) in the underlying stochastic dynamics of the RNNs. These fixed point shifts are noise-level dependent and bias the network outputs when the noise is removed, degrading performance. Analytical and numerical results show that the bias arises when neural states operate near activation-function nonlinearities, where noise is asymmetrically attenuated, and that performance optimization incentivizes operation near these nonlinearities; such performance incentives exist for networks with noise inside, but not outside, the activation function, explaining why only noise-in networks show the preference. Thus, networks can overfit to the training noise itself rather than just to the input-output data. The phenomenon is distinct from stochastic resonance, wherein nonzero noise enhances signal processing. Our findings reveal that training noise can become an integral part of the computation learned by neural networks, with implications for understanding neural population dynamics and for the design of robust artificial RNNs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RNNs中的悖论性噪声偏好
在用于建模生物神经网络的循环神经网络（RNNs）中，训练时通常引入噪声以模拟生物变异性并正则化学习。人们预期测试时移除噪声应能保持或提升性能。然而，我们发现连续时间RNNs（CTRNNs）通常在训练噪声水平或其附近表现最佳。这种噪声偏好通常出现在噪声注入神经激活函数内部时；而噪声注入激活函数外部的网络则在零噪声下表现最佳。该现象在足够大的训练噪声下于多种任务中稳健出现，我们还证明其同样存在于前馈神经网络中，不仅限于RNNs。我们的分析表明，该现象源于RNNs底层随机动力学中固定点（稳态分布）的噪声诱导偏移。这些固定点偏移依赖于噪声水平，当移除噪声时会偏置网络输出，从而降低性能。分析与数值结果表明，当神经状态运行在激活函数非线性区域附近时，噪声会非对称衰减，而性能优化会激励网络在此区域运行；这种性能激励仅存在于激活函数内部注入噪声的网络中，解释了为何仅此类网络表现出偏好。因此，网络可能过拟合于训练噪声本身，而不仅限于输入-输出数据。该现象不同于随机共振，后者中非零噪声可增强信号处理。我们的发现揭示了训练噪声可能成为神经网络所学习计算的核心组成部分，对理解神经群体动力学及设计鲁棒人工RNNs具有重要意义。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates why recurrent neural networks (RNNs) sometimes perform better when test-time noise matches training noise, contrary to the expectation that noise removal should improve generalization. The authors find that this 'paradoxical noise preference' arises when noise is injected inside the activation function, causing noise-induced shifts in the fixed points of the network’s stochastic dynamics, which bias outputs when noise is removed. Networks trained with noise outside the activation function avoid this bias and perform best with zero noise. The phenomenon occurs robustly across tasks and even in feedforward networks, and stems from performance incentives that push neural states toward nonlinear activation regions where noise asymmetry degrades output accuracy. This suggests RNNs can overfit to training noise itself, not just input-output mappings, and differs from stochastic resonance.
该研究探讨了为何递归神经网络（RNN）在测试时保留训练噪声反而表现最佳，这与通常认为无噪声推理应更优的直觉相悖。作者发现，当噪声注入激活函数内部时，会引发底层随机动力学的固定点偏移，导致去除噪声后输出偏差，从而降低性能；而噪声在激活函数外部注入的网络则无此问题，表现最佳。该现象在多种任务中稳健出现，甚至在前馈网络中也存在，根源在于网络为优化性能而倾向于在激活函数非线性区域工作，该区域噪声不对称衰减。这表明训练噪声可能成为神经网络所学习计算的一部分，而不仅是一种正则化手段。
State-Conditional Adversarial Learning: An Off-Policy Visual Domain Transfer Method for End-to-End Imitation Learning
Authors: Yuxiang Liu, Shengfan Cao
First: 2025-12-05T00:31:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:58:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study visual domain transfer for end-to-end imitation learning in a realistic and challenging setting where target-domain data are strictly off-policy, expert-free, and scarce. We first provide a theoretical analysis showing that the target-domain imitation loss can be upper bounded by the source-domain loss plus a state-conditional latent KL divergence between source and target observation models. Guided by this result, we propose State- Conditional Adversarial Learning, an off-policy adversarial framework that aligns latent distributions conditioned on system state using a discriminator-based estimator of the conditional KL term. Experiments on visually diverse autonomous driving environments built on the BARC-CARLA simulator demonstrate that SCAL achieves robust transfer and strong sample efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：状态条件对抗学习：一种面向端到端模仿学习的离策略视觉域迁移方法
我们在一个现实且具有挑战性的场景下研究端到端模仿学习中的视觉域迁移，该场景中目标域数据严格为离策略、无专家标注且稀缺。我们首先进行理论分析，表明目标域模仿损失可被源域损失加上源与目标观测模型间的状态条件潜在KL散度上界。基于此结果，我们提出状态条件对抗学习（SCAL），这是一种离策略对抗框架，通过基于判别器的条件KL项估计器，对系统状态条件下的潜在分布进行对齐。在基于BARC-CARLA模拟器构建的视觉多样化的自动驾驶环境中实验表明，SCAL实现了稳健的迁移和强大的样本效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of transferring imitation learning from abundant source domains to scarce, off-policy target domains without expert demonstrations, the paper proposes State-Conditional Adversarial Learning (SCAL), an off-policy adversarial framework that aligns latent distributions conditioned on system states using a discriminator to estimate the conditional KL divergence between source and target observation models. Theoretical analysis shows that target-domain imitation loss can be bounded by source-domain loss plus this KL term, guiding the design of SCAL. Experiments on visually diverse autonomous driving environments in BARC-CARLA demonstrate that SCAL achieves robust visual domain transfer and superior sample efficiency compared to baseline methods.
为解决在缺乏专家演示且数据稀缺的离策略目标域中，如何将端到端模仿学习从丰富源域有效迁移的问题，作者提出状态条件对抗学习（SCAL）框架，通过判别器估计状态条件下的源域与目标域观测模型间的KL散度，对齐状态条件下的潜在分布。理论分析表明，目标域模仿损失可被源域损失与该条件KL散度上界约束。在BARC-CARLA构建的视觉多样自动驾驶环境中实验表明，SCAL实现稳健的视觉域迁移并显著优于基线方法，具备更强的样本效率。
HumanNOVA: Photorealistic, Universal and Rapid 3D Human Avatar Modeling from a Single Image
Authors: Hezhen Hu, Wangbo Zhao, Lanqing Guo, Hanwen Jiang, Jonathan C. Liu, Zhiwen Fan, Kai Wang, Zhangyang Wang, Georgios Pavlakos
Venue: CVPR 2026 Highlight
First: 2026-06-01T17:58:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:58:11+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 Highlight
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
In this paper, we present HumanNOVA, a photorealistic, universal, and rapid model for generating 3D human avatars from a single RGB image. Achieving both photorealism and generalization is challenging due to the scarcity of diverse, high-quality 3D human data. To address this, we build a scalable data generation pipeline that follows two strategies. The first one is to leverage existing rigged assets and animate them with extensive poses from daily life. The second strategy is to utilize existing multi-camera captures of humans and employ fitting to generate more diverse views for training. These two strategies enable us to scale up to 100k assets, significantly enhancing both the quantity and the diversity of data for robust model training. In terms of the architecture, HumanNOVA adopts a feed-forward, token-conditioned avatar modeling framework that allows fast inference in less than one second and requires no test-time optimization. Given an input image and an estimated simplified human mesh (SMPL) without detailed geometry or appearance, the model first encodes both inputs into compact token representations. These tokens then act as conditioning signals and are fused through cross-attention to construct a triplane-based 3D avatar representation. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of our approach, both quantitatively and qualitatively, as well as its robustness under diverse input image conditions. Project page at https://HumanNOVA.github.io .
中文标题/摘要
标题：HumanNOVA：从单张图像快速生成逼真通用的3D人体虚拟形象
本文提出HumanNOVA，一种从单张RGB图像快速生成逼真、通用3D人体虚拟形象的模型。由于高质量多样化的3D人体数据稀缺，实现逼真与通用性极具挑战。为此，我们构建了一个可扩展的数据生成流程，采用两种策略：一是利用现有绑定资产并用日常姿势动画化；二是利用现有多相机人体捕捉数据，通过拟合生成更多样化的视角用于训练。这两种策略使我们能够扩展至10万级资产，显著提升数据量与多样性，从而实现稳健的模型训练。在架构上，HumanNOVA采用前馈、令牌条件化的虚拟形象建模框架，推理速度快于1秒，无需测试时优化。给定输入图像和一个无详细几何与外观的简化人体网格（SMPL），模型首先将两者编码为紧凑令牌表示，这些令牌作为条件信号通过交叉注意力融合，构建基于三平面的3D虚拟形象表示。在多个基准测试中，我们的方法在定量与定性上均表现优越，且在多样化的输入图像条件下具有鲁棒性。项目页面：https://HumanNOVA.github.io
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating photorealistic 3D human avatars from a single image while ensuring generalization across diverse appearances and poses, HumanNOVA introduces a scalable data pipeline combining rigged asset animation and multi-camera fitting to produce 100k training samples. The model employs a feed-forward, token-conditioned architecture that encodes input images and simplified SMPL meshes into tokens, which are fused via cross-attention to construct a triplane-based 3D avatar representation, enabling inference under one second without test-time optimization. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks confirm its superior performance and robustness under varied input conditions, outperforming existing methods both quantitatively and visually.
为解决从单张图像快速生成逼真且通用的3D人体虚拟形象的难题，HumanNOVA构建了结合骨骼动画与多视角拟合的可扩展数据生成流程，累计生成10万份多样化训练样本。其方法采用前馈式、令牌条件化的架构，将输入图像与简化SMPL网格编码为令牌，通过交叉注意力融合后构建三平面3D虚拟形象表示，推理速度低于一秒且无需测试时优化。在多个基准测试中，实验结果表明该方法在定量与定性指标上均表现优越，且对不同输入图像条件具有鲁棒性。
VISReg: Variance-Invariance-Sketching Regularization for JEPA training
Authors: Haiyu Wu, Randall Balestriero, Morgan Levine
First: 2026-06-01T17:58:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:58:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Self-supervised learning methods prevent embedding collapse via modeling heuristics or explicit regularization of the embedding space. Among the latter, VICReg decomposes regularization into variance and covariance objectives, offering flexibility and interpretability. However, covariance captures only second-order statistics -- encouraging decorrelation but failing to enforce the full distributional shape needed for stable training. Sketching-based methods such as SIGReg address this by aligning embeddings to an isotropic Gaussian, but lack flexibility and suffer from vanishing gradients under collapse. We propose Variance-Invariance-Sketching Regularization (VISReg), which replaces covariance with a Sliced-Wasserstein-based sketching objective that enforces full distributional shape, while retaining a variance term for scale control. By decoupling scale and shape, VISReg combines VICReg's flexibility with the distributional rigor of sketching methods, providing robust gradients even under collapse. We show that VISReg scales linearly, outperforms existing regularization on low-quality datasets, and is resilient to long-tailed and low-rank regimes. Pre-trained on ImageNet-1K, VISReg achieves state-of-the-art performance on out-of-distribution datasets. Pre-trained on ImageNet-22K, it matches DINOv2's OOD performance despite the latter using 10x more data (LVD-142M). Project and code: https://haiyuwu.github.io/visreg.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VISReg：用于JEPA训练的方差不变草图正则化
自监督学习方法通过建模启发式或显式嵌入空间正则化防止嵌入坍缩。后者中，VICReg将正则化分解为方差和协方差目标，提供灵活性和可解释性。然而，协方差仅捕捉二阶统计量——鼓励去相关性但无法强制执行稳定训练所需的完整分布形状。基于草图的方法如SIGReg通过将嵌入对齐到各向同性高斯分布解决此问题，但缺乏灵活性且在坍缩情况下易出现梯度消失。我们提出方差不变草图正则化（VISReg），用基于切片Wasserstein的草图目标替代协方差，强制执行完整分布形状，同时保留方差项以控制尺度。通过解耦尺度与形状，VISReg结合了VICReg的灵活性与草图方法的分布严谨性，在坍缩情况下仍提供稳健梯度。我们证明VISReg具有线性可扩展性，在低质量数据集上优于现有正则化方法，并对长尾和低秩场景具有鲁棒性。在ImageNet-1K上预训练后，VISReg在分布外数据集上达到最先进性能；在ImageNet-22K上预训练时，其分布外性能可媲美使用10倍数据量的DINOv2（LVD-142M）。项目与代码：https://haiyuwu.github.io/visreg。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of covariance-based regularization in self-supervised learning, which fails to capture full distributional shape and suffers from vanishing gradients under embedding collapse, the authors propose VISReg, a regularization method that replaces covariance with a Sliced-Wasserstein-based sketching objective to enforce distributional shape while retaining a variance term for scale control. This decoupling enhances gradient robustness and combines VICReg’s flexibility with sketching methods’ distributional rigor. Experiments show VISReg scales linearly, outperforms existing methods on low-quality datasets, and remains effective under long-tailed and low-rank conditions. Pre-trained on ImageNet-1K, it achieves state-of-the-art out-of-distribution performance, and on ImageNet-22K, matches DINOv2’s OOD performance despite using 10x less data.
为了解决现有自监督学习中基于协方差的正则化方法无法捕捉完整分布形状、在嵌入坍塌时梯度消失的问题，作者提出VISReg方法，用基于切片Wasserstein距离的草图目标替代协方差，以强制学习完整分布结构，同时保留方差项控制尺度。该方法通过解耦尺度与形状，结合了VICReg的灵活性与草图方法的分布严谨性，即使在嵌入坍塌时仍能保持梯度稳定。实验表明，VISReg具有线性可扩展性，在低质量数据集上优于现有方法，对长尾和低秩场景鲁棒，预训练于ImageNet-1K时达到最先进OOD性能，预训练于ImageNet-22K时性能可媲美DINOv2，尽管后者使用了10倍数据量。
Benchmarking Waitlist Mortality Prediction in Heart Transplantation Through Time-to-Event Modeling using New Longitudinal UNOS Dataset
Authors: Yingtao Luo, Reza Skandari, Carlos Martinez, Arman Kilic, Rema Padman
First: 2025-07-09T23:51:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:57:12+00:00
Comments: Best Student Paper Finalist in Proceedings of AMIA Annual Symposium 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decisions about managing patients on the heart transplant waitlist are currently made by committees of doctors who consider multiple factors, but the process remains largely ad-hoc. With the growing volume of longitudinal patient, donor, and organ data collected by the United Network for Organ Sharing (UNOS) since 2018, there is increasing interest in analytical approaches to support clinical decision-making at the time of organ availability. In this study, we benchmark machine learning models that leverage longitudinal waitlist history data for time-dependent, time-to-event modeling of waitlist mortality. We train on 23,807 patient records with 77 variables and evaluate both survival prediction and discrimination at a 1-year horizon. Our best model achieves a C-Index of 0.94 and AUROC of 0.89, significantly outperforming previous models. Key predictors align with known risk factors while also revealing novel associations. Our findings can support urgency assessment and policy refinement in heart transplant decision making.

为支持心脏移植等待名单的临床决策，本研究基于UNOS长期数据，评估机器学习模型对等待名单患者死亡时间的预测能力。使用23,807例患者的77个变量数据，模型在1年生存预测和区分能力上表现优异，最佳模型C指数达0.94、AUROC达0.89，显著优于以往方法，同时识别出已知与新的风险因素，有助于优化移植优先级评估与政策制定。
MineDraft: A Framework for Batch Parallel Speculative Decoding
Authors: Zhenwei Tang, Arun Verma, Zijian Zhou, Zhaoxuan Wu, Alok Prakash, Daniela Rus, Bryan Kian Hsiang Low
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-24T17:24:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:56:47+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model inference by using a smaller draft model to propose draft tokens that are subsequently verified by a larger target model. However, the performance of standard SD is often limited by the strictly sequential execution of these drafting and verification stages. To address this, this paper proposes MineDraft, a batch parallel speculative decoding (PSD) framework designed to effectively hide drafting latency by overlapping it with verification. Our theoretical analysis shows that PSD is substantially more efficient than standard SD. MineDraft realizes the PSD through a novel batch-parallel design that maintains two batches of requests, overlapping drafting for one batch with verification for the other. Our experimental results show significant improvements of MineDraft in both throughput (up to 75%) and end-to-end latency (up to 39%) over standard SD. Furthermore, we have implemented MineDraft as a plugin for vLLM, demonstrating its practicality for production-ready inference systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MineDraft：一种批处理并行推测解码框架
推测解码（SD）通过使用较小的草稿模型生成草稿标记，再由较大的目标模型验证，从而加速大语言模型推理。然而，标准SD的性能常受限于草稿生成与验证阶段的严格串行执行。为此，本文提出MineDraft，一种批处理并行推测解码（PSD）框架，旨在通过重叠草稿生成与验证过程来隐藏草稿延迟。理论分析表明，PSD显著优于标准SD。MineDraft通过新颖的批处理并行设计实现PSD，同时维护两个请求批次，使一个批次的草稿生成与另一个批次的验证重叠。实验结果表明，MineDraft在吞吐量（最高提升75%）和端到端延迟（最高降低39%）上均显著优于标准SD。此外，我们已将MineDraft作为插件集成至vLLM，证明其在生产级推理系统中的实用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the sequential bottleneck in standard speculative decoding that limits inference speed, this paper introduces MineDraft, a batch parallel speculative decoding framework that overlaps drafting and verification stages to hide latency. It achieves this by maintaining two batches of requests, allowing drafting for one batch to proceed concurrently with verification for the other. Experimental results demonstrate that MineDraft improves throughput by up to 75% and reduces end-to-end latency by up to 39% compared to standard SD, with a production-ready implementation via a vLLM plugin.
为解决标准推测解码中串行执行导致的推理瓶颈，本文提出MineDraft框架，通过批处理并行方式重叠草案生成与验证阶段以隐藏延迟。该框架维持两个请求批次，使一个批次的草案生成与另一个批次的验证并行执行。实验表明，MineDraft相较标准SD可提升吞吐量最高75%，端到端延迟降低最高39%，并已通过vLLM插件实现生产级部署。
AdaCodec: A Predictive Visual Code for Video MLLMs
Authors: Haowen Hou, Zhen Huang, Zheming Liang, Qingyi Si, Chenglin Li, Shuai Dong, Kele Shao, Ruilin Li, Dianyi Wang, Nan Duan, Jiaqi Wang
First: 2026-06-01T17:56:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:56:35+00:00
Comments: 23 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video is temporally redundant: adjacent frames usually share most objects, background, and layout. Yet existing video multimodal large language models (video MLLMs) usually encode each sampled frame as an independent RGB image, causing visual tokens to repeat content already present in earlier frames. This suggests a more direct video interface: send a full reference frame only when the scene cannot be predicted well from prior context, and otherwise transmit a compact description of inter-frame changes. We call this interface a \emph{predictive visual code}, and instantiate it for video MLLMs as \textbf{AdaCodec}. AdaCodec spends full visual tokens on a reference frame only when its conditional predictive cost is high; otherwise, it encodes inter-frame changes, including motion and prediction residuals, as compact P-tokens. Across all eleven benchmarks, AdaCodec improves over the Qwen3-VL-8B per-frame RGB baseline at a matched visual-token budget. Even at $1/7$ the budget, AdaCodec with 32k tokens surpasses the 224k baseline on all long-video benchmarks; on five general-video benchmarks, it raises the average score while substantially cutting time-to-first-token from 9.26s to 1.62s.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AdaCodec：面向视频多模态大语言模型的预测视觉编码
视频具有时间冗余性：相邻帧通常共享大部分物体、背景和布局。然而，现有视频多模态大语言模型（视频MLLMs）通常将每个采样帧编码为独立的RGB图像，导致视觉标记重复前帧已存在的内容。这提示了一种更直接的视频接口：仅在场景无法从先前上下文良好预测时发送完整参考帧，否则仅传输帧间变化的紧凑描述。我们将此接口称为“预测视觉编码”，并将其在视频MLLMs中具体实现为AdaCodec。AdaCodec仅在条件预测成本较高时，才将全部视觉标记用于参考帧；否则，它将帧间变化（包括运动和预测残差）编码为紧凑的P标记。在所有十一项基准测试中，AdaCodec在匹配视觉标记预算下优于Qwen3-VL-8B每帧RGB基线。即使在预算仅为基线1/7的情况下，AdaCodec使用32k标记仍优于224k基线在所有长视频基准测试中表现；在五个通用视频基准测试中，它提升了平均得分，同时将首次标记生成时间从9.26秒大幅缩短至1.62秒。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the temporal redundancy in video where adjacent frames share most visual content, the paper proposes AdaCodec, a predictive visual coding method for video MLLMs that avoids redundant encoding. Instead of processing every frame as an independent RGB image, AdaCodec uses a reference frame only when prediction from prior context is costly, otherwise encoding inter-frame changes as compact P-tokens. Experiments across eleven benchmarks show AdaCodec outperforms the per-frame RGB baseline at equal visual-token budgets, and even at 1/7 the token budget, it achieves superior performance on long-video tasks and significantly reduces time-to-first-token from 9.26s to 1.62s on five general-video benchmarks.
鉴于视频帧间存在大量视觉冗余，本文提出AdaCodec方法，旨在为视频多模态大语言模型提供预测式视觉编码。该方法仅在预测成本较高时使用完整参考帧，其余情况下以紧凑P-token编码帧间变化（如运动与预测残差）。在十一项基准测试中，AdaCodec在同等视觉token预算下超越逐帧RGB基线；即使在仅用1/7预算（32k token）时，其在长视频任务上表现更优，并在五项通用视频任务中提升平均得分，同时将首token生成时间从9.26秒缩短至1.62秒。
ClinEnv: An Interactive Multi-Stage Long Horizon EHR Environment for Agents
Authors: Yuxing Lu, Yushuhong Lin, Wenqi Shi, J. Ben Tamo, Xukai Zhao, Jinzhuo Wang, May Dongmei Wang
First: 2026-06-01T17:56:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:56:26+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 6 figures, 12 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Clinical practice is not the selection of an answer from enumerated options: a physician gathers heterogeneous information incrementally and commits to sequential, irreversible decisions under uncertainty. Static benchmarks cannot probe and existing interactive medical benchmarks each compromise on at least one of them. We present ClinEnv, an interactive benchmark that evaluates LLMs as attending physicians over real inpatient admissions under a paradigm we term Longitudinal Inpatient Simulation. Each case is automatically constructed into an ordered sequence of decision stages; at every stage the model must actively query four specialized agents before committing to medications, procedures, and diagnoses. ClinEnv scores both what the model decides, through deterministic ontology-grounded matching, and how it gathers information. Across seven models, the strongest reaches only 0.31 decision F1, and outcome quality is sharply decoupled from process quality. Difficulty concentrates in management decisions and later stages, where models recover discharge diagnoses far more reliably than management actions (0.51 vs. 0.17 F1) and continue to issue redundant queries as cases progress. ClinEnv makes this information-acquisition gap, invisible to outcome-only evaluation, directly measurable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ClinEnv：面向代理的交互式多阶段长周期电子健康记录环境
临床实践并非从预设选项中选择答案：医生会逐步收集异质信息，并在不确定性下做出连续且不可逆的决策。静态基准无法探测此类过程，现有交互式医学基准至少在其中一个方面有所妥协。我们提出ClinEnv，一个交互式基准，通过“纵向住院模拟”范式评估大语言模型作为住院医师在真实住院病例中的表现。每个病例自动构建为有序决策阶段序列；在每个阶段，模型必须在做出药物、手术和诊断决策前主动查询四个专业代理。ClinEnv不仅评估模型的决策结果（通过确定性本体匹配），还评估其信息获取过程。在七种模型中，表现最强的模型决策F1值仅为0.31，结果质量与过程质量高度脱钩。难度集中于管理决策和后期阶段，模型在出院诊断上的准确率远高于管理措施（0.51 vs. 0.17 F1），且在病例进展中持续发出冗余查询。ClinEnv使仅评估结果时不可见的信息获取差距变得可直接测量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate large language models as physicians in realistic, interactive clinical settings, the authors introduce ClinEnv, an interactive multi-stage benchmark simulating longitudinal inpatient care. Models must sequentially query four specialized agents at each decision stage before committing to diagnoses, procedures, or medications, with performance measured both on final decisions and information-gathering behavior. Across seven models, the best achieved only 0.31 F1 on decision accuracy, and outcome quality was largely disconnected from process quality, with management decisions and later stages proving especially difficult—discharge diagnoses were scored at 0.51 F1 versus 0.17 for management actions, and models continued issuing redundant queries as cases progressed.
为评估大语言模型在真实临床决策中的表现，ClinEnv构建了一个多阶段纵向模拟环境，要求模型在做出不可逆医疗决策前主动查询多个专业代理。每个病例被拆解为多个决策阶段，模型需逐步获取信息，评估既包括最终决策也包括信息获取过程。七种模型中表现最好的仅达到0.31的决策F1分数，结果质量与过程质量高度脱节；管理决策和后期阶段尤为困难，模型在诊断出院疾病时表现较好（0.51 F1），但在管理措施上表现较差（0.17 F1），且在病例推进过程中持续发出冗余查询。
Policy-based Foveated Imaging and Perception
Authors: Howard Xiao, Jan Ackermann, Boyang Deng, Gordon Wetzstein
First: 2026-06-01T17:55:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:55:05+00:00
Comments: Project website at https://howardxiao.ca/foveated/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ultra-high-resolution image sensors offer the potential to capture fine spatial details critical for many visual perception tasks, but acquiring and processing all pixels at full resolution is often infeasible under realistic bandwidth, latency, and power constraints. Existing approaches address this challenge through acquisition strategies such as spatial or temporal downsampling, which irrevocably discard information before task relevance can be assessed. In this work, we introduce a real-time, predictive, and task-aware foveated imaging system that operates directly at image acquisition time. Leveraging emerging dual-stream sensor architectures, our method dynamically allocates limited pixel bandwidth to task-relevant regions of interest while maintaining a low-resolution global context. We formulate foveated acquisition as a sensor attention policy-learning problem, in which past observations guide actions that determine future measurements, closing the perception-acquisition loop. Through extensive simulation across multiple perception tasks, we demonstrate that our approach achieves high task performance under strict pixel budgets and significantly outperforms relevant baselines operating at the same bandwidth. We further validate our system on a 200-megapixel dual-stream sensor, capturing real-world videos under realistic bandwidth and latency constraints, demonstrating the practical feasibility of task-driven, acquisition-time foveated imaging.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于策略的注视式成像与感知
超高分辨率图像传感器可捕捉对许多视觉感知任务至关重要的精细空间细节，但在现实的带宽、延迟和功耗约束下，获取并处理所有像素的全分辨率通常是不可行的。现有方法通过空间或时间下采样等采集策略应对这一挑战，但这些策略在任务相关性评估前就不可逆地丢弃信息。本文提出一种实时、预测且任务感知的注视式成像系统，直接在图像采集时运行。利用新兴的双流传感器架构，我们的方法动态分配有限的像素带宽至任务相关区域，同时保持低分辨率全局上下文。我们将注视式采集建模为传感器注意力策略学习问题，其中历史观测引导决策以确定未来测量，从而闭合感知-采集循环。通过在多个感知任务上的广泛仿真，我们证明了在严格的像素预算下，我们的方法能实现高任务性能，并显著优于同等带宽下运行的相关基线。我们进一步在2亿像素双流传感器上验证了该系统，实现在现实带宽和延迟约束下的真实世界视频采集，证明了任务驱动、采集时注视式成像的实用可行性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of uniform high-resolution imaging under bandwidth and power constraints, this work proposes a real-time, task-aware foveated imaging system that dynamically allocates sensor resources during acquisition. Leveraging dual-stream sensor architectures, it learns a policy to focus pixel bandwidth on regions predicted to be task-relevant, while preserving a low-resolution global context. Experiments across simulated perception tasks and real-world video capture on a 200-megapixel sensor show that the method achieves superior performance under strict pixel budgets compared to baselines using downsampling or fixed foveation.
为了解决高分辨率图像传感器在带宽和功耗受限下效率低下的问题，该研究提出了一种实时、任务感知的注视式成像系统，能在采集阶段动态分配像素资源。该方法利用双流传感器架构，将注视式采集建模为传感器注意力策略学习问题，依据历史观测动态决定未来采样区域，优先处理任务相关区域同时保留全局低分辨率上下文。在多个感知任务的仿真及真实世界2000万像素传感器视频采集实验中，该方法在严格像素预算下表现优异，显著优于依赖静态下采样或固定分辨率的基线方法。
WorldLens: Full-Spectrum Evaluations of Driving World Models in Real World
Authors: Ao Liang, Lingdong Kong, Tianyi Yan, Hongsi Liu, Wesley Yang, Ziqi Huang, Wei Yin, Jialong Zuo, Yixuan Hu, Dekai Zhu, Dongyue Lu, Youquan Liu, Guangfeng Jiang, Linfeng Li, Xiangtai Li, Long Zhuo, Lai Xing Ng, Benoit R. Cottereau, Changxin Gao, Liang Pan, Wei Tsang Ooi, Ziwei Liu
Venue: CVPR 2026 Oral Presentation
First: 2025-12-11T18:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:54:44+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 Oral Presentation; 80 pages, 37 figures, 29 tables; Project Page at https://worldbench.github.io/worldlens GitHub at https://github.com/worldbench/WorldLens
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Generative world models are reshaping embodied AI, enabling agents to synthesize realistic 4D driving environments that look convincing but often fail physically or behaviorally. Despite rapid progress, the field still lacks a unified way to assess whether generated worlds preserve geometry, obey physics, or support reliable control. We introduce WorldLens, a full-spectrum benchmark evaluating how well a model builds, understands, and behaves within its generated world. It spans five aspects -- Generation, Reconstruction, Action-Following, Downstream Task, and Human Preference -- jointly covering visual realism, geometric consistency, physical plausibility, and functional reliability. Across these dimensions, no existing world model excels universally: those with strong textures often violate physics, while geometry-stable ones lack behavioral fidelity. To align objective metrics with human judgment, we further construct WorldLens-26K, a large-scale dataset of human-annotated videos with numerical scores and textual rationales, and develop WorldLens-Agent, an evaluation model distilled from these annotations to enable scalable, explainable scoring. Together, the benchmark, dataset, and agent form a unified ecosystem for measuring world fidelity -- standardizing how future models are judged not only by how real they look, but by how real they behave.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WorldLens：真实世界中驾驶世界模型的全谱评估
生成式世界模型正在重塑具身人工智能，使代理能够合成视觉上逼真的4D驾驶环境，但这些环境往往在物理或行为上存在缺陷。尽管进展迅速，该领域仍缺乏统一的评估方法，以判断生成的世界是否保持几何一致性、遵循物理规律或支持可靠的控制。我们提出了WorldLens，一个全面评估模型在生成世界中构建、理解与行为表现能力的基准。该基准涵盖五个维度——生成、重建、动作跟随、下游任务和人类偏好——共同覆盖视觉真实感、几何一致性、物理合理性与功能可靠性。在这些维度上，现有世界模型均无法全面卓越：纹理逼真的模型常违反物理规律，而几何稳定的模型则缺乏行为保真度。为使客观指标与人类判断对齐，我们进一步构建了WorldLens-26K，一个包含人类标注视频、数值评分与文本理由的大规模数据集，并开发了WorldLens-Agent，该评估模型从标注中蒸馏而来，以实现可扩展且可解释的评分。该基准、数据集与代理共同构成一个统一生态系统，用于衡量世界保真度——不仅评估模型看起来多真实，更评估其行为多真实。
