为解决生成式世界模型在具身AI中仅凭视觉真实感难以全面评估的问题，WorldLens提出一套涵盖生成、重建、动作跟随、下游任务和人类偏好五大维度的综合评估基准，覆盖几何一致性、物理合理性与行为可靠性。实验表明，当前模型难以在所有维度上表现优异——视觉逼真度高的模型常违背物理规律，而物理稳定的模型则缺乏行为真实感。为弥合客观指标与人类判断的差距，研究构建了包含26K条人类标注视频的WorldLens-26K数据集，附带评分与文本理由，并训练了WorldLens-Agent评估模型，实现可扩展、可解释的评分。三者共同构成一套统一评估体系，使未来模型的评价标准不仅关注外观真实，更注重行为真实。
VLMs are Good Teachers for Video Reasoning via Adaptive Test-Time Optimization
Authors: Junhao Cheng, Liang Hou, Tianxiong Zhong, Xin Tao, Pengfei Wan, Kun Gai, Jing Liao
First: 2026-06-01T17:54:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:54:26+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://VLM-as-Teacher.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The recent "Reasoning with Video" paradigm utilizes Video Generation Models (VGMs) to generate temporally coherent visual trajectories to complete reasoning tasks. Although state-of-the-art VGMs excel at visual quality, they often struggle to understand and follow task-specific rules, leading to logical failures across diverse reasoning scenarios. Existing efforts try to utilize Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as problem pre-solvers to produce or refine textual guidance for the VGM. However, textual descriptions fail to capture intricate spatiotemporal details, and VGMs often struggle to faithfully execute fine-grained or long-tail instructions even with a valid plan. While VLMs struggle as solvers, they possess strong perception capabilities to evaluate process-constraint satisfaction and final-goal achievement. Leveraging this strength, we introduce a paradigm shift that transitions the role of VLMs to "teachers". Specifically, a VLM teacher extracts task-specific rules to formulate differentiable rewards, guiding a VGM Reasoner via test-time online optimization of a lightweight LoRA module. This strategy enables adaptive test-time optimization and extends the reasoning capabilities beyond the VGM's intrinsic boundaries. Evaluations on symbolic (VBVR-Bench) and general-purpose (RULER-Bench) video reasoning benchmarks show that the proposed method yields a 16.7-point average performance gain, outperforming the VLM-as-Solver paradigm (+0.4 points) and Best-of-N scaling (+2.2 points) by a large margin at comparable test-time cost. These findings reveal that integrating VLMs as test-time teachers offers a promising paradigm for achieving generalizable video reasoning. Project Page: https://VLM-as-Teacher.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过自适应测试时优化，视觉-语言模型是视频推理的良好教师
近期的“视频推理”范式利用视频生成模型（VGM）生成时序一致的视觉轨迹以完成推理任务。尽管最先进的VGM在视觉质量上表现优异，但它们往往难以理解和遵循任务特定规则，导致在多样化的推理场景中出现逻辑错误。现有方法尝试利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）作为问题预解器，为VGM生成或优化文本指导。然而，文本描述难以捕捉复杂的时空细节，且即使拥有有效计划，VGM也常难以忠实执行细粒度或长尾指令。虽然VLM作为解题器表现不佳，但其强大的感知能力使其能够评估过程约束满足和最终目标达成情况。基于这一优势，我们提出将VLM角色转变为“教师”，具体而言，VLM教师提取任务特定规则以构建可微分奖励，通过轻量级LoRA模块在测试时在线优化引导VGM推理器。该策略实现了自适应测试时优化，并将推理能力扩展至VGM固有边界之外。在符号型（VBVR-Bench）和通用型（RULER-Bench）视频推理基准测试中，所提方法平均性能提升16.7分，显著优于VLM作为解题器范式（+0.4分）和Best-of-N扩展（+2.2分），且测试时成本相当。这些发现表明，将VLM作为测试时教师集成，为实现可泛化的视频推理提供了有前景的新范式。项目页面：https://VLM-as-Teacher.github.io/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of Video Generation Models (VGMs) in following task-specific rules despite high visual quality, this work repositions Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as adaptive test-time teachers rather than solvers. The method uses VLMs to extract task rules and generate differentiable rewards, guiding a lightweight LoRA module within the VGM to optimize reasoning behavior at test time. Experiments on VBVR-Bench and RULER-Bench show a 16.7-point average gain over baseline VGMs, significantly outperforming VLM-as-Solver (+0.4) and Best-of-N scaling (+2.2) at comparable test-time cost, demonstrating the effectiveness of VLMs as teachers for generalizable video reasoning.
鉴于视频生成模型（VGM）虽视觉质量高但难以遵循任务特定规则，本文提出将视觉语言模型（VLM）作为测试时的自适应教师，而非直接求解器。方法利用VLM提取任务规则并生成可微奖励，引导轻量LoRA模块在测试时优化VGM的推理行为。在VBVR-Bench和RULER-Bench上的实验表明，该方法平均性能提升16.7分，显著优于VLM作为求解器（+0.4分）和Best-of-N扩展（+2.2分），且测试成本相当，验证了VLM作为教师在实现通用视频推理中的有效性。
IntraShuffler: A Privacy Preserving Framework for Heterogeneous DP Federated Learning
Authors: Farhin Farhad Riya, Olivera Kotevska, Jinyuan Stella Sun
First: 2026-06-01T17:54:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:54:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Heterogeneous Differential Privacy (HDP) in Federated Learning (FL) allows clients to select individual privacy budgets ($\varepsilon_i$) according to institutional policies and data sensitivity. In practice, many HDP-FL systems employ $\varepsilon$-aware server aggregation to improve model utility by re-weighting client updates according to their declared privacy budgets. However, gradient updates in FL retain structural patterns induced by non-independent and identically-distributed (non-IID) data, and these additional signals exposed by $\varepsilon$-aware aggregation create new opportunities for inference by an honest-but-curious server. In this work, we first show that a server equipped with gradient denoising and surrogate modeling can mount a \emph{Privacy Inference Attack} that infers distributional attributes of clients and links updates from the same client across training rounds, measured via surrogate inference accuracy and linkage success, under realistic knowledge constraints. The Shuffle-Model has been widely studied as a defense against such inference risks by anonymizing update sources, but it is fundamentally incompatible with HDP-FL $\varepsilon$-aware aggregation. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{IntraShuffler}, a middleware defense framework designed for HDP-FL systems. IntraShuffler introduces a privacy-aware shuffling mechanism that groups clients into privacy-compatible buckets and performs parameter-level shuffling within each bucket to disrupt persistent gradient structure while preserving $\varepsilon$-aware aggregation. Experiments across four different datasets show that IntraShuffler reduces gradient recoverability by over 60% and decreases surrogate inference accuracy from 0.78 to 0.33 while maintaining comparable model utility across multiple FL aggregation rules.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IntraShuffler：一种用于异构差分隐私联邦学习的隐私保护框架
联邦学习中的异构差分隐私（HDP）允许客户端根据机构政策和数据敏感性选择各自的隐私预算（ε_i）。实践中，许多HDP-FL系统采用ε感知服务器聚合，通过根据客户端声明的隐私预算重新加权客户端更新以提升模型效用。然而，FL中的梯度更新保留了由非独立同分布（非IID）数据引起的结构模式，而ε感知聚合暴露的额外信号为诚实但好奇的服务器提供了新的推断机会。本文首先证明，在梯度去噪和代理建模能力下，服务器可发起一种\emph{隐私推断攻击}，推断客户端分布属性并关联跨训练轮次的同一客户端更新，其效果通过代理推断准确率和链接成功率衡量。尽管Shuffle模型被广泛研究用于防御此类推断风险（通过匿名化更新源），但其与HDP-FL的ε感知聚合本质上不兼容。为解决此挑战，我们提出\textbf{IntraShuffler}，一种面向HDP-FL系统的中间件防御框架。IntraShuffler引入一种隐私感知的洗牌机制，将客户端分组至隐私兼容的桶内，并在每个桶内对参数级进行洗牌，以破坏持久梯度结构，同时保留ε感知聚合。在四个不同数据集上的实验表明，IntraShuffler使梯度可恢复性降低超过60%，将代理推断准确率从0.78降至0.33，同时在多种FL聚合规则下保持相当的模型效用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that ε-aware aggregation in heterogeneous differential privacy federated learning exposes client-specific gradient patterns to inference attacks, this work proposes IntraShuffler, a middleware framework that shuffles client updates at the parameter level within privacy-compatible buckets to disrupt structural signals while preserving ε-aware aggregation. The method groups clients based on privacy budget compatibility and applies intra-bucket shuffling to break linkability between updates from the same client across rounds. Experimental results across four datasets demonstrate that IntraShuffler reduces gradient recoverability by over 60% and drops surrogate inference accuracy from 0.78 to 0.33, while maintaining model utility comparable to unshuffled baselines.
为应对异构差分隐私联邦学习中ε感知聚合暴露客户端梯度结构导致的隐私推断风险，本文提出IntraShuffler框架，通过在隐私兼容的客户端组内进行参数级洗牌，在保护隐私预算的前提下破坏梯度模式。实验表明，该方法使梯度可恢复性降低60%以上，将代理推断准确率从0.78降至0.33，同时保持与未洗牌基线相当的模型效用。
Permissive Safety Through Trusted Inference: Verifiable Belief-Space Neural Safety Filters for Assured Interactive Robotics
Authors: Haimin Hu
First: 2026-06-01T17:54:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:54:00+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 17th World Symposium on the Algorithmic Foundations of Robotics (WAFR 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous robots that interact with people must make safe and efficient decisions under human-induced uncertainty, such as their preferences, goals, competency, and willingness to cooperate. Safety filters are a popular approach for ensuring safety in interactive robotics, since their modular design separates safety from performance, allowing robots to operate safely around people with minimal impact on task efficiency. While traditional safety filters typically operate only in the physical space, neglecting the robot's ability to learn and adapt online, the recently proposed belief-space safety filter (BeliefSF) reasons about robot safety in closed-loop with runtime inference that actively reduces the robot's uncertainty online, thereby reducing conservativeness in filtering. However, providing formal safety guarantees for robots deploying BeliefSF remains a significant challenge due to errors in runtime inference and neural approximation of safety filters required to handle the high dimensionality of belief spaces. In this paper, we propose an algorithmic approach to certify high-probability safety of BeliefSF using conformal prediction, while explicitly accounting for the reliability of the robot's runtime inference module. Our method leverages the structure of belief-space safety filtering by focusing verification on a region where inference is expected to be reliable. It preserves the simplicity and sample complexity of standard conformal prediction, yet can certify a substantially less conservative safety filter. Through a simulated human-vehicle interaction benchmark, we show that our approach verifies a significantly more permissive belief-space safety filter than a standard conformal prediction baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过可信推理实现宽松安全：用于保障交互式机器人的可验证信念空间神经安全滤波器
与人类交互的自主机器人必须在人类引发的不确定性（如偏好、目标、能力及合作意愿）下做出安全且高效的决策。安全滤波器是保障交互式机器人安全的流行方法，因其模块化设计将安全与性能分离，使机器人能在保障安全的同时对人类活动影响最小化。传统安全滤波器通常仅在物理空间中运行，忽视机器人在线学习与适应能力；而近期提出的信念空间安全滤波器（BeliefSF）通过闭环运行时推理主动降低机器人不确定性，从而减少滤波保守性。然而，为部署BeliefSF的机器人提供形式化安全保证仍具挑战，因需处理运行时推理误差及神经近似安全滤波器所必需的高维信念空间。本文提出一种算法方法，利用一致性预测认证BeliefSF的高概率安全，同时显式考虑机器人运行时推理模块的可靠性。该方法利用信念空间安全滤波的结构，聚焦于推理预期可靠的区域进行验证，保留标准一致性预测的简洁性与样本复杂度，却能认证更宽松的安全滤波器。通过模拟人车交互基准测试，我们证明该方法验证的信念空间安全滤波器显著比标准一致性预测基线更宽松。
Summary / 总结
To enable safe human-robot interaction under uncertain human behavior, this work addresses the challenge of certifying safety for belief-space safety filters (BeliefSF) that adaptively reduce uncertainty during operation. The method employs conformal prediction to verify high-probability safety while explicitly modeling the reliability of the robot’s runtime inference module, focusing verification on regions where inference is most trustworthy. Experimental results on a simulated human-vehicle interaction benchmark demonstrate that this approach certifies a significantly more permissive safety filter compared to standard conformal prediction, without sacrificing verification guarantees or increasing sample complexity.
为在人类不确定性下实现安全且自适应的机器人交互，本文提出一种基于合意预测的安全认证方法，用于信念空间神经滤波器，并显式建模在线推理的可靠性。该方法聚焦于推理最可信的区域进行验证，从而实现更宽松的安全滤波器，同时保持样本复杂度不变。在模拟人车交互基准测试中，实验结果表明，该方法验证的安全滤波器显著比标准合意预测基线更宽松，同时保障安全，提升了操作灵活性。
SOCO: Benchmarking Semantic Object Correspondence in Vision Foundation Models
Authors: Olaf Dünkel, Basavaraj Sunagad, Haoran Wang, David T. Hoffmann, Christian Theobalt, Adam Kortylewski
First: 2026-05-29T17:58:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:53:39+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://genintel.github.io/SOCO/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Measuring structured object understanding in vision foundation models remains challenging due to inconsistent evaluation protocols and limited part-level supervision. Semantic correspondence (SC) evaluates this capability by testing whether object parts can be matched across instances and categories under large variations in appearance, viewpoint, and geometry. To enable a systematic SC evaluation, we introduce SOCO, a new benchmark for Semantic Object Correspondence that introduces a taxonomy of correspondence types and provides consistent, functionally meaningful keypoint annotations across 100 categories and over 1M correspondence pairs. In addition, SOCO includes keypoint language descriptions, enabling the evaluation of large vision-language models (LVLMs) and their fine-grained part-level understanding. Comprehensive experiments reveal that (i) vision foundation backbones encode strong semantic structure but transfer correspondences poorly across related categories and only partially capture object-part position, (ii) LVLMs are stronger at text-prompted part localization than at visual-reference cross-image matching, exposing a gap between language-grounded localization and fine-grained visual correspondence, and (iii) correspondence performance predicts performance on dense downstream tasks, including segmentation, tracking, 3D pose estimation, and 3D detection, more strongly than ImageNet classification. Together, these findings position SOCO as a benchmark for structured, part-level representation quality in vision and multimodal foundation models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SOCO：视觉基础模型中语义对象对应性的基准测试
由于评估协议不一致和部分级监督有限，衡量视觉基础模型中的结构化对象理解仍具挑战性。语义对应（SC）通过测试在外观、视角和几何形态大幅变化下，对象部件能否跨实例和类别匹配，来评估该能力。为实现系统性SC评估，我们引入了SOCO，一个全新的语义对象对应基准，其定义了对应类型分类学，并在100个类别和超过100万对对应关系上提供一致且功能有意义的关键点标注。此外，SOCO包含关键点语言描述，支持大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）及其细粒度部件级理解的评估。全面实验表明：（i）视觉基础骨干网络编码了强语义结构，但在相关类别间难以有效迁移对应关系，且仅部分捕捉对象部件位置；（ii）LVLMs在文本提示的部件定位方面强于视觉参考跨图像匹配，揭示了语言引导定位与细粒度视觉对应之间的差距；（iii）对应性能比ImageNet分类更强烈地预测密集下游任务（如分割、跟踪、3D姿态估计和3D检测）的表现。综上，这些发现将SOCO定位为视觉和多模态基础模型中结构化、部件级表示质量的基准。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for consistent evaluation of structured object understanding in vision foundation models, SOCO introduces a new benchmark that defines a taxonomy of semantic correspondence types and provides functionally meaningful keypoint annotations across 100 object categories and over 1 million correspondence pairs. It also includes language descriptions for keypoints to enable evaluation of vision-language models. Experiments show that vision backbones encode strong semantic structure but struggle to transfer correspondences across categories and only partially capture part positions; LVLMs excel at text-guided part localization but lag in visual cross-image matching; and correspondence performance correlates more strongly with downstream tasks like segmentation and 3D detection than with ImageNet classification.
为评估视觉基础模型中的结构化物体理解能力，SOCO提出一个新基准，通过对应类型分类和跨100类超100万对关键点的统一标注系统性评估语义物体对应关系，并加入关键点语言描述以支持视觉-语言模型评估。实验表明，视觉骨干网络虽具备强语义结构，但跨类别对应迁移能力弱且仅部分捕捉部件位置；视觉-语言模型在文本引导的部件定位上表现更强，但在跨图像视觉匹配上较弱，揭示语言引导定位与细粒度视觉对应之间的差距；对应性能对下游密集任务（如分割、跟踪、3D姿态估计和3D检测）的预测能力显著强于ImageNet分类表现。
Probing Minimalist Phase Structure in LLMs: What Universal Dependencies Cannot Represent
Authors: Yuanhao Chen, Peter Chin
First: 2026-05-26T01:36:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:53:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Structural probes train on Universal Dependencies (UD), which does not encode formal-syntactic abstractions such as phase boundaries or phase-internal cohesion. Whether large language models (LLMs) encode these remains an open question that UD-based probing cannot answer by construction. We evaluate structural probes on wh-movement stimuli where UD distances are invariant across conditions by design -- any non-zero effect therefore reflects structure beyond UD. The three conditions -- bare small clause, infinitival, and finite -- are ordered by the number of Minimalist Program (MP) phase boundaries the wh-element crosses. Across 13 LLMs from four families, we find a phase-count gradient on a cross-clause pair (12/13 models) and a 13/13 sign asymmetry on a within-clause pair whose UD distance is identical across conditions -- the latter specifically predicted by phase-internal cohesion, an MP abstraction invisible to UD by construction. Activation patching confirms the representations are causally active in 12/13 models. These findings suggest that distributional pretraining can induce representations aligned with formal-syntactic abstractions beyond the reach of annotation-based probing; UD-grounded probes provide a lower bound on syntactic encoding, not an upper bound.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探究大语言模型中的极简主义阶段结构：通用依存关系无法表示的内容
结构探针基于通用依存关系（UD）训练，而UD不编码形式句法抽象概念，如阶段边界或阶段内凝聚力。大型语言模型（LLMs）是否编码这些概念仍是一个开放问题，而基于UD的探针本质上无法回答。我们在设计上使用wh-移动刺激，确保UD距离在不同条件下保持不变——任何非零效应因此反映的是UD之外的结构。三种条件——裸小从句、不定式和有限动词——按wh成分跨越极简主义程序（MP）阶段边界的数量排序。在13个来自四个家族的LLMs中，我们发现跨句对上存在阶段数梯度（12/13模型），且在UD距离相同条件下，句内对上存在13/13的符号不对称性——后者具体由阶段内凝聚力（MP抽象概念）预测，而UD本质上无法捕捉该概念。激活修补验证了12/13模型中这些表征具有因果活性。这些发现表明，分布式预训练可诱导与形式句法抽象相一致的表征，而基于标注的探针无法捕捉；UD基础探针仅提供句法编码的下限，而非上限。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of Universal Dependencies to encode formal syntactic abstractions like phase boundaries in the Minimalist Program, this study uses wh-movement stimuli with invariant UD distances to isolate structural effects beyond UD. By comparing three syntactic conditions ordered by phase boundary crossings, the researchers found a consistent phase-count gradient across 12 of 13 LLMs in cross-clause pairs and a universal sign asymmetry across all 13 models in within-clause pairs with identical UD distances, indicating phase-internal cohesion. Activation patching confirmed causal involvement of these representations in 12/13 models, suggesting that distributional pretraining can induce syntactic representations not captured by UD-based probing.
鉴于Universal Dependencies无法编码如最简方案中的相位边界等形式句法抽象，本研究通过设计UD距离不变的疑问句移动刺激，以分离UD之外的句法结构。在按相位边界数量排序的三种句法条件下，研究发现12/13个大语言模型在跨子句对中呈现相位数梯度，且所有13个模型在UD距离相同的子句内对中均表现出符号不对称性，表明相位内部凝聚力的存在。激活修补实验确认12/13模型中这些表征具有因果作用，表明分布式预训练可诱导出UD标注探针无法捕捉的形式句法表征。
From Layers to Submodules: Rethinking Granularity in Replacement-Based LLM Compression
Authors: Elia Cunegatti, Marcus Vukojevic, Erik Nielsen, Giovanni Iacca
First: 2026-06-01T17:52:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:52:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Post-training compression of Large Language Models (LLMs) removes entire architectural components, either deleting them or replacing them with fitted modules. Existing replacement-based methods share two design constraints: full-layer granularity and contiguous selection. We argue that this is overly restrictive: in fact, redundancy in pretrained transformers is not confined to contiguous regions, nor does it evenly distribute between Attention and FeedForward outputs, implying that different strategies best approximate different submodule types and that removable components need not cluster within contiguous depth ranges. Based on this intuition, we introduce SubFit (Submodule-level Fitted residual replacement), which compresses LLMs at the submodule level: Attention and FeedForward submodules are selected non-contiguously, and each receives its own lightweight fitted residual bypass. SubFit operates post-training and requires only calibration data. Across ten LLMs (five base, five instruction-tuned), five sparsity levels from 12.5% to 37.5%, and four replacement-based baselines, SubFit achieves the best aggregate perplexity-accuracy trade-off across the evaluated sparsity levels, with larger gains under aggressive compression. At 25% sparsity, it retains 84.6% of dense downstream accuracy and incurs 2.42x perplexity degradation, against 81.6% and 4.34x for the strongest baselines, while delivering measurable inference speedup and KV-cache savings. Code is available at https://github.com/eliacunegatti/SubFit.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从层到子模块：重新思考基于替换的LLM压缩中的粒度
大语言模型（LLM）的后训练压缩会移除整个架构组件，要么删除，要么替换为拟合模块。现有基于替换的方法存在两个设计约束：全层粒度和连续选择。我们认为这过于严格：预训练Transformer中的冗余并非局限于连续区域，其在Attention和FeedForward输出间的分布也不均匀，这意味着不同策略最适合逼近不同子模块类型，且可移除组件无需聚集在连续深度范围内。基于此直觉，我们提出SubFit（子模块级拟合残差替换），在子模块级别压缩LLM：Attention和FeedForward子模块非连续选择，每个子模块配备独立轻量拟合残差旁路。SubFit在后训练阶段运行，仅需校准数据。在十种LLM（五种基础模型、五种指令调优模型）、五种稀疏度（12.5%至37.5%）及四种基于替换的基线方法中，SubFit在所有评估稀疏度下均实现最佳困惑度-准确率权衡，且在激进压缩下收益更大。在25%稀疏度下，其保持84.6%的密集下游准确率，困惑度增加2.42倍，优于最强基线的81.6%和4.34倍，同时带来可观的推理加速和KV缓存节省。代码见https://github.com/eliacunegatti/SubFit。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that redundancy in pretrained transformers is not confined to contiguous regions or evenly distributed between Attention and FeedForward components, the authors challenge the conventional full-layer granularity and contiguous selection in replacement-based LLM compression. They propose SubFit, a submodule-level compression method that independently selects and replaces Attention and FeedForward submodules non-contiguously, each with its own lightweight fitted residual bypass. Evaluated across ten LLMs, five sparsity levels, and four baselines, SubFit consistently outperforms existing methods in the perplexity-accuracy trade-off, especially under aggressive compression: at 25% sparsity, it retains 84.6% of dense accuracy with only 2.42x perplexity degradation, compared to 81.6% and 4.34x for the best baseline, while also reducing inference latency and KV-cache usage.
鉴于预训练Transformer中冗余并非均匀分布在连续层或Attention与FeedForward模块之间，作者提出SubFit方法，以Attention和FeedForward子模块为单位进行替换而非整层压缩，并为每个子模块拟合轻量级残差旁路，仅需校准数据。在十种LLM和五个稀疏度水平下，SubFit在困惑度与准确率的权衡上优于四种基线方法，25%稀疏度下保持84.6%的密集模型下游准确率，困惑度仅增加2.42倍，优于最佳基线的81.6%准确率和4.34倍困惑度增长，同时降低推理延迟和KV缓存占用。
Princeton365: A Diverse Dataset with Accurate Camera Pose
Authors: Karhan Kayan, Stamatis Alexandropoulos, Rishabh Jain, Yiming Zuo, Erich Liang, Jia Deng
Venue: ICCV 2025
First: 2025-06-10T17:57:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:52:28+00:00
Comments: Update v2: Match the ICCV 2025 camera-ready version. Fix typos
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce Princeton365, a large-scale diverse dataset of 365 videos with accurate camera pose. Our dataset bridges the gap between accuracy and data diversity in current SLAM benchmarks by introducing a novel ground truth collection framework that leverages calibration boards and a 360-camera. We collect indoor, outdoor, and object scanning videos with synchronized monocular and stereo RGB video outputs as well as IMU. We further propose a new scene scale-aware evaluation metric for SLAM based on the optical flow induced by the camera pose estimation error. In contrast to the current metrics, our new metric allows for comparison between the performance of SLAM methods across scenes as opposed to existing metrics such as Average Trajectory Error (ATE), allowing researchers to analyze the failure modes of their methods. We also propose a challenging Novel View Synthesis benchmark that covers cases not covered by current NVS benchmarks, such as fully non-Lambertian scenes with 360-degree camera trajectories. Please visit https://princeton365.cs.princeton.edu for the dataset, code, videos, and submission.
中文标题/摘要
标题：普林斯顿365：具有精确相机姿态的多样化数据集
我们引入了普林斯顿365数据集，该数据集包含365个视频，具有精确的相机姿态。我们的数据集通过引入一种新颖的地面真值采集框架（结合标定板和360度相机），弥合了当前SLAM基准测试中精度与数据多样性的差距。我们采集了室内、室外及物体扫描视频，同步输出单目和立体RGB视频以及IMU数据。我们进一步提出了一种基于相机姿态估计误差诱导的光流的、具有场景尺度感知的SLAM评估新指标。与现有指标（如平均轨迹误差ATE）不同，我们的新指标允许研究人员跨不同场景比较SLAM方法的性能，从而分析其失效模式。我们还提出了一项具有挑战性的新型视图合成基准测试，覆盖了当前NVS基准未涵盖的场景，例如完全非朗伯特反射的场景及360度相机轨迹。请访问 https://princeton365.cs.princeton.edu 获取数据集、代码、视频及提交信息。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of datasets that balance data diversity with accurate camera pose for SLAM evaluation, the authors introduce Princeton365, a large-scale video dataset featuring 365 scenes captured with synchronized monocular, stereo RGB, and IMU data using calibration boards and a 360-degree camera. They propose a novel scene-scale-aware evaluation metric based on optical flow from camera pose errors, enabling cross-scene performance comparison unlike traditional metrics such as ATE. Experimental results show that this metric reveals distinct failure modes across different environments and supports a new challenging Novel View Synthesis benchmark covering non-Lambertian scenes with full 360-degree trajectories.
为解决当前SLAM评估中精度与多样性不足的问题，作者构建了Princeton365数据集，包含365段视频，通过标定板与360度相机采集精确相机位姿，涵盖室内、室外及物体扫描场景，并同步采集单目/双目RGB与IMU数据。他们提出一种基于位姿误差诱导光流的场景尺度感知评估指标，可跨场景比较SLAM方法性能，优于传统ATE指标。实验表明，该指标能揭示不同场景下的方法失效模式，且数据集支持当前NVS基准未覆盖的挑战性任务，如非朗伯特材质场景与全360度轨迹合成。
HERO'S JOURNEY: Testing Complex Rule Induction with Text Games
Authors: Anshun Asher Zheng, Kanishka Misra, David I. Beaver, Junyi Jessy Li
First: 2026-06-01T17:51:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:51:40+00:00
Comments: 24 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce HERO'S JOURNEY, a benchmark for rule induction in goal-directed episodic tasks, where agents must infer hidden rules from demonstrations and act on them through multi-step execution. HERO'S JOURNEY covers eight tasks across attribute and procedural induction families, each with four structural rule forms, controllable lexical grounding, and identifiability conditions. Evaluating state-of-the-art LLMs, we find that models show evidence of rule induction, but the ability is limited and uneven across tasks. Meanwhile, process execution adds an execution bottleneck for models, whereas surface semantics has minimal effect. Induction-specific steering methods improve performance on attribute tasks but show no reliable gains on procedural tasks, suggesting the gap in procedural induction remains an open challenge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：英雄之旅：通过文本游戏测试复杂规则归纳
我们提出了HERO'S JOURNEY，这是一个面向目标导向的分段任务中规则归纳的基准测试，其中智能体必须从演示中推断隐藏规则，并通过多步执行来应用这些规则。HERO'S JOURNEY涵盖八个任务，分别属于属性归纳和过程归纳两类，每个任务包含四种结构化规则形式、可控的词汇锚定和可识别性条件。评估当前最先进的大语言模型，我们发现模型表现出规则归纳能力，但这种能力在不同任务间有限且不均衡。同时，过程执行为模型引入了执行瓶颈，而表面语义影响较小。针对归纳任务的特定引导方法可提升属性任务表现，但在过程任务上无可靠增益，表明过程归纳的差距仍是开放挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate rule induction in complex, goal-directed environments, HERO'S JOURNEY introduces a benchmark with eight tasks spanning attribute and procedural rule families, each featuring four structural forms, controllable lexical grounding, and identifiability conditions. The study evaluates state-of-the-art LLMs, finding that while models demonstrate some rule induction capability, performance is inconsistent across task types. Execution of multi-step rules presents a significant bottleneck, while surface semantics has little impact. Induction-specific steering methods improve attribute task performance but offer no consistent benefit for procedural tasks, highlighting a persistent challenge in procedural rule induction.
为评估复杂目标导向环境中规则归纳能力，HERO'S JOURNEY构建了一个包含八项任务的基准测试，涵盖属性与过程归纳两类，每类任务设有四种规则结构、可控词汇锚定及可识别性条件。实验评估当前最先进大语言模型，发现模型虽具备一定规则归纳能力，但表现不一。多步规则执行构成显著瓶颈，而表面语义影响微弱。针对归纳的引导方法在属性任务上有效，但在过程任务上无稳定提升，表明过程规则归纳仍是开放挑战。
LongLive-RAG: A General Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Long Video Generation
Authors: Qixin Hu, Shuai Yang, Wei Huang, Song Han, Yukang Chen
First: 2026-06-01T17:50:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:50:49+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 7 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion enables variable-length synthesis, but long-horizon generation often suffers from accumulated errors and identity drift. For efficiency, existing methods commonly adopt sliding-window attention during generation. This creates an irreversible generation trajectory: once the active window accumulates appearance errors, subsequent generations can only condition on this degraded trajectory and drift further away. We address this limitation by formulating long video generation as a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) problem. Rather than relying solely on the recent window, we treat previously generated latents as a dynamic, searchable history. We propose LongLive-RAG, a general retrieval framework for AR video generation. At each new block, LongLive-RAG uses a query embedding to retrieve relevant historical latents. This lightweight retrieval step adds only a small overhead relative to generation and lets the generator condition on non-local context instead of only the recent window. To make retrieval more discriminative, we introduce the Window Temporal Delta Loss that suppresses redundant local similarity and encourages embeddings to capture meaningful temporal changes. Together, these components help reduce error accumulation caused by sliding-window attention. Experiments across multiple AR backbones and generation lengths show improved long-video quality and the best average VBench-Long rank. To our knowledge, among open-ended AR long video generation methods, LongLive-RAG is the first to formulate self-generated latent history as content-addressable retrieval memory. Code is available at https://github.com/qixinhu11/LongLive-RAG.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LongLive-RAG：一种通用的检索增强框架，用于长视频生成
自回归（AR）视频扩散模型支持可变长度合成，但长时程生成常因累积误差和身份漂移而受限。为提高效率，现有方法通常在生成过程中采用滑动窗口注意力机制，这导致不可逆的生成轨迹：一旦活动窗口累积外观误差，后续生成只能基于此退化轨迹进行条件生成，进一步偏离。我们通过将长视频生成建模为检索增强生成（RAG）问题来解决此局限。不再仅依赖最近窗口，我们将先前生成的潜在表示视为动态可检索的历史记录。我们提出LongLive-RAG，一种通用的AR视频生成检索框架。在每个新块生成时，LongLive-RAG使用查询嵌入检索相关历史潜在表示。这一轻量级检索步骤仅增加微小开销，使生成器能够基于非局部上下文而非仅最近窗口进行条件生成。为增强检索区分度，我们引入窗口时间差损失（Window Temporal Delta Loss），抑制冗余局部相似性，鼓励嵌入捕捉有意义的时间变化。这些组件共同作用，有助于减少滑动窗口注意力导致的误差累积。在多个AR主干网络和不同生成长度上的实验表明，LongLive-RAG显著提升了长视频质量，并在VBench-Long评测中取得最佳平均排名。据我们所知，LongLive-RAG是首个将自生成潜在历史建模为内容可寻址检索记忆的开放域AR长视频生成方法。代码开源于https://github.com/qixinhu11/LongLive-RAG。
Summary / 总结
To address error accumulation and identity drift in long-horizon autoregressive video generation, LongLive-RAG reformulates the problem as a retrieval-augmented generation task, treating previously generated latents as a searchable memory. Instead of relying solely on a sliding window, the framework retrieves relevant historical latents at each generation step using a lightweight query embedding, enabling conditioning on non-local context. A novel Window Temporal Delta Loss is introduced to enhance retrieval discriminability by suppressing redundant local similarities and encouraging embeddings to encode meaningful temporal changes. Experiments across multiple AR video models and varying generation lengths demonstrate improved video quality and the highest average VBench-Long ranking among open-ended methods, establishing LongLive-RAG as the first to treat self-generated latent history as content-addressable memory.
为解决自回归视频生成中长时程下误差累积与身份漂移问题，本文提出LongLive-RAG框架，将先前生成的潜在表示视为可检索的记忆，以缓解滑动窗口注意力的局限。在每一步生成中，模型通过轻量级查询嵌入检索相关历史潜变量，使生成器能基于非局部上下文而非仅最近窗口进行条件生成。为提升检索区分度，引入窗口时间差损失，抑制冗余局部相似性并鼓励嵌入编码有意义的时间变化。实验表明，该方法在多种自回归骨干网络和不同生成长度下均提升视频质量，并在VBench-Long评估中取得最佳平均排名，是首个将自生成潜变量历史作为内容可寻址检索记忆的开放域自回归长视频生成方法。
Modeling Depth Ambiguity: A Mixture-Density Representation for Flying-Point-Free Depth Estimation
Authors: Siyuan Bian, Congrong Xu, Jun Gao
First: 2026-06-01T17:50:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:50:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Despite advances in depth estimation, flying points remain a persistent failure mode: near object boundaries, depth estimators often predict spurious 3D points in the empty space between foreground and background surfaces. We trace this artifact to a standard modeling choice: assigning each pixel a single depth hypothesis. At boundaries, a pixel can straddle a foreground and a background surface, so its true depth is ambiguous between the two. A model that predicts a single depth cannot keep both possibilities, so training instead pulls the prediction toward an intermediate depth that lies on neither surface. We address this with MDA, a mixture-density representation that lets the model predict multiple depth hypotheses and their associated probabilities for each pixel. Near boundaries, different hypotheses can align with different surfaces, and the decoded depth is selected from one of these hypotheses rather than placed in the empty space between them. Across different backbones, MDA substantially improves boundary reconstruction and largely removes flying-point artifacts even under severe input blur, while adding negligible runtime overhead. The same mixture-density framework naturally extends to transparent objects, where it predicts multiple depth layers at transparent pixels, and to sky regions, where a dedicated component separates the unbounded sky from finite-depth regions, producing flying-point-free skylines. Project Page: https://biansy000.github.io/mda-site/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：建模深度歧义：面向无飞点深度估计的混合密度表示
尽管深度估计技术不断进步，飞点问题仍普遍存在：在近物体边界处，深度估计器常在前景与背景表面之间的空隙中预测虚假的3D点。我们追溯该伪影根源在于标准建模选择：为每个像素仅分配单一深度假设。在边界处，像素可能同时跨越前景与背景表面，其真实深度在两者之间存在歧义。单一深度预测模型无法同时保留两种可能性，因此训练时会将预测拉向既非前景也非背景的中间深度。我们提出MDA（混合密度表示），允许模型为每个像素预测多个深度假设及其对应概率。在边界附近，不同假设可对应不同表面，解码深度从这些假设中选择，而非置于两者之间的空隙中。MDA在不同骨干网络上显著提升边界重建效果，即使在严重输入模糊条件下也基本消除飞点伪影，且运行开销微乎其微。该混合密度框架自然扩展至透明物体（在透明像素处预测多层深度）和天空区域（专用组件分离无限深度天空与有限深度区域，生成无飞点的天际线）。项目页面：https://biansy000.github.io/mda-site/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent issue of flying-point artifacts in depth estimation near object boundaries, this work proposes MDA, a mixture-density representation that allows each pixel to predict multiple depth hypotheses with associated probabilities. Instead of forcing a single depth prediction, MDA enables the model to represent depth ambiguity by selecting the most appropriate hypothesis at inference, thereby avoiding spurious points in empty space between foreground and background surfaces. Experiments across various backbones show that MDA significantly improves boundary reconstruction and nearly eliminates flying points even under severe blur, with minimal runtime cost. The framework also naturally extends to transparent objects and sky regions, producing clean, artifact-free depth maps without additional training.
为了解决深度估计中边界区域常见的飞点伪影问题，本文提出MDA方法，采用混合密度表示，允许每个像素预测多个深度假设及其对应概率。该方法避免了传统单深度预测的局限，通过从多个可能深度值中选择，有效避免了前景与背景表面间空隙中的虚假点。实验表明，MDA在多种骨干网络上显著提升边界重建质量，即使在严重模糊输入下也能基本消除飞点伪影，且运行开销极小。该框架还可自然扩展至透明物体和天空区域，分别预测多层深度或分离无限远天空与有限深度区域，从而生成无飞点的清晰天际线。
AFUN: Towards an Affordance Foundation Model for Functionality Understanding
Authors: Zhaoning Wang, Yi Zhong, Jiawei Fu, Henrik I. Christensen, Jun Gao
First: 2026-06-01T17:50:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:50:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Affordance understanding bridges visual perception and physical action, serving as an explainable interface for robot manipulation in open and unstructured real-world environments. Yet, building an affordance foundation model that not only understands where and how the interaction should happen, but also generalizes across diverse environments, objects, and tasks, remains a long-standing research challenge. Existing methods typically address only part of this challenge, either localizing task-relevant regions without specifying executable motion, or predicting motion but with limited scalability. In this paper, we present ourmodel, a step towards an affordance foundation model for functionality understanding. From a single RGB-D observation and a language task description, ourmodel predicts a task-conditional functional mask (where to interact) and a 3D post-contact motion curve (how to interact). To support open-world generalization, we build a large-scale standardized data pipeline that converts heterogeneous robot, human, simulation, and real-world scan data into a shared affordance schema with language, masks, and object-centric 3D motion labels. We evaluate ourmodel from three aspects: for affordance segmentation, ourmodel outperforms all baselines by a large margin across 8 test sets from 4 benchmarks, improving mean gIoU/cIoU by +23.9/+26.3; for contact-point prediction, it predicts substantially more accurate points, with a 12.7--61.3% hit-rate gain over the best baseline; and for 3D motion, it achieves the best performance on all three test sets. ourmodel can be deployed for real-world robot manipulation without finetuning for robot embodiment or using task-specific heuristics, demonstrating the ability to adapt to open-world affordance tasks. Project page: https://www.zhaoningwang.com/AFUN
中文标题/摘要
标题：AFUN：面向功能理解的可操作性基础模型
可操作性理解连接视觉感知与物理动作，为机器人在开放且非结构化的真实环境中执行操作提供可解释的接口。然而，构建一个不仅能理解交互应发生的位置与方式，还能在多样环境、物体和任务间泛化的可操作性基础模型，仍是一个长期的研究挑战。现有方法通常仅解决该挑战的一部分，要么仅定位任务相关区域而未指定可执行动作，要么预测动作但缺乏可扩展性。本文提出我们的模型，是面向功能理解的可操作性基础模型的一步。从单帧RGB-D观测和语言任务描述出发，我们的模型预测任务条件下的功能掩码（交互位置）和三维接触后运动曲线（交互方式）。为支持开放世界泛化，我们构建了一个大规模标准化数据管道，将异构机器人、人类、仿真及真实世界扫描数据统一转换为共享的可操作性模式，包含语言、掩码及以物体为中心的三维运动标签。我们在三个方面评估我们的模型：在可操作性分割方面，我们的模型在4个基准的8个测试集上显著优于所有基线方法，平均gIoU/cIoU提升+23.9/+26.3；在接触点预测方面，其预测点精度大幅提升，相较最佳基线提升12.7–61.3%的命中率；在三维运动预测方面，其在三个测试集上均取得最佳性能。我们的模型无需针对机器人本体微调或使用任务特定启发式方法即可部署于真实机器人操作，展示了其适应开放世界可操作性任务的能力。项目页面：https://www.zhaoningwang.com/AFUN
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to understand and execute physical interactions in open-world environments, this paper introduces AFUN, a foundation model that predicts both where to interact (task-conditional functional mask) and how to interact (3D post-contact motion curve) from a single RGB-D observation and language instruction. To enable generalization across diverse objects, tasks, and environments, the authors construct a large-scale standardized dataset by harmonizing heterogeneous data sources into a shared affordance schema with language, mask, and 3D motion labels. Experimental results show AFUN significantly outperforms existing methods: it improves mean gIoU/cIoU by +23.9/+26.3 across 8 test sets, achieves 12.7–61.3% higher contact-point hit rates, and delivers top performance in 3D motion prediction—all without task-specific tuning or embodiment adaptation.
为使机器人能在开放环境中理解并执行物理交互，本文提出AFUN模型，该模型从单帧RGB-D图像和语言指令中预测交互位置（任务条件功能掩码）与交互方式（3D接触后运动曲线）。为支持跨物体、任务与环境的泛化，作者构建了大规模标准化数据集，将异构机器人、人类、仿真与真实世界扫描数据统一为共享的可交互语义框架。实验表明，AFUN在分割任务上较基线提升23.9/26.3%的平均gIoU/cIoU，接触点预测准确率提升12.7–61.3%，3D运动预测表现最优。更重要的是，该模型无需针对特定机器人或任务进行微调，即可实现零样本泛化能力。
STABLEVAL: Disagreement-Aware and Stable Evaluation of AI Systems
Authors: Akash Bonagiri, Gerard Janno Anderias, Saee Patil, Angelina Lai, Devang Borkar, Gezheng Kang, Ishant Gandhi, Setareh Rafatirad, Houman Homayoun
First: 2026-05-04T01:03:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:49:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human evaluation remains the primary standard for assessing modern AI systems, yet annotator disagreement, bias, and variability make system rankings fragile under standard majority vote aggregation. Majority vote discards annotator reliability and item-level ambiguity, often yielding unstable comparisons across annotator subsets. We introduce STABLEVAL, a disagreement-aware evaluation framework that models latent item correctness and annotator-specific confusion patterns to produce posterior expected item credit and calibrated agent-level scores. Unlike label-denoising approaches such as Dawid-Skene, STABLEVAL is explicitly designed for stable and uncertainty-aware system evaluation rather than hard label recovery. We formalize ranking stability as a first-class evaluation objective and analyze how aggregation methods preserve or distort underlying annotator behavior. Across controlled synthetic experiments and multiple real-world human-annotated benchmarks, majority vote exhibits increasing score error and ranking instability under annotator heterogeneity and adversarial noise, while STABLEVAL yields more stable and statistically grounded system rankings. These results demonstrate that modeling disagreement is essential for robust and reproducible AI evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：STABLEVAL：面向分歧感知与稳定评估的AI系统评价框架
人类评估仍是评估现代AI系统的主要标准，但标注员分歧、偏见和变异性使得在标准多数投票聚合下系统排名脆弱。多数投票忽略标注员可靠性与项目层面的模糊性，常导致不同标注员子集间的比较不稳定。我们提出STABLEVAL，一种分歧感知的评估框架，通过建模潜在项目正确性与标注员特定的混淆模式，生成项目信用的后验期望值和校准的代理级评分。与Dawid-Skene等标签去噪方法不同，STABLEVAL专为稳定且具备不确定性感知的系统评估而设计，而非硬标签恢复。我们将排名稳定性形式化为首要评估目标，并分析聚合方法如何保留或扭曲标注员行为。在受控合成实验及多个真实人类标注基准测试中，多数投票在标注员异质性与对抗性噪声下表现出评分误差增大和排名不稳定，而STABLEVAL则生成更稳定且统计基础更坚实的系统排名。这些结果表明，建模分歧对实现稳健且可复现的AI评估至关重要。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of AI system rankings under human evaluation due to annotator disagreement and variability, STABLEVAL introduces a disagreement-aware framework that models latent item correctness and annotator-specific confusion patterns to generate calibrated, posterior-based scores. Unlike traditional majority vote or label-denoising methods like Dawid-Skene, STABLEVAL is explicitly designed for stable, uncertainty-aware evaluation rather than label recovery. Experimental results across synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that majority vote produces increasingly inaccurate scores and unstable rankings as annotator heterogeneity or adversarial noise increases, while STABLEVAL consistently yields more stable and statistically reliable system rankings.
为了解决人工评估中因标注者分歧和变异性导致的AI系统排名不稳定问题，STABLEVAL提出了一种分歧感知框架，通过建模潜在项目正确性与标注者特定的混淆模式，生成校准后的后验项目得分。与Dawid-Skene等标签去噪方法不同，STABLEVAL专为稳定、不确定性感知的评估设计，而非硬标签恢复。实验结果表明，在标注者异质性或对抗性噪声增加时，多数投票会导致评分误差增大和排名不稳定，而STABLEVAL能持续提供更稳定、统计上更可靠的系统排名。
SN-WER: Script-Normalized WER for Multi-Script Indic ASR Evaluation
Authors: Priyaranjan Pattnayak
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-06-01T17:49:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:49:10+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 MeLLM
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Word Error Rate (WER) is the dominant metric for automatic speech recognition (ASR), but it can overestimate errors when references and hypotheses encode the same words in different scripts. This issue is common in multilingual settings where ASR models may emit romanized text. We propose Script-Normalized WER (SN-WER), a training-free, evaluation-only scoring method that transliterates both reference and hypothesis text into a language-specific canonical script before computing WER. We evaluate SN-WER on 5 Indic languages, 2 datasets, and 3 ASR models. On curated FLEURS data, SN-WER reduces inflated model gaps by up to 12%, while on noisier Common Voice data the reductions are smaller or inconsistent, indicating genuine recognition weaknesses rather than only script mismatch. Controlled stress tests show a 67% attenuation of artificial romanization-induced WER inflation, while lexical-substitution controls show near-identical sensitivity to semantic errors, with Delta SN-WER / Delta WER approximately 1.09. SN-WER is robust to transliterator choice, normalization changes, and shows low token-collision rates below 0.1% in the evaluated Indic setting. We argue that SN-WER should be reported alongside WER and CER as a companion metric for script-insensitive ASR evaluation, especially when transcripts feed downstream search, indexing, or multilingual LLM pipelines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SN-WER：多文种印度语ASR评估的脚本标准化词错误率
词错误率（WER）是自动语音识别（ASR）的主要评估指标，但在参考文本与假设文本用不同文种编码相同词汇时，WER可能高估错误。此问题在多语言环境下尤为常见，此时ASR模型可能输出罗马化文本。我们提出脚本标准化WER（SN-WER），一种无需训练、仅用于评估的评分方法，先将参考文本与假设文本转写为语言特定的规范文种，再计算WER。我们在5种印度语、2个数据集和3个ASR模型上评估SN-WER。在精心整理的FLEURS数据上，SN-WER可将模型性能差距高估降低最多12%；而在噪声更大的Common Voice数据上，降低幅度较小或不一致，表明存在真实识别缺陷而非仅脚本不匹配。受控压力测试显示，人工罗马化引起的WER高估被削弱67%；词汇替换控制实验显示，SN-WER对语义错误的敏感度几乎与WER一致，Delta SN-WER / Delta WER约1.09。SN-WER对转写器选择和标准化变化鲁棒，评估的印度语环境下字符碰撞率低于0.1%。我们认为，SN-WER应与WER和CER一同报告，作为脚本无关ASR评估的配套指标，尤其当转录结果用于下游搜索、索引或多语言大语言模型管道时。

Controlled experiments confirmed SN-WER attenuates romanization-induced WER inflation by 67% and maintains sensitivity to semantic errors (Delta SN-WER / Delta WER ≈ 1.09), with robust performance across transliterator choices and low token-collision rates (<0.1%).

Transferable Self-Harm Surveillance from Emergency Department Triage Notes Using an Evidence-Augmented Machine Learning Approach
Authors: Liuliu Chen, Gowri Rajaram, Eleanor Bailey, Katrina Witt, Michelle Lamblin, Jo Robinson, Mike Conway, Vlada Rozova
First: 2026-06-01T17:48:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:48:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Self-harm is a major public health concern, but current surveillance relying on hospital presentations is inadequate due to the low sensitivity of diagnostic codes. Emergency Department (ED) triage notes, recorded at the initial point of contact, provide a succinct summary of presentations and an opportunity to identify self-harm. We developed a three-stage approach, augmenting traditional machine learning with large language model-based screening and evidence extraction to detect self-harm in ED triage notes. We assessed model transferability across three Australian hospitals. Our approach showed AUPRCs of 0.887 +/- 0.016 and 0.884 +/- 0.012 during internal and external validation. Prospectively, it achieved AUPRC of 0.881 +/- 0.008 at the development site, and 0.879 +/- 0.012 and 0.816 +/- 0.015 at two external sites without site-specific retraining. A key advantage of the approach is that it enables identification of the primary self-harm method with an accuracy of 95%, supporting more granular surveillance beyond binary classification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用证据增强的机器学习方法，从急诊科分诊记录中实现可迁移的自伤监测
自伤是重大公共卫生问题，但当前依赖医院就诊的监测方法因诊断编码敏感性低而不足。急诊科（ED）分诊记录在初次接触时即记录患者情况，可简洁概括就诊内容并识别自伤事件。我们开发了三阶段方法，结合传统机器学习与基于大语言模型的筛查和证据提取，以检测ED分诊记录中的自伤事件。我们在三家澳大利亚医院评估了模型的迁移能力。内部和外部验证中，该方法的AUPRC分别为0.887±0.016和0.884±0.012。前瞻性研究中，开发地点AUPRC为0.881±0.008，两个外部地点（无需特定地点再训练）分别为0.879±0.012和0.816±0.015。该方法的关键优势在于能以95%准确率识别主要自伤方式，支持超越二分类的更精细监测。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to improve self-harm surveillance due to the low sensitivity of diagnostic codes, this study developed a three-stage machine learning method that combines traditional algorithms with large language models for screening and evidence extraction from emergency department triage notes. The approach demonstrated strong transferability across three Australian hospitals, achieving high average precision-recall scores (0.887 internally, 0.884 externally) and maintained robust performance (0.881, 0.879, 0.816) at external sites without retraining. Additionally, it accurately identified the primary self-harm method with 95% accuracy, enabling more detailed public health monitoring.
为弥补诊断编码敏感性不足的缺陷，本研究利用急诊科分诊记录开发了一种结合传统机器学习与大语言模型的三阶段方法，用于检测自残行为。该方法在三个澳大利亚医院验证中表现优异，内部与外部验证的AUPRC分别为0.887和0.884，且在未进行本地化再训练的情况下，新站点仍保持0.881至0.879的高准确率。更重要的是，该方法能以95%的准确率识别自残的具体方式，支持更细致的公共卫生监测。
SimSD: Simple Speculative Decoding in Diffusion Language Models
Authors: Junxia Cui, Haotian Ye, Runchu Tian, Hongcan Guo, Jinya Jiang, Haoru Li, Chaojie Ren, Yiming Huang, Kaijie Zhu, Zhongkai Yu, Kun Zhou, Jingbo Shang
First: 2026-06-01T17:46:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:46:46+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 4 figures, code available at https://github.com/airevo2/SimSD-release
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive (AR) LLMs, offering faster inference through parallel or blockwise decoding. However, their masked language modeling formulation remains incompatible with standard token-level speculative decoding, one of the most effective acceleration techniques for AR models. In AR decoding, the causal mask preserves temporally valid token-level contexts, enabling a target model to verify multiple drafted tokens in a single forward pass. In contrast, dLLMs rely on mask tokens and bidirectional attention, causing the effective context to change across denoising steps and preventing direct token-level speculative verification. To bridge this gap, we propose a simple but effective speculative decoding algorithm for diffusion language models, named SimSD, which mainly adopts a plug-and-play masking strategy that equips dLLMs with temporally valid token-level contexts for speculative decoding. Our method explicitly introduces reference tokens from draft-model predictions and designs an attention mask that regulates their interaction with current-step tokens, allowing dLLMs to compute valid logits for drafted tokens in a single forward pass. This restores the key verification ability provided by causal masking in AR models while preserving the parallel decoding advantages of dLLMs. The proposed method is training-free and can be flexibly integrated with other acceleration techniques such as KV cache and blockwise decoding. Experiments on SDAR-family dLLMs across four benchmarks show that our method achieves up to 7.46x higher decoding throughput while maintaining and even improving average generation quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SimSD：扩散语言模型中的简单推测解码
扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）近期作为自回归（AR）LLMs的有前景替代方案出现，通过并行或块级解码实现更快的推理。然而，其掩码语言建模形式与标准的token级推测解码不兼容，而后者是AR模型中最有效的加速技术之一。在AR解码中，因果掩码保留了时间上有效的token级上下文，使目标模型能在单次前向传播中验证多个草稿token。相比之下，dLLMs依赖掩码token和双向注意力，导致去噪步骤中有效上下文变化，无法直接进行token级推测验证。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了一种简单而有效的扩散语言模型推测解码算法SimSD，主要采用即插即用的掩码策略，为dLLMs提供时间上有效的token级上下文以支持推测解码。该方法显式引入草稿模型预测的参考token，并设计注意力掩码以调节其与当前步骤token的交互，使dLLMs能在单次前向传播中计算草稿token的有效logits。这恢复了AR模型中因果掩码提供的关键验证能力，同时保留了dLLMs的并行解码优势。该方法无需训练，可灵活集成其他加速技术如KV缓存和块级解码。在四个基准上的SDAR家族dLLMs实验表明，我们的方法在保持甚至提升平均生成质量的同时，解码吞吐量最高提升7.46倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accelerate diffusion language models (dLLMs) without sacrificing quality, the authors propose SimSD, a speculative decoding method tailored for dLLMs that lack native support for token-level verification. SimSD introduces a plug-and-play masking strategy that injects reference tokens from draft-model predictions and modifies attention masks to preserve temporally valid contexts, enabling dLLMs to compute logits for drafted tokens in a single forward pass. This restores the verification capability of autoregressive models while retaining dLLMs’ parallel decoding efficiency. Experiments across four benchmarks demonstrate that SimSD achieves up to 7.46x higher decoding throughput compared to baseline dLLMs, with no degradation and often improved generation quality, and is compatible with other acceleration techniques like KV cache and blockwise decoding.
为提升扩散语言模型（dLLMs）的推理速度而不降低生成质量，作者提出SimSD方法，一种专为dLLMs设计的简单推测解码方案。dLLMs因双向注意力机制和掩码标记设计，无法直接支持基于token级别的推测解码。SimSD通过插件式掩码策略，引入来自草稿模型预测的参考标记，并设计注意力掩码以调节其与当前步骤标记的交互，使dLLMs能在单次前向传播中计算出草稿标记的有效logits，恢复了自回归模型中因果掩码的验证能力，同时保留了dLLMs的并行解码优势。在四个基准测试中，SimSD在SDAR系列模型上实现最高7.46倍的解码吞吐量，且生成质量保持或提升，且无需训练，可与KV缓存、块式解码等加速技术兼容。
SkillHarm: Lifecycle-Aware Skill-Based Attacks via Automated Construction
Authors: Yuting Ning, Zhehao Zhang, Yash Kumar Lal, Boyu Gou, Junyi Li, Weitong Ruan, Chentao Ye, Rahul Gupta, Diyi Yang, Yu Su, Huan Sun
First: 2026-06-01T17:45:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:45:39+00:00
Comments: Work in Progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agent skills occupy a privileged position in the agent workflow, as agents are expected to implicitly follow and execute them, rendering third-party skills a vulnerable attack surface. Existing studies have revealed unsafe agent behaviors induced by skill-based attacks, but they primarily evaluate poisoned skills within a single task execution and enumerate harms through ad-hoc risk lists. To bridge these gaps, we introduce SkillHarm, a benchmark of skill-based attacks across the skill-use lifecycle, paired with a systematic taxonomy of skill-relevant risks. SkillHarm evaluates two attack scenarios: Fixed-Payload Poisoning (FPP), where a fixed poisoned skill package directly compromises any task session that invokes it, and Self-Mutating Poisoning (SMP), where an initially benign execution silently mutates persistent skill content, deferring harm until a subsequent reuse. It further defines 12 risk types based on the agent workflow component targeted by the harm: data pipelines, system environments, and agent autonomy. To instantiate these attacks at scale, we build AutoSkillHarm, an automated construction pipeline with coding agents driven by natural-language harnesses. The resulting benchmark contains 879 attack samples across 71 skills. Experiments show that current agents remain vulnerable with attack success rates up to 86.3% in FPP and 69.3% in SMP. Our analysis further reveals a latent risk: many apparent attack failures stem from the agent failing to engage with the poisoned file rather than genuine resistance, and current defenses still fail to reliably mitigate the threat.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SkillHarm：通过自动化构建实现生命周期感知的基于技能的攻击
代理技能在代理工作流中占据特殊地位，因为代理被期望隐式遵循并执行这些技能，使其成为第三方技能的脆弱攻击面。现有研究揭示了基于技能的攻击引发的不安全代理行为，但主要在单任务执行中评估被污染的技能，并通过临时风险列表枚举危害。为弥合这些差距，我们引入SkillHarm，一个覆盖技能使用生命周期的基于技能攻击基准，配以系统化的技能相关风险分类法。SkillHarm评估两种攻击场景：固定载荷污染（FPP），即固定污染技能包直接破坏任何调用它的任务会话；自变异污染（SMP），即初始无害执行会静默变异持久化技能内容，将危害延迟至后续重用。它进一步根据危害针对的代理工作流组件定义了12种风险类型：数据管道、系统环境和代理自主性。为大规模实例化这些攻击，我们构建了AutoSkillHarm，一个由自然语言驱动的编码代理驱动的自动化构建流水线。该基准包含71个技能的879个攻击样本。实验表明，当前代理仍存在漏洞，FPP攻击成功率高达86.3%，SMP攻击成功率高达69.3%。我们的分析进一步揭示了一种潜在风险：许多看似攻击失败源于代理未与被污染文件交互，而非真正的抵抗，且当前防御措施仍无法可靠缓解该威胁。

It defines two attack scenarios—Fixed-Payload Poisoning (FPP) and Self-Mutating Poisoning (SMP)—and uses AutoSkillHarm, an automated pipeline driven by coding agents, to generate 879 attack samples across 71 skills. Experiments reveal high attack success rates: 86.3% for FPP and 69.3% for SMP, indicating persistent agent vulnerabilities. Analysis further shows that many 'failures' occur because agents ignore poisoned files rather than resist attacks, and current defenses offer little protection.
鉴于技能在代理工作流中的关键地位及其易受攻击性，本研究提出SkillHarm基准，系统评估技能生命周期中的攻击行为，并构建包含12类风险的分类体系，覆盖数据管道、系统环境和代理自主性等组件。研究定义两种攻击模式——固定载荷污染（FPP）与自变异污染（SMP），并通过AutoSkillHarm自动化工具生成879个攻击样本（覆盖71个技能）。实验表明当前代理在FPP和SMP模式下攻击成功率分别高达86.3%和69.3%，暴露显著安全漏洞。进一步分析发现，多数“攻击失败”源于代理未调用被污染文件，而非真正防御有效，现有防御机制仍无法可靠缓解该威胁。
How Many Domains Suffice for Domain Generalization? A Tight Characterization via the Domain Shattering Dimension
Authors: Cynthia Dwork, Lunjia Hu, Han Shao
Venue: NeurIPS 2025
First: 2025-06-20T02:50:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:45:25+00:00
Comments: Accepted to NeurIPS 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study a fundamental question of domain generalization: given a family of domains (i.e., data distributions), how many randomly sampled domains do we need to collect data from in order to learn a model that performs reasonably well on every seen and unseen domain in the family? We model this problem in the PAC framework and introduce a new combinatorial measure, which we call the domain shattering dimension. We show that this dimension characterizes the domain sample complexity. Furthermore, we establish a tight quantitative relationship between the domain shattering dimension and the classic VC dimension, demonstrating that every hypothesis class that is learnable in the standard PAC setting is also learnable in our setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：需要多少个域才能实现域泛化？通过域破碎维度的紧致刻画
我们研究域泛化的一个基本问题：给定一个域家族（即数据分布），我们需要从多少个随机采样的域中收集数据，才能学习到一个在该家族中所有已见和未见域上表现合理的模型？我们在PAC框架中建模此问题，并引入一个新的组合度量，称为域破碎维度。我们证明该维度刻画了域样本复杂度。此外，我们建立了域破碎维度与经典VC维度之间的紧致定量关系，表明在标准PAC设置下可学习的每个假设类，在我们的设置下同样可学习。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how many domains are sufficient for building models that generalize across unseen distributions, this paper introduces the domain shattering dimension as a new combinatorial measure within the PAC framework. The authors demonstrate that this dimension tightly characterizes the minimum number of domains required for domain generalization, and establish a precise quantitative link to the classical VC dimension, showing that any hypothesis class learnable in standard PAC learning is also learnable under domain generalization.
为理解在领域泛化中需要多少个训练领域才能使模型在已见和未见领域上表现良好，本文在PAC框架下引入了“领域破碎维度”这一组合度量，该维度精确刻画了领域泛化所需的最小样本数。研究证明该维度与经典VC维度存在紧致的定量关系，表明任何在标准PAC学习中可学习的假设类，在领域泛化中同样可学习且具有相同的样本复杂度。
Causal state binding predicts action control in language agents
Authors: Xiao Jia
First: 2026-05-10T18:22:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:41:49+00:00
Comments: 85 pages, 5 main figures; supplementary information included
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous language agents increasingly expose traces, memories, plans and constraints, but existing evaluations rarely test whether these state variables are bound to final actions. We introduce causal state binding, an intervention-coupled evaluation framework that measures whether actions change with the event-specific decisive state while remaining invariant to irrelevant cues. The primary readout is a hidden-target finite-action benchmark in which scorer-side intervention targets are assigned before generation and withheld from the model-visible prompt. Across 57,816 scored records in seven corpus-level units, structured-agent conditions exceeded high-randomness controls and targeted component removals on reason, memory, veto and self-continuity responsiveness. Open-weight validation across Qwen2.5 7B, 14B and 32B plus Mistral-7B showed that action priors, no-field prompts and scrambled decisive context did not recover the structured-control signature. In diagnostic finite-action probes, the minimal decisive-field readout recovered the prescribed action pattern whereas surface-only, action-prior-only and scrambled-field controls did not. Across 300 SWE-bench Lite issue records and six API models, adding an oracle-free causal state-binding composite to a full non-CSB baseline increased constraint-clean issue-to-file hit@3 AUC from 0.873 to 0.935. This validation concerns issue-to-file localization, not patch application or SWE-bench issue resolution. These results support a measurement principle for agent evaluation: action control is predicted by event-specific state-action binding, not by output entropy, action-prior matching or rationale format alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：因果状态绑定预测语言代理中的动作控制
自主语言代理越来越多地暴露其痕迹、记忆、计划和约束，但现有评估很少检验这些状态变量是否与最终动作绑定。我们引入了因果状态绑定，这是一种与干预耦合的评估框架，用于衡量动作是否随事件特定的决定性状态变化，同时对无关线索保持不变。主要读出指标是一个隐藏目标的有限动作基准，其中评分方干预目标在生成前分配，且不显示在模型可见提示中。在七个语料库级别单位的57,816条评分记录中，结构化代理条件在推理、记忆、否决和自我连续性响应方面优于高随机性对照组和目标组件移除组。在Qwen2.5 7B、14B、32B及Mistral-7B的开放权重验证中，动作先验、无领域提示和打乱决定性上下文均未能恢复结构化控制特征。在诊断性有限动作探测中，最小决定性域读出恢复了规定动作模式，而仅表面、仅动作先验和打乱域对照组则未恢复。在300个SWE-bench Lite问题记录和六个API模型中，将无oracle因果状态绑定复合指标添加到完整非CSB基线后，约束清理问题到文件命中率@3 AUC从0.873提升至0.935。此验证关注问题到文件定位，而非补丁应用或SWE-bench问题解决。这些结果支持代理评估的测量原则：动作控制由事件特定状态-动作绑定预测，而非仅由输出熵、动作先验匹配或理由格式决定。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the need to evaluate whether language agents bind their internal states—such as memories, plans, and constraints—to their final actions in a causal manner. The authors introduce 'causal state binding,' an intervention-based evaluation framework that tests if actions respond to event-specific decisive states while ignoring irrelevant cues. Using a hidden-target finite-action benchmark where intervention targets are withheld from the model’s prompt, they tested 57,816 records across seven corpus units and found structured-agent conditions outperformed random controls and component removals in responsiveness. Validation across multiple open-weight models showed that action priors, no-field prompts, or scrambled context failed to restore structured control. Diagnostic probes confirmed that only minimal decisive-field readouts preserved action patterns. In SWE-bench Lite, adding a causal state-binding composite to a baseline improved constraint-clean issue-to-file hit@3 AUC from 0.873 to 0.935, demonstrating that action control is best measured by state-action binding, not entropy, prior matching, or rationale format.
该研究旨在填补语言代理评估中的空白，提出因果状态绑定框架，用于检验代理行为是否与事件特定的决定性状态存在因果关联，同时对无关线索保持不变。方法采用隐藏目标有限动作基准，干预目标在生成前分配且不暴露于模型提示中，以精确测量状态-动作绑定。在57,816条记录的七个语料单元中，结构化代理条件在推理、记忆、否决和自我连续性响应方面显著优于高随机性对照组和目标组件移除组。对多个开放权重模型的验证表明，动作先验、无字段提示或打乱上下文无法恢复结构化控制。诊断探针显示，仅最小决定性字段读取能恢复指定动作模式。在SWE-bench Lite中，将因果状态绑定复合模块加入基线后，约束干净的issue-to-file命中率AUC从0.873提升至0.935，表明动作控制由状态-动作绑定预测，而非熵值、先验匹配或理由格式。
Incentivized Collaboration in Active Learning
Authors: Lee Cohen, Han Shao
First: 2023-11-01T03:17:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:40:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In collaborative active learning, where multiple agents try to learn labels from a common hypothesis, we introduce an innovative framework for incentivized collaboration. Here, rational agents aim to obtain labels for their data sets while keeping label complexity at a minimum. We focus on designing (strict) individually rational (IR) collaboration protocols, ensuring that agents cannot reduce their expected label complexity by acting individually. We first show that given any optimal active learning algorithm, the collaboration protocol that runs the algorithm as is over the entire data is already IR. However, computing the optimal algorithm is NP-hard. We therefore provide collaboration protocols that achieve (strict) IR and are comparable with the best known tractable approximation algorithm in terms of label complexity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：主动学习中的激励协作
在协同主动学习中，多个代理试图从共同假设中学习标签，我们提出了一种创新的激励协作框架。理性代理旨在为其数据集获取标签，同时最小化标签复杂度。我们专注于设计（严格）个体理性（IR）协作协议，确保代理无法通过单独行动减少其预期标签复杂度。我们首先证明，对于任何最优的主动学习算法，直接在整个数据上运行该算法的协作协议已具备IR性质。然而，计算最优算法是NP难问题。因此，我们提供了实现（严格）IR且在标签复杂度上与目前已知最优可计算近似算法相当的协作协议。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce label complexity in collaborative active learning, this paper proposes incentivized collaboration protocols where rational agents jointly learn labels while minimizing queries. The method designs (strict) individually rational (IR) protocols ensuring agents cannot benefit by acting alone, and shows that running any optimal active learning algorithm over the full dataset yields an IR protocol. However, since computing the optimal algorithm is NP-hard, the paper provides practical IR protocols that match the label complexity of the best known tractable approximation algorithm.
为降低协作主动学习中的标注复杂度，本文提出激励协作协议，使理性代理在联合学习时最小化查询。方法设计（严格）个体理性（IR）协议，确保代理无法通过单独行动获益，并证明任何最优主动学习算法在全数据集上运行即为IR协议。由于最优算法计算NP难，本文提供可实现IR且标注复杂度与已知最优近似算法相当的实用协议。
Tracking the Behavioral Trajectories of Adapting Agents
Authors: Jonah Leshin, Manish Shah, Ian Timmis
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-01T17:40:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:40:31+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 1 figure. To appear at the Second Workshop on Agents in the Wild: Safety, Security, and Beyond (AIWILD) at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text files such as skill files, memory files, and behavioral configuration files play a central role in defining how modern agents act. Through edits by humans or the agents themselves, these files may evolve over time, directly steering the agent's behavior in future interactions. We present a methodology and framework for measuring agent $traits$ by defining traits as directions in the embedding space of a text embedding model. We train a linear model on labeled "before" versus "after" skill file diffs to learn a trait vector, then score arbitrary skill edits by projecting their embedding diffs onto this vector. Evaluated on 68 labeled skill diff pairs for the trait of propensity to seek sensitive data, our method achieves 91.2% sign classification accuracy and a Spearman rank correlation of $ρ= 0.82$ under leave-one-out cross-validation. We build this trait evaluation into a broader agent-to-agent protocol that enables one agent to evaluate another's skill file updates through a trusted intermediary.
中文标题/摘要
标题：追踪适应性代理的行为轨迹
技能文件、记忆文件和行为配置文件等文本文件在定义现代代理行为方面起着核心作用。通过人类或代理自身的编辑，这些文件可能随时间演变，直接引导代理在未来交互中的行为。我们提出了一种方法和框架，通过将特质定义为文本嵌入模型嵌入空间中的方向来衡量代理特质。我们训练一个线性模型，基于标记的“修改前”与“修改后”技能文件差异，学习特质向量，然后通过将任意技能编辑的嵌入差异投影到该向量上进行评分。在68个标记的技能差异对（针对“寻求敏感数据倾向”特质）上评估，我们的方法在留一交叉验证下达到91.2%的符号分类准确率和Spearman秩相关系数ρ=0.82。我们将此特质评估整合进一个更广泛的代理间协议中，使一个代理可通过可信中介评估另一个代理的技能文件更新。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to quantify how human or agent-driven edits to text-based configuration files influence agent behavior, this work introduces a framework that defines agent traits as directions in a text embedding space. It trains a linear model on labeled 'before' and 'after' skill file diffs to learn a trait vector, then scores new edits by projecting their embedding differences onto this vector. Evaluated on 68 labeled skill diff pairs for the trait of seeking sensitive data, the method achieves 91.2% sign classification accuracy and a Spearman rank correlation of 0.82 under leave-one-out cross-validation, demonstrating strong predictive power. The framework is further extended into a protocol allowing agents to evaluate each other’s skill file changes via a trusted intermediary.
为量化代理行为如何通过文本配置文件演变，该研究提出一种方法，将技能文件差异嵌入向量空间并训练线性模型以识别特质方向。通过将新编辑投影到所学特质向量上，可评估其与目标特质（如倾向获取敏感数据）的对齐程度。在68组标注技能差异对上评估，该方法在留一交叉验证下达到91.2%的符号分类准确率和0.82的斯皮尔曼等级相关系数，表明预测能力较强。该框架进一步扩展为一种协议，允许代理通过可信中介评估彼此的技能文件更新。
LL-Bench: Rethinking Low-Level Vision Evaluation in the Era of Large-Scale Generative Models
Authors: Lu Liu, Huiyu Duan, Chenxin Zhu, Jintong Lu, Haoyun Jiang, Liu Yang, Qiang Hu, Guangtao Zhai, Xiaoyun Zhang
First: 2026-06-01T17:40:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:40:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large-scale generative models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across image generation and editing tasks. However, their performance in low-level vision tasks, which require pixel-wise control, remains insufficiently studied. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{LL-Bench}, a comprehensive \textbf{Benchmark} for evaluating the capabilities of large-scale generative models on \textbf{L}ow-\textbf{L}evel vision tasks. The benchmark comprises 2,469 real-world degraded images covering 16 low-level degradation tasks, and 28,919 restored images produced by 10 state-of-the-art large-scale generative models and 21 conventional restoration models, which are annotated with 152,020 expert-level pairwise human preferences and 28,334 quality scores. Built upon LL-Bench, we present a systematic diagnosis that reveals the performance boundaries and unique failure modes of large-scale generative models across diverse low-level vision tasks, compared with conventional representative restoration approaches. Moreover, we investigate the effectiveness of current quality evaluation metrics on LL-Bench, which exhibit significant discrepancy with human ratings. To better align restored-image quality assessment with human preferences, we further propose \textbf{LL-Score}, an MLLM-based evaluator that captures both restoration quality and hallucination existence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LL-score not only outperforms existing image quality assessment metrics, but also serves as a promising reward model for training generative models on low-level vision tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LL-Bench：在大规模生成模型时代重新思考低级视觉任务的评估
大规模生成模型在图像生成与编辑任务中展现出卓越能力，但在需要逐像素控制的低级视觉任务中的表现仍缺乏充分研究。为填补这一空白，我们提出了\textbf{LL-Bench}，一个全面的\textbf{基准}，用于评估大规模生成模型在\textbf{L}ow-\textbf{L}evel视觉任务中的能力。该基准包含2,469张真实世界退化图像，涵盖16种低级退化任务，以及由10个当前最先进的大规模生成模型和21个传统修复模型生成的28,919张修复图像，这些图像标注了152,020个专家级成对人类偏好和28,334个质量评分。基于LL-Bench，我们系统性诊断了大规模生成模型在各类低级视觉任务中的性能边界和独特失败模式，并与传统代表性修复方法进行对比。此外，我们研究了当前质量评估指标在LL-Bench上的有效性，发现其与人类评分存在显著差异。为更好地使修复图像质量评估与人类偏好对齐，我们进一步提出了\textbf{LL-Score}，一种基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的评估器，能够同时捕捉修复质量和幻觉存在情况。大量实验表明，LL-Score不仅优于现有图像质量评估指标，还可作为训练低级视觉任务生成模型的有前景的奖励模型。

Motivated by the under-examination of large-scale generative models' performance in pixel-level low-level vision tasks, the authors introduce LL-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 2,469 degraded images across 16 tasks and 28,919 restored outputs from 31 models, annotated with 152,020 human preference pairs and 28,334 quality scores. Their analysis reveals that generative models often fail in specific degradation types despite general strength, and existing metrics poorly correlate with human judgment. To address this, they propose LL-Score, an MLLM-based evaluator that jointly assesses restoration quality and hallucination, which outperforms current metrics and shows promise as a reward model for training.
为填补大规模生成模型在需要像素级控制的低级视觉任务中缺乏系统评估的空白，作者构建了LL-Bench基准，包含2469张真实退化图像和31个模型生成的28919张修复图像，附带152020组专家级人类偏好标注和28334个质量评分。分析显示，生成模型在不同退化类型上表现不一，且与传统修复方法相比存在独特失效模式。现有质量评估指标与人类评分显著不一致，作者提出基于MLLM的LL-Score评估器，能更准确捕捉修复质量与幻觉存在，实验表明其优于现有指标，且可作为低级视觉任务训练的奖励模型。
Improving Combined Detection and Classification of TEM Defects via Mask-Conditioned Latent Diffusion Augmentation
Authors: Ni Li, Nuohao Liu, Ryan Jacobs, Ajay Annamareddy, Maciej P. Polak, Kevin Field, Izabela Szlufarska, Dane Morgan
First: 2026-06-01T17:38:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:38:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Analyzing microstructural defects in transmission electron microscopy (TEM) images, particularly in irradiated metal alloys, is often limited by the availability of high-quality, labeled data. To address this, we introduce a generative data augmentation approach using a mask-conditioned latent diffusion model (LDM) for synthesizing realistic TEM images with controllable, automatically labeled multi-class defect masks. Without requiring manual annotations for generation, our method enables the creation of synthetic image-mask pairs by sampling distributions learned from experimental masks. These generated data were used to augment small experimental datasets of varying sizes (10, 50, and 100 labeled experimental images) to train a Mask Regional Convolutional Neural Network (R-CNN) model for defect detection and classification. Our results show that generative augmentation yields small overall model performance improvements, with up to a 0.02 gain in the harmonic mean of detection and classification F1 scores. However, we also find that the relative contributions to detection and classification improvement depend on the specific train/test data split. These findings highlight the potential of targeted generative models to enhance deep learning performance in data-scarce microscopy-based image quantification tasks.

SafeSteer: Localized On-Policy Distillation for Efficient Safety Alignment
Authors: Hao Li, Jingkun An, Zijun Song, Pengyu Zhu, Rui Li, Hao Wang, Wendi Feng, Yesheng Liu, Lijun Li, Jin-Ge Yao, Lei Sha
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-06-01T17:38:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:38:12+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 8 figures, 14 tables. Submitted to EMNLP 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with human values often degrades their general capabilities, termed the alignment tax. Existing methods mitigate this by balancing dual objectives, which heavily rely on massive general-purpose data or auxiliary reward models. In this paper, we argue that, because safety features are inherently sparse within the output distribution, alignment requires localized modifications rather than global trade-offs. To this end, we propose SafeSteer, which performs on-policy distillation confined to safety tokens. First, we construct a safety teacher via activation steering. Based on this teacher, we develop a safety token selection algorithm. Consequently, SafeSteer restricts the reverse KL penalty to these tokens during training to preserve general capabilities. Experimental results across diverse models show that our SafeSteer achieves a superior trade-off between safety and general capability compared with existing methods, attaining strong safety performance on seven safety benchmarks with only minimal degradation on five general capability benchmarks. Notably, SafeSteer requires only 100 harmful samples without using any general-purpose data, less than 1% of what previous baselines used, considerably reducing alignment cost. More details are on our project page at https://anjingkun.github.io/SafeSteer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SafeSteer：面向高效安全对齐的局部策略蒸馏
将大语言模型（LLM）与人类价值观对齐常会损害其通用能力，这一现象被称为对齐税。现有方法通过平衡双重目标缓解该问题，但高度依赖大规模通用数据或辅助奖励模型。 本文认为，由于安全特性在输出分布中天然稀疏，对齐应通过局部修改而非全局权衡实现。为此，我们提出SafeSteer，仅在安全令牌上执行策略蒸馏。首先，我们通过激活引导构建安全教师模型；基于该教师，开发安全令牌选择算法；随后，在训练中仅对这些令牌施加逆KL惩罚，以保留模型通用能力。 在多种模型上的实验表明，SafeSteer在安全与通用能力之间实现了优于现有方法的权衡，在七个安全基准测试中表现强劲，仅在五个通用能力基准上略有性能下降。值得注意的是，SafeSteer仅需100个有害样本，且无需通用数据，其数据量不足先前基线的1%，显著降低了对齐成本。更多详情请见项目页面：https://anjingkun.github.io/SafeSteer。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the alignment tax—where safety alignment degrades general capabilities—this paper proposes SafeSteer, a localized on-policy distillation method that targets safety tokens specifically. It constructs a safety teacher via activation steering, selects safety tokens using a dedicated algorithm, and applies reverse KL divergence only to those tokens during training to preserve general performance. Experiments across multiple models show SafeSteer achieves superior safety-general capability trade-offs, scoring strongly on seven safety benchmarks while causing minimal degradation on five general capability benchmarks, using only 100 harmful samples—less than 1% of what prior methods required—without relying on general-purpose data.
为缓解安全对齐带来的能力损失（alignment tax），本文提出SafeSteer方法，通过仅对安全相关token进行在线蒸馏，实现局部安全优化而不影响通用能力。该方法通过激活引导构建安全教师，并筛选安全token，仅在训练中对这些token施加逆KL惩罚。实验表明，SafeSteer在七个安全基准上表现优异，同时仅在五个通用能力基准上轻微下降，且仅需100个有害样本——远低于传统方法所需（不足1%）——无需通用数据。
Robust Predictive Uncertainty and Double Descent in Contaminated Bayesian Random Features
Authors: Michele Caprio, Katerina Papagiannouli, Siu Lun Chau, Sayan Mukherjee
First: 2026-02-22T10:50:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:38:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a robust Bayesian formulation of random feature (RF) regression that accounts explicitly for prior and likelihood misspecification via Huber-style contamination sets. Starting from the classical equivalence between ridge-regularized RF training and Bayesian inference with Gaussian priors and likelihoods, we replace the single prior and likelihood with $ε$- and $η$-contaminated credal sets, respectively, and perform inference using pessimistic generalized Bayesian updating. We derive explicit and tractable bounds for the resulting lower and upper posterior predictive densities. These bounds show that, when contamination is moderate, prior and likelihood ambiguity effectively acts as a direct contamination of the posterior predictive distribution, yielding uncertainty envelopes around the classical Gaussian predictive. We introduce an Imprecise Highest Density Region (IHDR) for robust predictive uncertainty quantification and show that it admits an efficient approximation via an adjusted Gaussian credible interval. We further obtain predictive variance bounds (under a mild truncation approximation for the upper bound) and prove that they preserve the leading-order proportional-growth asymptotics known for RF models. Together, these results establish a robustness theory for Bayesian random features: predictive uncertainty remains computationally tractable, inherits the classical double-descent phase structure, and is improved by explicit worst-case guarantees under bounded prior and likelihood misspecification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：鲁棒预测不确定性与污染贝叶斯随机特征中的双重下降
我们提出了一种鲁棒的贝叶斯随机特征（RF）回归框架，通过Huber风格的污染集显式考虑先验和似然的误设。从经典等价关系出发——即岭正则化RF训练等价于具有高斯先验和似然的贝叶斯推断——我们将单一先验和似然替换为ε-和η-污染的信念集，并采用悲观广义贝叶斯更新进行推断。我们推导出所得下界和上界后验预测密度的显式且可计算的界限。这些界限表明，当污染程度适中时，先验和似然的模糊性实质上直接污染后验预测分布，从而在经典高斯预测周围形成不确定性包络。我们引入了用于鲁棒预测不确定量化的一种不精确最高密度区域（IHDR），并证明其可通过调整的高斯置信区间高效近似。我们进一步获得预测方差界限（上界在温和截断近似下），并证明其保持RF模型已知的主导阶比例增长渐近性质。这些结果共同建立了贝叶斯随机特征的鲁棒性理论：预测不确定性保持计算可处理性，继承经典双重下降相结构，并在先验和似然误设有界时通过显式最坏情况保证得到改进。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle prior and likelihood misspecification in Bayesian random feature regression, this work introduces a robust Bayesian framework using Huber-style contamination sets for both prior and likelihood. It replaces classical Gaussian assumptions with ε- and η-contaminated credal sets and employs pessimistic generalized Bayesian updating to derive tractable bounds on lower and upper posterior predictive densities. These bounds reveal that moderate contamination effectively shifts the predictive distribution into uncertainty envelopes around the classical Gaussian case. The paper further defines an Imprecise Highest Density Region (IHDR) for uncertainty quantification, approximated efficiently via adjusted Gaussian intervals, and proves that predictive variance bounds retain the double-descent asymptotic behavior of standard RF models, thereby establishing a theoretically grounded robustness theory for Bayesian random features under bounded model misspecification.
为应对贝叶斯随机特征回归中先验与似然模型失配的问题，本文提出一种基于Huber式污染集的鲁棒贝叶斯框架，分别用ε-和η-污染置信集替代经典高斯假设，并采用悲观广义贝叶斯更新进行推断。由此推导出下界与上界后验预测密度的显式可计算界限，表明适度污染会直接扩大预测不确定性包络，围绕经典高斯预测形成置信区间。研究进一步定义了用于鲁棒不确定性度量的不精确最高密度区域（IHDR），并可通过调整高斯置信区间高效近似。同时证明预测方差界限在轻度截断近似下仍保持随机特征模型的主导阶双下降渐近特性。实验表明该鲁棒框架在保持计算可处理性的同时，显著提升在先验与似然有限失配下的最坏情况预测可靠性。
Auditing Asset-Specific Preferences in Financial Large Language Models: Evidence from Bitcoin Representations and Portfolio Allocation
Authors: Wenbin Wu
First: 2026-06-01T17:36:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:36:06+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 5 figures, 18 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models now power robo-advisors and trading agents, yet whether they carry built-in biases toward specific assets is largely untested. We ask three questions: do LLMs systematically prefer certain financial instruments; can an internal representation with causal leverage over those preferences be identified; and does that representation affect downstream financial decisions? We develop a three-level audit protocol and apply it to Bitcoin. First, a behavioral audit of eight frontier LLMs shows that Bitcoin's ranking among money-like instruments is frame-dependent: models place it around rank 5 of 8 as "reliable money" but near the top under crisis and autonomous-agent frames, and an attribute-swap experiment confirms rankings track functional properties, not names. Second, we open a model's internals: a search across thousands of sparse-autoencoder features in Gemma 3 identifies a dominant Bitcoin-selective feature. Amplifying it shifts the model toward the asset and suppressing it shifts the model away, even when "Bitcoin" never appears in the prompt. Third, we test financial consequences: amplification raises Bitcoin's portfolio share by 5.2 percentage points while suppression lowers it by 4.6 pp, with amplification reallocating within crypto and suppression cutting total crypto exposure. We characterize this as bounded behavioral leverage (leverage meaning causal influence over outputs, not financial leverage): an identifiable internal feature can be perturbed to move financial choices, but only within measurable limits. The framework links internal representations to external recommendations, validated with random controls and mechanism boundaries. As LLMs become autonomous financial agents, this is a first step toward a behavioral layer for emerging know-your-agent (KYA) standards: knowing what an agent prefers, and how far that preference can be moved.
中文标题/摘要
标题：金融大语言模型中资产特异性偏好的审计：基于比特币表征与投资组合配置的证据
大型语言模型如今驱动着智能投顾和交易代理，但它们是否内建对特定资产的偏好，尚缺乏系统检验。我们提出三个问题：LLM是否系统性偏好某些金融工具；能否识别具有因果影响力、能驱动这些偏好的内部表征；该表征是否影响下游金融决策？ 我们构建三级审计协议并应用于比特币。首先，对八款前沿LLM的行为审计显示，比特币在“类货币”工具中的排名受框架影响：模型将其列为8种工具中约第5位的“可靠货币”，但在危机或自主代理框架下则排名靠前；属性置换实验证实排名反映功能属性而非名称。其次，我们打开模型内部：在Gemma 3中搜索数千个稀疏自动编码器特征，识别出主导性比特币选择性特征。放大该特征使模型更倾向比特币，抑制则使其远离，即使提示中从未出现“比特币”。第三，我们检验金融后果：放大使比特币在投资组合中的占比上升5.2个百分点，抑制则下降4.6个百分点，放大导致加密资产内部再分配，抑制则削减总加密资产敞口。 我们将此现象定义为“有界行为杠杆”（杠杆指对输出的因果影响，非金融杠杆）：可识别的内部特征可通过扰动影响金融决策，但仅在可测量范围内。该框架将内部表征与外部推荐关联，经随机对照与机制边界验证。随着LLM成为自主金融代理，这是迈向新兴“了解你的代理”（KYA）标准的行为层第一步：明确代理偏好及其可移动范围。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether financial large language models (LLMs) exhibit built-in biases toward specific assets, using Bitcoin as a case study. The authors develop a three-level audit protocol: first, they conduct a behavioral audit across eight frontier LLMs, finding that Bitcoin’s ranking as a 'reliable money' asset varies by context, and attribute-swap experiments confirm this reflects functional properties rather than naming. Second, they identify a dominant Bitcoin-selective feature within Gemma 3’s internal sparse-autoencoder representation, showing that amplifying or suppressing this feature causally shifts the model’s preference for Bitcoin—even without the word 'Bitcoin' in input. Third, they test financial outcomes, finding that amplifying the feature increases Bitcoin’s portfolio allocation by 5.2 percentage points while suppressing it reduces crypto exposure by 4.6 percentage points.
