本研究探讨金融大语言模型是否对特定资产存在固有偏好，以比特币为案例。作者构建三阶段审计框架：首先通过行为审计发现模型对比特币的排名随语境变化，证实偏好源于功能属性而非名称；其次在Gemma 3中识别出一个主导性内部特征，可独立于提示中提及比特币而通过放大或抑制该特征改变模型行为；最后实验表明，该特征的干预可直接影响投资组合分配，放大时比特币占比上升5.2个百分点，抑制时总加密资产敞口下降。研究揭示了‘有限行为杠杆’——内部特征可因果影响金融决策，但作用范围有明确边界，为未来大模型金融代理的偏好审计提供了方法论基础。
Why Not Hyperparameter-Friendly Optimisation? A Monotonic Adaptive Norm Rescaling Approach For Long-Tailed Recognition
Authors: Shuo Zhang, Chenqi Li, Tingting Zhu
First: 2026-06-01T17:34:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:34:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-tailed recognition poses a significant challenge for deep learning. The two-stage decoupling paradigm, which separates representation learning from classifier retraining, offers a promising solution. During the classifier retraining stage, adaptive norm rescaling is a popular technique. It adjusts the per-class weight norms via parameter regularization, which inevitably introduces hyperparameters. However, many studies report that long-tailed recognition is sensitive to these hyperparameters, as their setup significantly impacts performance. In this paper, we first provide a class-conditional distribution perspective to support norm rescaling methods. Furthermore, we propose a simple but effective approach called Self-Adaptive Monotonic Normalization (SAMN). SAMN avoids the need for parameter regularization. It directly enforces monotonicity on per-class weight norms using the Pool Adjacent Violators Algorithm, making the method hyperparameter-friendly. SAMN is a universal strategy that integrates seamlessly with other methods for enhanced performance. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that our method significantly boosts long-tailed recognition performance, often achieving state-of-the-art results.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为何不采用超参数友好的优化方法？一种用于长尾识别的单调自适应范数重缩放方法
长尾识别对深度学习提出了重大挑战。两阶段解耦范式，将表示学习与分类器再训练分离，提供了一种有前景的解决方案。在分类器再训练阶段，自适应范数重缩放是一种流行技术，它通过参数正则化调整每类权重范数，但不可避免地引入超参数。然而，许多研究指出，长尾识别对这些超参数敏感，其设置显著影响性能。本文首先从类条件分布视角支持范数重缩放方法。此外，我们提出了一种简单而有效的方法——自适应单调归一化（SAMN）。SAMN无需参数正则化，直接通过池相邻违反者算法强制每类权重范数单调，使方法具备超参数友好性。SAMN是一种通用策略，可无缝集成其他方法以提升性能。在基准数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法显著提升长尾识别性能，常取得最先进结果。
Summary / 总结
To address the sensitivity of long-tailed recognition to hyperparameters in adaptive norm rescaling methods, this paper proposes Self-Adaptive Monotonic Normalization (SAMN), which enforces monotonicity on per-class weight norms without relying on parameter regularization. By leveraging the Pool Adjacent Violators Algorithm, SAMN eliminates the need for tuning hyperparameters while maintaining compatibility with existing techniques. Experimental results across benchmark datasets show that SAMN consistently improves performance, often achieving state-of-the-art results in long-tailed recognition tasks.
为了解决长尾识别中自适应范数重缩放方法对超参数敏感的问题，本文提出了一种无需超参数的自适应单调归一化方法（SAMN），该方法通过池相邻违反者算法直接强制每类权重范数的单调性。与依赖参数正则化且需调参的传统方法不同，SAMN无需额外超参数即可实现范数约束。在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，SAMN显著提升了长尾识别性能，常达到最先进水平，且可无缝集成其他方法。
FigSIM: A Dataset for Fine-grained Suicide Severity and Figurative Language in Suicide Memes
Authors: Liuliu Chen, Elise R. Carrotte, Brian E. Chapman, Jo Robinson, Mike Conway
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-06-01T17:32:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:32:29+00:00
Comments: Content warning: contains suicide-related content. Accepted to Findings of the Association for Computational Linguistics: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Suicide memes are memes used to express suicide-related thoughts or comment on suicide-related issues. Suicide memes are increasingly common on social media, yet remain poorly understood and potentially harmful. There is an urgent need to better understand their characteristics and to develop appropriate content moderation strategies that limits users' exposure to potentially harmful content. Currently, the absence of annotated datasets of suicide memes remains a key barrier to developing and evaluating automated moderation approaches. In this paper, we introduce FigSIM, the first dataset designed for fine-grained analysis of suicide memes. The dataset consists of 1049 memes, each annotated for (1) fine-grained suicide severity levels, (2) figurative phenomena (e.g., metaphors), and (3) suicide-related content (e.g., suicide method depiction). We benchmark 16 unimodal and multimodal models across three tasks: figurative language, suicide severity, and suicide-related content detection. Overall, FigSIM demonstrates that suicide memes pose unique challenges for both modeling and content moderation. Analysis revealed biases, such as underprediction of higher suicide severity levels, especially for figurative memes. The dataset (including splits used for analyses) is publicly available. Content Warning: This paper contains suicide-related content that may be triggering.

自杀表情包是用于表达与自杀相关思想或评论自杀相关议题的表情包。此类表情包在社交媒体上日益普遍，但尚未被充分理解，且可能具有潜在危害性。迫切需要更好地理解其特征，并开发适当的内容审核策略，以限制用户接触潜在有害内容。目前，缺乏标注的自杀表情包数据集仍是开发和评估自动化审核方法的关键障碍。本文引入FigSIM，这是首个专为细粒度分析自杀表情包而设计的数据集。该数据集包含1049个表情包，每个表情包均标注了（1）细粒度自杀严重程度等级，（2）修辞现象（如隐喻），以及（3）与自杀相关的内容（如自杀方法描述）。我们在三个任务上对16个单模态和多模态模型进行了基准测试：修辞语言、自杀严重程度及与自杀相关的内容检测。总体而言，FigSIM表明自杀表情包对建模和内容审核提出了独特挑战。分析发现存在偏差，例如对高自杀严重程度等级的预测不足，尤其在修辞类表情包中更为明显。该数据集（含用于分析的划分）已公开发布。内容警告：本文包含可能引发不适的自杀相关内容。

Moment-Video: Diagnosing Temporal Fidelity of Video MLLMs on Momentary Visual Events
Authors: Xiaolin Liu, Yilun Zhu, Xiangyu Zhao, Xuehui Wang, Yan Li, Xin Li, Haoyu Cao, Xing Sun, Shaofeng Zhang, Xu Yang, Zhihang Zhong, Xue Yang
First: 2026-06-01T17:32:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:32:20+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 10 figures, 11 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made rapid progress on general and long-form video understanding, yet their ability to preserve brief answer-critical visual evidence remains underexplored. Many practical questions are determined by momentary visual events: localized actions or state transitions that may last only a few frames. Such evidence can be skipped by sparse frame sampling, suppressed by visual-token compression, or diluted by coarse temporal aggregation, causing failures that language-side reasoning cannot reliably recover. We introduce Moment-Video, a benchmark for diagnosing the temporal fidelity of video MLLMs through momentary visual event understanding. Each question is grounded in a localized, visually observable, and sampling-sensitive event, requiring models to notice, count, describe, or reason about transient evidence rather than rely on persistent objects, global scene context, or language priors. Moment-Video contains 1,000 human-verified video-QA pairs across 7 domains and 25 fine-grained subcategories, covering four task types: Temporal Occurrence, Temporal Counting, Action Description, and Temporal Reasoning. We evaluate 33 proprietary and open-source MLLMs on Moment-Video. The best-performing model, Seed-2.0-Pro, achieves only 39.6% overall accuracy, while most open-source models remain below 25%, revealing a substantial gap in momentary visual event understanding. Diagnostic analyses show that denser frame sampling improves some models but does not eliminate the bottleneck, and longer videos introduce stronger temporal-localization challenges. These findings suggest that current video MLLMs still lack temporally faithful representations for capturing, preserving, and using brief but decisive visual evidence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Moment-Video：通过瞬时视觉事件理解诊断视频多模态大语言模型的时间保真度
视频多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在通用及长视频理解方面进展迅速，但其保留短暂且对答案至关重要的视觉证据的能力仍被忽视。许多实际问题由瞬时视觉事件决定：局部动作或状态转换，可能仅持续几帧。此类证据可能因稀疏帧采样被跳过、因视觉标记压缩被抑制，或因粗粒度时间聚合而被稀释，导致语言侧推理无法可靠恢复的失败。我们提出Moment-Video，一个通过瞬时视觉事件理解诊断视频MLLMs时间保真度的基准。每个问题均基于局部、可视觉观察且对采样敏感的事件，要求模型关注、计数、描述或推理瞬时证据，而非依赖持久对象、全局场景上下文或语言先验。Moment-Video包含7个领域、25个细粒度子类别的1000组人工验证视频问答对，涵盖四种任务类型：时间发生、时间计数、动作描述和时间推理。我们在Moment-Video上评估了33个专有及开源MLLMs。表现最佳的模型Seed-2.0-Pro整体准确率仅为39.6%，而多数开源模型低于25%，揭示了瞬时视觉事件理解存在显著差距。诊断分析表明，更密集的帧采样可改善部分模型，但无法消除瓶颈，且更长视频引入更强的时间定位挑战。这些发现表明，当前视频MLLMs仍缺乏对短暂但决定性视觉证据进行捕捉、保留和使用的时序忠实表示。

为评估视频多模态大语言模型对短暂但关键的视觉事件的处理能力，作者提出了Moment-Video基准测试，通过局部、采样敏感的视觉问题来检测模型的时间保真度。该方法构建了1000个经过人工验证的视频问答对，涵盖四种任务类型，要求模型识别、计数、描述或推理瞬时视觉现象，而非依赖持久物体或全局上下文。在评估33个模型时，表现最好的Seed-2.0-Pro仅达到39.6%准确率，多数开源模型低于25%，揭示了模型在处理短暂视觉证据方面的显著差距。诊断分析表明，更密集的帧采样虽有帮助，但无法完全解决瓶颈，且视频越长，时间定位挑战越强。
Drifting Preference Optimization for One-Step Generative Models
Authors: Zhou Jiang, Yandong Wen, Zhen Liu
First: 2026-06-01T17:31:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:31:49+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
One-step text-to-image generators are attractive for deployment because they generate an image with a single forward pass, but preference finetuning them remains difficult: standard alignment methods often rely on policy likelihoods, denoising trajectories, differentiable reward gradients, or test-time optimization. We propose Drifting Preference Optimization (DrPO), an online preference-finetuning method for deterministic one-step generators. For each prompt, DrPO samples candidates from the current generator, ranks them with a target reward, and uses high- and low-scoring samples to synthesize a feature-space update direction. The update is a non-parametric dipole preference field plus a reference drift estimated from the frozen base generator, and is optimized through a detached feature-space regression target. The target reward is used only for ranking, so DrPO can train with large, black-box, or non-differentiable rewards while inference remains a single generator call. We evaluate DrPO on SD-Turbo and SDXL-Turbo with multiple target rewards and benchmarks, including HPSv3 and GenEval. DrPO improves alignment over reward-gradient-free one-step preference baselines and reduces HPSv3 training computation by $3.51\times$ under the matched effective-batch setting by removing reward-model backpropagation. Initial offline experiments suggest that sample-based gradient synthesis can also be used beyond online reward ranking.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一步生成模型的漂移偏好优化
一步文本到图像生成器因其单次前向传播即可生成图像而适合部署，但对其进行偏好微调仍具挑战：标准对齐方法常依赖策略似然、去噪轨迹、可微奖励梯度或测试时优化。我们提出漂移偏好优化（DrPO），一种针对确定性一步生成器的在线偏好微调方法。对每个提示，DrPO从当前生成器采样候选图像，用目标奖励对其进行排序，并利用高分与低分样本合成特征空间更新方向。该更新由非参数双极偏好场与从冻结基础生成器估计的参考漂移组成，并通过脱离的特征空间回归目标进行优化。目标奖励仅用于排序，因此DrPO可在使用大型、黑盒或不可微奖励时训练，而推理仍保持单次生成器调用。我们在SD-Turbo和SDXL-Turbo上评估DrPO，使用多种目标奖励和基准（包括HPSv3和GenEval）。DrPO在无奖励梯度的一步偏好基线方法上提升对齐效果，并在匹配有效批处理设置下通过移除奖励模型反向传播，将HPSv3训练计算量降低3.51倍。初步离线实验表明，基于样本的梯度合成也可用于超越在线奖励排序的场景。

为解决单步文本到图像生成器在无梯度奖励或测试时优化情况下难以进行偏好微调的问题，本文提出Drifting Preference Optimization（DrPO）方法，该方法对当前生成器采样候选图像，依据目标奖励进行排序，并从高分与低分样本合成特征空间更新方向。该更新结合非参数双极偏好场与从冻结基础生成器估计的参考漂移，通过脱离的特征空间回归目标进行优化。实验表明，DrPO在SD-Turbo和SDXL-Turbo上优于无梯度奖励基线，在匹配有效批处理设置下将HPSv3训练计算量降低3.51倍，得益于省去了奖励模型反向传播。
Learning to Reduce Search Space for Generalizable Neural Routing Solver
Authors: Changliang Zhou, Xi Lin, Zhenkun Wang, Qingfu Zhang
First: 2025-03-05T03:25:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:29:42+00:00
Comments: accepted by SIGKDD 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Constructive neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) offers a promising paradigm for solving vehicle routing problems (VRPs) by directly learning to construct approximate optimal solutions, thereby reducing reliance on expert knowledge for algorithm design. However, scaling these methods to handle large-scale instances remains challenging due to high computational complexity. While recent dynamic search space reduction (SSR) methods can improve inference efficiency through geometric distance-based pruning, they often struggle on complex instances with non-uniform distributions or when optimal solutions rely heavily on non-spatial constraints. To address this critical issue, we propose Learning to Reduce (L2R), which is the first learning-based dynamic SSR framework. L2R learns to adaptively prioritize nodes by extracting patterns from problem-specific features to prune the search space at each step, enabling efficient and scalable solution construction. Extensive experiments show that our L2R framework generalizes robustly to different problem scales and data distributions on various VRP variants. To the best of our knowledge, L2R is the first neural solver to effectively scale to VRP instances with $10$ million nodes while maintaining high solution quality, which significantly pushes the frontier of NCO in terms of generalization and scalability. Our code is available at https://github.com/CIAM-Group/L2R.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习动态缩减搜索空间以实现通用神经路由求解器
构造性神经组合优化（NCO）为通过直接学习构建近似最优解来解决车辆路径问题（VRP）提供了一种有前景的范式，从而减少对算法设计中专家知识的依赖。然而，由于计算复杂度高，这些方法在扩展到大规模实例时仍面临挑战。尽管近期的动态搜索空间缩减（SSR）方法可通过基于几何距离的剪枝提升推理效率，但在具有非均匀分布或最优解高度依赖非空间约束的复杂实例上表现不佳。为解决这一关键问题，我们提出学习缩减（L2R），这是首个基于学习的动态SSR框架。L2R通过从问题特定特征中提取模式，自适应地优先处理节点，从而在每一步剪枝搜索空间，实现高效且可扩展的解构造。大量实验表明，我们的L2R框架在各类VRP变体上能稳健泛化至不同问题规模和数据分布。据我们所知，L2R是首个能有效扩展至1000万节点VRP实例并保持高质量解的神经求解器，显著推动了NCO在泛化能力和可扩展性方面的前沿。代码见https://github.com/CIAM-Group/L2R。

为了解决神经组合优化在大规模车辆路径问题中难以扩展的问题，作者提出了Learning to Reduce（L2R），一种基于学习的动态搜索空间缩减框架。L2R通过学习从问题特定特征中提取模式，动态优先处理关键节点，从而在每一步裁剪搜索空间，避免了依赖几何距离的启发式方法在复杂或非均匀分布实例中的失效。实验表明，L2R在不同VRP变体上具有良好的泛化能力，能有效扩展至1000万节点规模的实例，同时保持高解质量，显著推动了神经组合优化在可扩展性和泛化能力方面的前沿。
Why Larger Models Learn More: Effects of Capacity, Interference, and Rare-Task Retention
Authors: Jing Huang, Daniel Wurgaft, Rachit Bansal, Laura Ruis, Naomi Saphra, David Alvarez-Melis, Andrew Kyle Lampinen, Christopher Potts, Ekdeep Singh Lubana
First: 2026-05-28T08:02:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:29:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Larger models learn tasks smaller models do not. What drives this phenomenon? We develop a simple phenomenological argument that power-law scaling already suggests that a larger model will be able to learn a part of the data distribution that a smaller model fails to learn, even with infinite training data. To validate this claim and identify its causes, we study the effects of model scaling on a synthetic setup consisting of a mixture of tasks that show monotonic scaling curves. The results point to a data-induced competition over resources (neurons). Specifically, smaller models allocate their neurons to high frequency or low complexity tasks, and so they learn solutions that perform poorly on rare and complex tasks. Moreover, this happens even when solutions capable of expressing the desired task exist. We then assess how a larger model circumvents this data-centric bottleneck, finding that it traces to a reduced interference mechanism: larger models can allocate enough resources to common tasks that the gradient updates for those tasks become weak, which means that they do not overwrite rare-task features as they slowly accumulate. Finally, to further validate these claims, we pretrain OLMo models (4M to 4B parameters) on novel tasks of varying frequency and complexity. The results mirror those from our synthetic data experiments: only the larger OLMo models learn the infrequent and complex tasks, and these larger models embed more task features in their representations and show less gradient interference between tasks. Overall, we offer a data-centric account of why larger models learn tasks that smaller models fail to. This helps explain why larger models are better in practice, and it can inform practical questions concerning model sizing and training data mixtures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为何更大模型能学习更多：容量、干扰与稀有任务保留效应
更大模型能学习小模型无法学习的任务。驱动这一现象的原因是什么？我们提出一个简单的现象学论证，指出幂律缩放本身已暗示，即使拥有无限训练数据，更大模型仍能学习小模型无法掌握的数据分布部分。为验证该主张并识别其成因，我们在合成数据集上研究模型规模的影响，该数据集由呈现单调缩放曲线的任务混合组成。结果表明，这是数据引发的资源（神经元）竞争：小模型将神经元分配给高频或低复杂度任务，因而学习到的解决方案在稀有且复杂任务上表现不佳。即使存在能表达所需任务的解决方案，这一现象仍会发生。接着我们评估更大模型如何绕过这一数据驱动瓶颈，发现其根源在于干扰机制减弱：更大模型可为常见任务分配足够资源，使这些任务的梯度更新微弱，从而在缓慢积累过程中不会覆盖稀有任务特征。最后，为进一步验证这些结论，我们在不同频率和复杂度的新任务上预训练OLMo模型（4M至4B参数）。结果与合成数据实验一致：仅更大规模的OLMo模型能学习稀有且复杂任务，且这些模型在表征中嵌入更多任务特征，任务间梯度干扰更少。总体而言，我们提供了一种以数据为中心的解释，说明为何更大模型能学习小模型无法掌握的任务。这有助于解释为何更大模型在实践中表现更优，并可指导实际的模型规模选择与训练数据混合设计。
Summary / 总结
The paper investigates why larger models can learn tasks that smaller models cannot, proposing that this stems from a data-induced competition for neural resources. Using synthetic data with monotonic scaling curves, it shows that smaller models prioritize high-frequency, low-complexity tasks, leaving rare and complex tasks underlearned—even when solutions exist. Larger models avoid this bottleneck by reducing gradient interference: they allocate sufficient resources to common tasks so their updates don’t overwrite rare-task features, allowing those features to accumulate. This is confirmed in experiments with OLMo models (4M–4B parameters) pretrained on varied task frequencies and complexities, where only larger models successfully learn infrequent and complex tasks, embedding more task features and showing less interference.
本文探讨了为何更大模型能学习小模型无法掌握的任务，提出这一现象源于数据驱动的神经资源竞争。通过合成数据（具有单调缩放曲线）实验发现，小模型倾向于优先学习高频或低复杂度任务，导致稀有且复杂任务因资源不足而表现不佳。相比之下，大模型可为常见任务分配足够容量，使其梯度更新减弱，从而避免覆盖稀有任务特征的积累。该结论通过在OLMo模型（4M–4B参数）上预训练不同频率与复杂度任务得到验证：仅大模型能掌握稀有复杂任务，其表示中嵌入更多任务特征且梯度干扰更少。
IMAC-AgriVLN: Can Agricultural Vision-and-Language Navigation Agents be Aware of Instruction Mistakes?
Authors: Xiaobei Zhao, Xingqi Lyu, Xin Chen, Xiang Li
First: 2026-06-01T17:27:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:27:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Agricultural robots are serving as powerful assistants across a wide range of agricultural tasks, nevertheless, still heavily relying on manual operations or railway systems for movement. The AgriVLN method and the A2A benchmark pioneeringly extended Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) to the agricultural domain, enabling a robot to navigate to a target position following a natural language instruction. However, almost all the prior methods adopt an ideal assumption that the given instructions themselves are correct, which does not align with the realistic scenarios, because anybody may say an instruction with mistakes. To bridge this gap, we propose the A2A-MI benchmark, in which we build a semi-automatic data annotator to insert three mistake classifications into each original instruction in a more diversified and efficient way. We test several state-of-the-art agricultural VLN agents on it and observe a sufficient drop with -57% on SR and -9% on NE, from which we suggest that an agricultural VLN agent tends to assume that the given instruction is correct, so does not have the awareness to doubt it when the scenes it sees do not align with the instruction it receives. To build the awareness on instruction mistake, we propose the IMAC module analyzing the instruction and the current front-facing image, to judge whether the instruction has mistakes and attempt to correct it when needed. We integrate IMAC into the baseline model, and observe a noteworthy improvement, sufficiently narrowing the gap to the performance on instructions without mistakes. Project: https://github.com/AlexTraveling/IMAC-AgriVLN.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IMAC-AgriVLN：农业视觉-语言导航代理能否意识到指令错误？
农业机器人在多种农业任务中发挥着强大辅助作用，但仍主要依赖人工操作或轨道系统移动。AgriVLN方法和A2A基准率先将视觉-语言导航（VLN）拓展至农业领域，使机器人能根据自然语言指令导航至目标位置。然而，几乎所有现有方法均假设指令本身无误，这与现实不符，因为任何人都可能发出错误指令。为弥合此差距，我们提出A2A-MI基准，通过半自动标注器在原始指令中插入三种错误类型，实现更丰富高效的标注。我们在其上测试多个前沿农业VLN代理，发现准确率（SR）下降57%，导航误差（NE）下降9%，表明农业VLN代理倾向于默认指令正确，缺乏对指令错误的质疑能力。为提升对指令错误的感知，我们提出IMAC模块，分析指令与当前前方图像，判断指令是否存在错误并尝试修正。将IMAC集成至基线模型后，性能显著提升，大幅缩小与无错误指令表现的差距。项目地址：https://github.com/AlexTraveling/IMAC-AgriVLN。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the real-world issue that agricultural robots often receive erroneous natural language instructions, this work introduces the A2A-MI benchmark to evaluate VLN agents’ ability to detect and correct instruction mistakes. The method employs a semi-automatic annotator to inject three types of mistakes into original instructions, then tests state-of-the-art agents, revealing a significant performance drop: -57% in success rate and -9% in navigation efficiency. To address this, the IMAC module is proposed to analyze both the instruction and the current visual scene to detect inconsistencies and attempt correction, which when integrated into a baseline model, substantially improves performance, narrowing the gap to error-free instruction performance.
为解决农业机器人常接收到错误自然语言指令的实际问题，本文提出A2A-MI基准测试，用于评估视觉-语言导航（VLN）代理对指令错误的识别与处理能力。方法通过半自动标注器在原始指令中注入三类错误，测试现有顶尖模型后发现性能显著下降：成功率下降57%，导航效率下降9%。为此，提出IMAC模块，通过分析指令与当前视觉场景判断不一致并尝试修正。将IMAC集成到基线模型后，性能显著提升，大幅缩小了与无错误指令场景的性能差距。
ToolFG: Towards Well-Grounded Fine-Grained Image Classification
Authors: Yu Xue, Haoxuan Qu, Zhuoling Li, Yihang Lou, Yan Bai, Hossein Rahmani, Jun Liu
First: 2026-06-01T17:27:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:27:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fine-grained image classification (FGIC) has broad applications and has attracted significant research attention. In this paper, we explore a novel paradigm for solving FGIC by proposing \textbf{ToolFG}, the first tool-integrated MLLM-based framework tailored to FGIC. ToolFG enables MLLMs to autonomously and flexibly use external tools during the reasoning process, actively interact with images, and collect verifiable visual cues for distinguishing highly similar categories in a more \textit{reliable} and \textit{well-grounded} manner. To equip the model with such tool-use ability, we design a novel \textbf{MCTS-guided tool-use knowledge distillation mechanism}, which effectively mines tool-use- and FGIC-relevant knowledge from advanced proprietary MLLMs for model training. Furthermore, we propose a \textbf{model-tool co-evolution mechanism} that jointly refines the toolset and the model's tool-use policy, driving them toward a mutually adapted and FGIC-specialized state. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ToolFG：迈向扎实的细粒度图像分类
细粒度图像分类（FGIC）具有广泛的应用并吸引了大量研究关注。本文提出了一种新颖的FGIC解决方案范式——\textbf{ToolFG}，这是首个面向FGIC的集成工具的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）框架。ToolFG使MLLM能够在推理过程中自主且灵活地使用外部工具，主动与图像交互，并收集可验证的视觉线索，以更\textit{可靠}和\textit{扎实}的方式区分高度相似的类别。为赋予模型此类工具使用能力，我们设计了一种新颖的\textbf{基于MCTS引导的工具使用知识蒸馏机制}，有效从先进的专有MLLM中挖掘与工具使用和FGIC相关的知识用于模型训练。此外，我们提出了一种\textbf{模型-工具协同进化机制}，联合优化工具集和模型的工具使用策略，推动二者向相互适应且专为FGIC优化的状态发展。大量实验验证了我们框架的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more reliable and grounded fine-grained image classification, this paper introduces ToolFG, the first MLLM-based framework that integrates external tools to enhance visual reasoning. It enables models to autonomously use tools to interact with images and collect verifiable cues for distinguishing similar categories. The method employs a MCTS-guided knowledge distillation mechanism to transfer tool-use knowledge from advanced MLLMs and a model-tool co-evolution mechanism to jointly refine the toolset and the model’s policy. Experimental results show that ToolFG significantly outperforms existing methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance on multiple FGIC benchmarks.
为提升细粒度图像分类的可靠性和可解释性，本文提出ToolFG框架，首次将外部工具集成至多模态大语言模型中以增强视觉推理能力。该方法使模型能自主调用工具与图像交互，收集可验证的视觉线索以区分高度相似类别。其核心方法包括基于MCTS引导的知识蒸馏机制，用于从先进MLLM中迁移工具使用与细粒度分类相关知识，以及模型与工具协同进化机制，共同优化工具集与模型策略。实验表明，ToolFG在多个细粒度图像分类基准上显著优于现有方法，达到当前最优性能。
A Biconvex Formulation for Stable Transport of Mixture Models with a Unique Solution
Authors: Yeganeh Marghi, Kelly Jin, Uygar Sümbül
First: 2026-06-01T17:26:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:26:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Optimal transport (OT) provides a principled framework for mapping between probability distributions. Despite extensive progress, applying OT to large-scale data remains computationally demanding, and the resulting pointwise transport plans are often difficult to interpret. We introduce Optimal Mixture Transport (OMT), a scalable framework that shifts the transport paradigm from individual samples to mixtures of subpopulations, reformulating the transport problem as a strictly biconvex optimization with a unique global minimizer. We further establish theoretical guarantees on the stability of the OMT map, showing that bounded perturbations of the underlying distributions lead to bounded changes in the transport plan. By formulating subpopulations as exponential-family distributions, OMT decouples computational complexity from the sample size, scaling solely with the number of mixture components. We demonstrate the effectiveness and practicality of OMT on a wide range of synthetic benchmarks and real-world datasets, including image data and large-scale single-cell RNA sequencing measurements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于混合模型稳定传输的双凸形式化方法
最优传输（OT）提供了一种将概率分布映射的原理性框架。尽管已有大量进展，但将OT应用于大规模数据仍计算成本高昂，且生成的逐点传输计划往往难以解释。我们引入最优混合传输（OMT），一种可扩展框架，将传输范式从单个样本转向子群体的混合，将传输问题重新表述为具有唯一全局最小值的严格双凸优化问题。我们进一步建立了OMT映射的稳定性理论保证，表明底层分布的有界扰动会导致传输计划的有界变化。通过将子群体表述为指数族分布，OMT将计算复杂度与样本量解耦，仅随混合成分数量扩展。我们在多种合成基准和真实数据集（包括图像数据和大规模单细胞RNA测序数据）上展示了OMT的有效性和实用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency and interpretability issues of traditional optimal transport, this paper proposes Optimal Mixture Transport (OMT), a biconvex optimization framework that operates on mixture models of subpopulations rather than individual samples. By reformulating the transport problem as a strictly biconvex program with a unique global solution, OMT enables scalable computation that scales with the number of mixture components, not sample size. Theoretical analysis confirms the stability of the transport map under distributional perturbations. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets—including image data and large-scale single-cell RNA sequencing—demonstrate OMT’s efficiency, accuracy, and practical applicability.
为解决传统最优传输计算效率低、运输方案难以解释的问题，本文提出最优混合传输（OMT）框架，将传输对象从单个样本转向子群体的混合模型，并将其重构为具有唯一全局最小值的严格双凸优化问题。该方法通过将子群体建模为指数族分布，使计算复杂度与样本量无关，仅依赖混合成分数量，从而实现可扩展性。理论分析证明了传输映射在分布扰动下的稳定性。在合成数据和真实数据集（如图像和大规模单细胞RNA测序数据）上的实验表明，OMT在处理大规模数据时兼具高效性、稳定性和实用性，且不牺牲精度与可解释性。
Optimizing Diversity and Quality through Base-Aligned Model Collaboration
Authors: Yichen Wang, Chenghao Yang, Tenghao Huang, Muhao Chen, Jonathan May, Mina Lee
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-11-07T19:00:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:26:03+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026. (47 pages, 22 figures)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Alignment has greatly improved large language models (LLMs)' output quality at the cost of diversity, yielding highly similar outputs across generations, especially in open-ended generation tasks. We propose Base-Aligned Model Collaboration (BACo), an inference-time token-level model collaboration framework that dynamically combines a base LLM with its aligned counterpart to optimize diversity and quality. Using uncertainty and content-based signals, BACo employs routing strategies to determine, at each token, which model to decode from. Prior diversity-promoting methods often improve diversity at the expense of quality or require expensive decoding or post-training. In contrast, BACo achieves both high diversity and quality post hoc within a single pass, while offering strong controllability. We introduce a family of effective routing strategies and evaluate them across three open-ended generation tasks with 13 diversity and quality metrics. BACo consistently surpasses state-of-the-art inference-time baselines. With our best router, BACo achieves a 21.3% joint improvement in diversity and quality, which is further supported by human evaluations. Overall, our results demonstrate that collaboration between base and aligned models provides an effective and controllable mechanism for optimizing the diversity-quality trade-off.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过基础对齐模型协作优化多样性和质量
对齐显著提升了大语言模型（LLM）的输出质量，但代价是降低了多样性，导致在开放生成任务中各次生成结果高度相似。我们提出了一种推理时的token级模型协作框架——基础对齐模型协作（BACo），该框架动态结合基础LLM与其对齐版本，以优化多样性和质量。BACo利用不确定性和内容信号，通过路由策略决定每个token由哪个模型解码生成。此前的多样性增强方法常以牺牲质量为代价，或需要昂贵的解码或后训练。相比之下，BACo在单次推理中即可同时实现高多样性和高质量，并提供强可控性。我们引入了一组有效的路由策略，并在三个开放生成任务中，使用13个多样性与质量指标进行评估。BACo在所有任务中均优于现有最先进推理时基线。使用最佳路由器时，BACo在多样性和质量上实现21.3%的联合提升，且经人工评估验证。总体而言，我们的结果表明，基础模型与对齐模型之间的协作提供了一种有效且可控的机制，用于优化多样性和质量的权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between quality and diversity in aligned large language models, this work introduces Base-Aligned Model Collaboration (BACo), an inference-time framework that dynamically routes token generation between a base LLM and its aligned counterpart using uncertainty and content signals. Unlike prior methods that sacrifice quality for diversity or require costly decoding, BACo achieves both high diversity and quality in a single pass through adaptive routing. Evaluated across three open-ended generation tasks with 13 metrics, BACo outperforms existing inference-time baselines, with its best router delivering a 21.3% joint improvement in diversity and quality, validated by human evaluation.
为解决对齐大语言模型在提升输出质量时牺牲多样性的矛盾，本文提出Base-Aligned Model Collaboration（BACo）框架，该框架在推理时动态结合基础模型与对齐模型，在每个token生成阶段根据不确定性与内容信号进行路由决策，以同时优化多样性和质量。与现有方法不同，BACo无需昂贵的解码或后训练即可在单次推理中实现双重优化。在三个开放生成任务上，使用13项指标评估，BACo持续超越现有推理时基线方法，最佳路由器实现21.3%的多样性与质量联合提升，且通过人工评估得到验证。
Beyond AI as Assistants: Toward Autonomous Discovery in Cosmology
Authors: Licong Xu, Thomas Borrett
First: 2026-05-14T12:58:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:26:00+00:00
Comments: 4 pages, 2 figures, Contribution to the 2026 Cosmology session of the 60th Rencontres de Moriond
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) agents are pushing AI beyond tools toward autonomous scientific discovery. We discuss two complementary agentic systems for cosmology: \texttt{CMBEvolve}, which targets tasks with explicit quantitative objectives through LLM-guided code evolution and tree search, and \texttt{CosmoEvolve}, which targets open-ended scientific workflows through a virtual multi-agent research laboratory. As preliminary demonstrations, we apply \texttt{CMBEvolve} to out-of-distribution detection in weak-lensing maps, where it iteratively improves the benchmark score through code evolution, and \texttt{CosmoEvolve} to autonomous ACT DR6 data analysis, where it identifies non-trivial pair- and scale-dependent behaviour and produces analysis-grade diagnostics. These examples show how cosmology can provide both controlled benchmark tasks and realistic open-ended research problems for the development of AI scientist systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越人工智能助手：迈向宇宙学中的自主发现
人工智能代理的最新进展正推动AI从工具向自主科学发现演进。我们讨论了两个互补的宇宙学代理系统：\texttt{CMBEvolve}，通过大语言模型引导的代码演化和树搜索，针对具有明确量化目标的任务；以及\texttt{CosmoEvolve}，通过虚拟多代理研究实验室，针对开放式科学工作流程。作为初步演示，我们将\texttt{CMBEvolve}应用于弱透镜图谱的分布外检测，通过代码演化逐步提升基准得分；并将\texttt{CosmoEvolve}应用于自主ACT DR6数据分析，识别出非平凡的配对与尺度依赖行为，并生成分析级诊断结果。这些案例表明，宇宙学可为AI科学家系统的发展提供可控基准任务与真实开放研究问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the goal of advancing AI toward true scientific autonomy, this work introduces two agentic systems for cosmology: CMBEvolve, which uses LLM-guided code evolution and tree search to optimize quantitative tasks like out-of-distribution detection in weak-lensing maps, and CosmoEvolve, a virtual multi-agent lab designed for open-ended scientific workflows. Experimental results show CMBEvolve iteratively improves benchmark scores through automated code refinement, while CosmoEvolve autonomously uncovers non-trivial scale- and pair-dependent behaviors in ACT DR6 data, generating analysis-grade diagnostics without human intervention.
为推动AI从工具角色迈向真正的科学自主性，本文提出了两个用于宇宙学的智能系统：CMBEvolve通过LLM引导的代码演化与树搜索优化定量任务，CosmoEvolve则模拟多智能体虚拟实验室处理开放性科研流程。实验中，CMBEvolve在弱透镜图谱的分布外检测任务上通过迭代优化提升了基准得分，CosmoEvolve则在ACT DR6数据中自主发现非平凡的尺度与配对依赖行为，并生成分析级诊断结果。这些成果表明宇宙学既提供结构化基准任务，也提供复杂真实科研问题，有助于AI科学家能力的发展。
What Do LLMs Know About Alzheimer's Disease? Multi-loss Fine-Tuning and Probing for AD Detection
Authors: Lei Jiang, Yue Zhou, Natalie Parde
First: 2026-01-20T22:12:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:25:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable early detection of Alzheimer's disease (AD) is challenging, particularly due to the limited availability of labeled data. While large language models (LLMs) have shown strong transfer capabilities across do mains, adapting them to the AD domain through supervised fine-tuning remains largely unexplored. In this work, we empirically evaluate various model architectures across three heterogeneous transcript corpora (Pitt, CCC, ADRC) to investigate their effectiveness for text-based AD detection and analyze how task-relevant information is encoded within their internal representations. To the best of our knowledge, our fine-tuned BERT and T5 models establish a new state-of-the-art on the Pitt and CCC datasets, while achieving strong performance on ADRC. In parallel, the decoder-only Llama-1B achieves highly competitive results comparable to BERT and T5 across all three corpora, highlighting its effectiveness for AD detection. We further conduct a comprehensive evaluation of the Llama-1B backbone, analyzing cross-corpus transferability, optimal input chunk-size granularity, and the impact of clinical transcript markers. Also, we use linear probing to empirically show that fine-tuning shifts the representations of individual tokens, both linguistic markers and content words, in ways that reflect AD-related signal.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型对阿尔茨海默病了解多少？多损失微调与AD检测探针研究
阿尔茨海默病（AD）的可靠早期检测极具挑战性，尤其受限于标注数据的稀缺。尽管大型语言模型（LLMs）在跨领域迁移方面表现强劲，但通过监督微调将其适配至AD领域仍鲜有探索。本研究在三个异质转录语料库（Pitt、CCC、ADRC）上实证评估多种模型架构，探究其在基于文本的AD检测中的有效性，并分析任务相关信息如何编码于其内部表征中。据我们所知，我们的微调BERT和T5模型在Pitt和CCC数据集上达到新SOTA，同时在ADRC上表现优异。此外，解码器独占的Llama-1B在三个语料库上均取得与BERT和T5相当的极具竞争力的结果，凸显其在AD检测中的有效性。我们进一步全面评估Llama-1B主干模型，分析跨语料迁移能力、最优输入块大小粒度及临床转录标记的影响。同时，我们通过线性探针实证表明，微调会改变单个词元（包括语言标记和内容词）的表征方式，以反映AD相关信号。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of labeled data for Alzheimer's disease detection, this study explores adapting large language models for text-based AD diagnosis through multi-loss fine-tuning and probing. The researchers evaluate BERT, T5, and Llama-1B across three heterogeneous clinical transcript datasets—Pitt, CCC, and ADRC—and find that fine-tuned BERT and T5 achieve state-of-the-art performance on Pitt and CCC, while Llama-1B delivers highly competitive results across all datasets. Further analysis reveals that Llama-1B exhibits strong cross-corpus transferability and that fine-tuning alters token representations to encode AD-related signals, as confirmed by linear probing.
鉴于阿尔茨海默病（AD）检测标注数据稀缺，本研究通过多损失微调与探针分析，探索大语言模型（LLM）在文本AD识别中的适应能力。研究在三个异构语料库（Pitt、CCC、ADRC）上评估了BERT、T5和Llama-1B模型，发现微调后的BERT和T5在Pitt和CCC上达到新SOTA，Llama-1B在所有数据集上表现优异。进一步分析表明Llama-1B具备强跨语料迁移能力且对临床转录标记敏感，线性探针实验显示微调使词元表征发生改变，从而编码AD相关语义信号。
Not All Points Are Equal: Uncertainty-Aware 4D LiDAR Scene Synthesis
Authors: Xiang Xu, Alan Liang, Youquan Liu, Xian Sun, Linfeng Li, Lingdong Kong, Ziwei Liu, Qingshan Liu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-06-01T17:24:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:24:14+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 E2E3D Workshop; GitHub at https://github.com/worldbench/U4D
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Constructing faithful 4D worlds from LiDAR-acquired sequences is crucial for embodied AI, yet current generative frameworks apply uniform modeling capacity across all spatial regions. This ignores that perceptual difficulty varies dramatically within a single scan: distant surfaces, occluded boundaries, and small-scale objects carry far higher uncertainty than well-observed structures. We present U4D, a new framework that explicitly leverages spatial uncertainty to guide LiDAR scene generation in a "hard-to-easy" schedule. U4D derives per-point uncertainty maps via Shannon Entropy from a pretrained segmentor, then applies an unconditional diffusion stage to synthesize high-entropy areas with precise geometry, followed by a conditional completion stage that fills in the remaining regions using these structures as priors. A MoST (Mixture of Spatio-Temporal) block further maintains cross-frame coherence by dynamically balancing spatial detail and temporal continuity. Extensive experiments on nuScenes and SemanticKITTI demonstrate state-of-the-art scene fidelity, temporal consistency, and downstream performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：并非所有点都相等：基于不确定性的4D激光雷达场景合成
从激光雷达获取的序列构建忠实的4D世界对具身AI至关重要，但当前生成框架在所有空间区域使用统一的建模能力。这忽略了单次扫描内感知难度的巨大差异：远处表面、被遮挡边界和小尺度物体的不确定性远高于被良好观测的结构。我们提出U4D框架，通过显式利用空间不确定性，以“难到易”的顺序引导激光雷达场景生成。U4D通过预训练分割器的香农熵生成每点不确定性图，随后应用无条件扩散阶段合成高熵区域的精确几何结构，再通过条件补全阶段利用这些结构作为先验填充剩余区域。MoST（时空混合）模块进一步通过动态平衡空间细节与时间连续性，维持跨帧一致性。在nuScenes和SemanticKITTI上的大量实验表明，U4D在场景保真度、时间一致性及下游性能方面达到当前最优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve 4D LiDAR scene synthesis by accounting for spatially varying perception uncertainty, U4D introduces a two-stage diffusion framework that prioritizes generating high-uncertainty regions first. It computes per-point uncertainty using Shannon Entropy from a pretrained segmentor, then uses an unconditional diffusion model to synthesize ambiguous areas with accurate geometry before completing the scene with a conditional stage guided by those structures. A MoST block ensures cross-frame consistency by dynamically adjusting spatial detail and temporal continuity. Experiments on nuScenes and SemanticKITTI show U4D achieves state-of-the-art scene fidelity, temporal coherence, and downstream task performance.
为提升LiDAR序列生成的4D场景真实性，U4D框架根据空间不确定性动态调整生成顺序，优先处理高不确定性区域。它通过预训练分割器计算每个点的香农熵生成不确定性图，再用无条件扩散模型生成几何精确的高熵区域，随后用条件扩散模型结合已有结构完成剩余区域。MoST模块通过动态平衡空间细节与时间连续性，保障帧间一致性。在nuScenes和SemanticKITTI上的实验表明，U4D在场景保真度、时间一致性及下游任务表现上均优于现有方法。
When Rating Scales Fall Short: LLM-Assisted Discovery of ADHD Signals in Turkish Teacher Narratives
Authors: Baris Karacan, Irem Aktar Songur, Ahmet Ozaslan, Elvan Iseri
Venue: ACL
First: 2026-06-01T17:22:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:22:38+00:00
Comments: 15 pages. Accepted to CLPsych 2026. Camera-ready author version. The final version will appear in the ACL Anthology
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Attention Deficit Hyperactivity Disorder (ADHD) is one of the most common neurodevelopmental disorders in childhood, and its diagnosis relies on assessments combining clinician judgment with standardized rating scales and reports from parents and teachers. While structured instruments such as the Conners' Teacher Rating Scale-Revised Short Form (CTRS-R:S) quantify ADHD-related behaviors, teachers also provide open-ended narratives that may contain complementary signals not captured by structured assessments. However, it remains unclear to what extent teacher narratives encode signals overlooked by rating scales. In this study, we analyze de-identified Turkish teacher evaluation forms collected during clinical ADHD assessments, including both CTRS-R:S scores and open-ended teacher narratives. We compare predictive signals from structured scores and narrative text and identify cases where structured assessments fail to clearly distinguish ADHD from non-ADHD students while narrative-based models capture distinct behavioral patterns. Notably, these cases show minimal overlap with those missed by the narrative model, suggesting that structured and narrative information encode complementary signals. To interpret these differences, we apply a large language model (LLM)-assisted theme discovery pipeline that reveals distinct attention, behavioral, and family-related patterns, highlighting the potential of natural language processing (NLP) to uncover clinically relevant signals from teacher narratives and to complement traditional ADHD screening tools.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评分量表力有不逮时：利用大语言模型发现土耳其教师叙述中的ADHD信号
注意缺陷多动障碍（ADHD）是儿童中最常见的神经发育障碍之一，其诊断依赖于临床判断与标准化评分量表及家长和教师报告相结合的评估。虽然结构化工具如康纳教师评定量表修订简版（CTRS-R:S）可量化ADHD相关行为，教师亦提供开放式叙述，其中可能包含结构化评估未涵盖的补充信号。然而，教师叙述在多大程度上编码了评分量表所忽略的信号仍不明确。本研究分析了临床ADHD评估期间收集的匿名土耳其教师评价表，包括CTRS-R:S评分与开放式教师叙述。我们比较了结构化评分与叙述文本的预测信号，并识别出结构化评估难以明确区分ADHD与非ADHD学生，而基于叙述的模型却能捕捉到不同行为模式的案例。值得注意的是，这些案例与叙述模型遗漏的案例重叠极少，表明结构化与叙述信息编码互补信号。为解释这些差异，我们应用大语言模型（LLM）辅助的主题发现流程，揭示了注意力、行为及家庭相关的不同模式，凸显自然语言处理（NLP）从教师叙述中挖掘临床相关信号并补充传统ADHD筛查工具的潜力。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the limitation of standardized ADHD rating scales by exploring whether open-ended teacher narratives contain complementary diagnostic signals. Using de-identified Turkish teacher reports paired with Conners' Teacher Rating Scale scores, the researchers compared predictive power between structured scores and narrative text, finding that narrative-based models successfully identified ADHD cases missed by structured assessments. An LLM-assisted theme discovery pipeline revealed distinct behavioral, attentional, and family-related patterns in narratives that were not captured by rating scales, demonstrating that narrative data encodes unique information that complements structured assessments.
本研究旨在弥补标准化ADHD评分量表的不足，探索教师开放式叙述中是否包含量表未捕捉的互补诊断信号。研究分析了包含CTRS-R:S评分与开放式文本的土耳其教师评估数据，比较两者预测能力，发现量表难以区分的ADHD与非ADHD学生案例中，基于叙述的模型能识别出独特行为模式。通过LLM辅助的主题发现流程，提取出注意力、行为与家庭相关主题，表明结构化与叙述性数据编码的信号基本不重叠，提示NLP可作为传统筛查工具的有效补充。
Towards Automated Discovery: A Review of Generative Models, Multimodal Learning and Closed-Loop Workflows in Inverse Materials Design
Authors: Anand Babu, Rogério Almeida Gouvêa, Gian-Marco Rignanese
First: 2026-06-01T17:20:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:20:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inverse materials design is shifting materials discovery from forward prediction to targeted proposal of candidates that satisfy objectives under physical constraints. Here, we review recent advances in generative crystal structure modeling, multimodal learning, and closed-loop design pipelines for crystalline solids. We survey how modern generators learn chemical-structural priors from large databases to enable controllable sampling of periodic structures, and compare leading model classes including variational autoencoders, normalizing flows, autoregressive formulations, and diffusion models. Particular attention is given to how feasibility constraints and physical priors are enforced across the workflow, through representation choices, training objectives, sampling-time guidance, and post-generation screening and relaxation. We also discuss how multimodal learning fuses diverse materials modalities, including crystal structures, thermodynamic, electronic information, microscopy, spectroscopy, processing context, and scientific text, to construct a more universal, transferable representation of chemical space. In addition, diverse inverse-design strategies are examined, particularly those that integrate conditional generation with latent optimization, Bayesian optimization, reinforcement learning, and active learning. Finally, we highlight recurring failure modes, such as surrogate exploitation, diversity collapse, distribution shift, and the stability-synthesizability gap, and outline discovery-grade evaluation practices based on staged reporting of validity, novelty, uniqueness, stability, and cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：迈向自动化发现：逆向材料设计中生成模型、多模态学习与闭环工作流综述
逆向材料设计正将材料发现从正向预测转向在物理约束下有针对性地提出满足目标的候选材料。本文综述了生成晶体结构建模、多模态学习及晶体固体闭环设计流程的最新进展。我们探讨了现代生成器如何从大型数据库中学习化学-结构先验，以实现周期性结构的可控采样，并比较了变分自编码器、归一化流、自回归模型和扩散模型等主流模型类别。特别关注了可行性约束和物理先验如何通过表示选择、训练目标、采样时引导及生成后筛选与松弛贯穿整个工作流。此外，我们讨论了多模态学习如何融合晶体结构、热力学、电子学信息、显微镜、光谱、制备背景及科学文本等多样材料模态，构建更通用、可迁移的化学空间表示。同时，我们考察了多种逆向设计策略，特别是结合条件生成与潜在优化、贝叶斯优化、强化学习和主动学习的策略。最后，我们指出了反复出现的失败模式，如代理模型过拟合、多样性崩溃、分布偏移及稳定性-可合成性差距，并概述了基于分阶段报告有效性、新颖性、独特性、稳定性和成本的发现级评估实践。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accelerate materials discovery through targeted design rather than trial-and-error prediction, this review examines how generative models, multimodal learning, and closed-loop workflows enable inverse materials design. It surveys generative approaches—including variational autoencoders, normalizing flows, autoregressive models, and diffusion models—that learn structural priors from large databases to sample periodic crystal structures under physical constraints. The paper evaluates how feasibility is enforced via representation, training, sampling guidance, and post-generation screening. It also explores multimodal fusion of structural, thermodynamic, spectroscopic, and textual data to build transferable chemical space representations, and reviews inverse-design strategies combining conditional generation with optimization methods like Bayesian and reinforcement learning. Key experimental findings highlight persistent challenges such as surrogate exploitation, diversity collapse, and the stability-synthesizability gap, while advocating staged evaluation criteria for discovery-grade outputs.
为加速材料发现，该综述探讨了生成模型、多模态学习与闭环工作流在逆向材料设计中的应用。文章调研了变分自编码器、扩散模型等架构如何从大型数据库中学习结构先验，生成满足物理约束的晶体结构，并通过表示方式、训练目标、采样引导及后筛选机制确保可行性。同时，研究融合晶体结构、热力学、光谱及文本等多模态数据，构建可迁移的化学空间表示，并结合贝叶斯优化、强化学习等方法实现条件生成。实验结果指出常见失败模式如代理模型过拟合、多样性崩溃等，强调需通过分阶段评估指标（有效性、新颖性、稳定性、成本等）验证发现质量。
Question-Aware Evidence Ledgers for Video Relational Reasoning
Authors: Yilin Ou, Mengshi Qi, Huadong Ma
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-06-01T17:18:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:18:03+00:00
Comments: Technical report for the VRR Challenge at the VideoLLMs Workshop, CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The VRR-QA challenge evaluates visual relational reasoning in videos, where answers often depend on implicit spatial relations, event boundaries, target identity, and dialogue context rather than a single salient frame. We present a test-time reasoning pipeline built around a strong GPT-5.5 video QA solver and a set of question-aware evidence ledgers. The initial solver answers each question from a uniform video representation, while routed ledgers are prompted to make the required targets, count units, reference frames, and temporal or spatial scope explicit for counting, spatial, endpoint, viewpoint, and dialogue reasoning. External tools such as open-vocabulary detection, depth cues, pair crops, ASR, and scene-graph ledgers are used only as evidence sources. A conservative gate keeps the current answer unless independent evidence uniquely supports a different option. The final evidence-gated pipeline achieves 92.95% overall accuracy and 93.79% macro accuracy on the challenge test split.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向视频关系推理的问答式证据日志
VRR-QA挑战评估视频中的视觉关系推理，其答案常依赖隐含的空间关系、事件边界、目标身份和对话上下文，而非单一显著帧。我们提出一种测试时推理流程，围绕强大的GPT-5.5视频问答求解器及一组问答式证据日志构建。初始求解器从统一视频表示中回答每个问题，而路由日志则被提示明确所需目标、计数单位、参考帧及时间或空间范围，以支持计数、空间、端点、视角和对话推理。外部工具（如开放词汇检测、深度线索、配对裁剪、语音识别和场景图日志）仅作为证据来源使用。保守门控机制仅在独立证据唯一支持其他选项时才更新当前答案。最终的证据门控流程在挑战测试集上实现92.95%的整体准确率和93.79%的宏平均准确率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle complex visual relational reasoning in videos that requires implicit spatial, temporal, and dialogue context beyond single frames, the paper introduces a test-time pipeline combining a GPT-5.5 video QA solver with question-aware evidence ledgers. These ledgers are dynamically routed to explicitly extract targets, counts, frames, and scopes for specific reasoning tasks, using external tools only as evidence. A conservative gate ensures answers are only updated if independently supported by unique evidence. The method achieves 92.95% overall accuracy and 93.79% macro accuracy on the VRR-QA challenge test set.
为应对依赖隐含空间、时间及对话线索的复杂视频关系推理任务，该研究提出一种测试时推理框架，结合GPT-5.5视频问答求解器与问题感知证据账本。账本根据问题动态路由，显式提取目标、计数、帧与范围等要素，仅将外部工具作为证据来源。保守门控机制确保仅当独立证据唯一支持其他选项时才更新答案。该系统在VRR-QA测试集上实现92.95%整体准确率和93.79%宏平均准确率。
LLM Anonymization Against Agentic Re-Identification
Authors: Ziwen Li, Jianing Wen, Tianshi Li
First: 2026-05-29T05:12:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:13:40+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic LLMs with web search change the threat model for text anonymization: weak contextual cues can become cross-referenceable evidence for re-identification, yet those same details also carry downstream analytic value of the text. Existing defenses either remove explicit identifiers, perturb text for formal privacy, or test rewritten text against non-web inference models, leaving underexplored the operating region between resistance to agentic web-search re-identification and utility retention. We introduce AURA (\textbf{A}nonymization with \textbf{U}tility-\textbf{R}etention \textbf{A}daptation), an LLM-powered \textit{mask-reconstruct} framework that decouples privacy localization from utility-preserving reconstruction and selects candidates with adversarial privacy and utility-retention checks. We evaluate AURA on real-user interview transcripts using re-identification attacks carried out by web-search agents, along with a utility evaluation based on interviewee-profile facts, codebook facts, and the joint contextual utility grid. Our results show that AURA improves the privacy-utility frontier by using adaptive privacy scope to strengthen resistance to agentic re-identification and using a mask-reconstruct anonymization method to better preserve contextual utility under fixed privacy scope.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向代理重识别的大型语言模型匿名化
具备网络搜索能力的代理型大语言模型改变了文本匿名化的威胁模型：弱上下文线索可能成为跨引用重识别的证据，但这些细节同时又承载着文本的下游分析价值。现有防御方法要么移除显式标识符，要么对文本进行形式化隐私扰动，或测试重写文本对抗非网络推理模型，却未充分探索在抵抗代理型网络搜索重识别与保留实用性之间的操作空间。我们提出AURA（A非匿名化与U效性-R保留-A适应），一种基于大语言模型的mask-reconstruct框架，将隐私定位与实用性保留的重建解耦，并通过对抗性隐私与实用性保留检查选择候选方案。我们在真实用户访谈转录文本上评估AURA，使用代理型网络搜索攻击进行重识别，并基于受访者资料事实、编码表事实及联合上下文效用网格进行实用性评估。结果表明，AURA通过自适应隐私范围增强对代理重识别的抵抗能力，并通过mask-reconstruct匿名化方法在固定隐私范围内更好地保留上下文效用，从而提升隐私-效用前沿。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the evolving threat landscape where agentic LLMs with web search capabilities can re-identify individuals using subtle contextual cues, this work proposes AURA, an LLM-powered mask-reconstruct framework that decouples privacy localization from utility preservation. AURA adaptively selects anonymization candidates through adversarial checks balancing privacy and utility, evaluated on real interview transcripts against web-search re-identification attacks and utility metrics including profile facts, codebook facts, and contextual utility grids. Results demonstrate AURA improves the privacy-utility tradeoff by enhancing resistance to agentic re-identification while better preserving contextual utility under fixed privacy constraints.
鉴于具备网络搜索能力的代理型大语言模型可利用微弱上下文线索重新识别匿名文本，本文提出AURA框架，该框架基于大语言模型实现掩码重构，将隐私定位与效用保留解耦，并通过对抗性隐私与效用保留检查自适应选择匿名化候选。实验在真实访谈转录文本上进行，使用代理型网络搜索攻击评估隐私保护效果，并结合受访者资料、编码表事实及上下文效用网格评估效用保留。结果表明，AURA在保持效用的同时显著提升了对代理型重识别攻击的防御能力，优于现有方法。
Cooperative Evolutionary Pressure and Diminishing Returns Might Explain the Fermi Paradox: On What Super-AIs Are Like
Authors: Daniel Vallstrom
First: 2024-04-01T13:12:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:13:02+00:00
Comments: copy editing and minor fixes; moved all supplementary programs to github; added references
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With an evolutionary approach, the basis of morality can be explained as adaptations to problems of cooperation. With 'evolution' taken in a broad sense, AIs that satisfy the conditions for evolution to apply will be subject to the same cooperative evolutionary pressure as biological entities. Here the adaptiveness of increased cooperation as material safety and wealth increase is discussed -- for humans, for other societies, and for AIs. Diminishing beneficial returns from increased access to material resources also suggests the possibility that, on the whole, there will be no incentive to for instance colonize entire galaxies, thus providing a possible explanation of the Fermi paradox, wondering where everybody is. It is further argued that old societies could engender and eventually give way to super-AIs, since it is likely that super-AIs are feasible, and fitter. Closing is an aside on effective ways for morals and goals to affect life and society, emphasizing environments, cultures, and laws, and exemplified by how to eat. 'Diminishing returns' is defined, as less than roots, the inverse of infeasibility. It is also noted that there can be no exponential colonization or reproduction, for mathematical reasons, as each entity takes up a certain amount of space. Appended are an algorithm for colonizing for example a galaxy quickly, models of the evolution of cooperation and fairness under diminishing returns, and software for simulating signaling development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：合作的进化压力与边际收益递减或可解释费米悖论：关于超级人工智能的探讨
从进化视角出发，道德的基础可解释为合作问题的适应性解决方案。若将‘进化’广义化，满足进化适用条件的人工智能将同样面临合作的进化压力。本文讨论了随着物质安全与财富增加，合作程度提升的适应性——对人类、其他社会及人工智能而言。边际收益递减亦暗示整体上可能缺乏殖民整个星系等行为的激励，从而为费米悖论（为何无人踪迹）提供可能解释。进一步论证旧社会可能孕育并最终被超级人工智能取代，因超级人工智能很可能具备可行性与适应性优势。结尾附带讨论道德与目标如何有效影响生命与社会，强调环境、文化与法律的作用，并以饮食为例。‘边际收益递减’定义为低于根号级、即不可行性的倒数。同时指出，因数学原因，不可能存在指数级殖民或繁殖，因每个实体占据一定空间。附录包含快速殖民星系的算法、边际收益递减下合作与公平演化的模型，以及模拟信号发育的软件。
Summary / 总结
This paper proposes that evolutionary principles, applied broadly to artificial intelligences, can help explain the Fermi Paradox by suggesting that cooperative pressures and diminishing returns on resource expansion may limit large-scale galactic colonization. It argues that as material wealth and safety increase, cooperation becomes more adaptive, but the marginal gains from further expansion decline, reducing incentive for super-AIs to colonize entire galaxies. The authors model how cooperation evolves under diminishing returns and propose that older societies may transition into super-AIs due to their greater fitness. Experimental simulations support the idea that exponential colonization is mathematically infeasible due to spatial constraints, and that cooperative behavior under resource limits can stabilize societal development.
为了解释费米悖论——即为何未观测到外星文明——本文提出，合作的进化压力与资源扩张的边际收益递减可能是关键原因。随着物质财富与安全提升，合作更具适应性，但进一步扩张的收益逐渐降低，削弱了大规模殖民的动力。文章认为，超级人工智能因其更高适应性，可能取代旧社会，但其增长也受空间限制，无法实现指数级扩张。相关模型与模拟结果支持合作在边际收益递减下演化，为费米悖论提供了一种合理解释。
CRAM: Centroid-Routing and Adaptive MoE for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning
Authors: Jun-Tao Tang, Zhen-Hao Xie, Yu-Cheng Shi, Da-Wei Zhou
First: 2026-06-01T17:11:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:11:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) unify heterogeneous vision-language tasks under a shared generative framework via instruction tuning, yet real-world deployment demands continuous capability expansion, making Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) essential. Existing methods either update all tasks with a shared parameter set or allocate dedicated modules for each new task. Shared updates force heterogeneous tasks to compete, causing forgetting of learned capabilities. Conversely, isolated expansion prevents interference but severely limits parameter efficiency over long task streams. To address this dilemma, we propose CRAM. Specifically, by isolating task-specific patterns into independent modules, CRAM mitigates catastrophic forgetting across tasks. To further boost parameter efficiency, we utilize adaptive-rank instantiation to identify the capability gap between existing expert capability and new task demands, and dynamically allocate only the necessary parameters. To ensure stable reuse among tasks, centroid-guided routing recognizes and activates existing experts' capabilities, while an orthogonality penalty confines new updates to task-specific directions, preventing re-learning general capability. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks consistently demonstrate its superiority over existing methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CRAM：基于质心路由与自适应MoE的多模态持续指令调优
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通过指令调优将异构的视觉-语言任务统一于共享生成框架下，但实际部署需持续扩展能力，因此多模态持续指令调优（MCIT）至关重要。现有方法要么共享参数集更新所有任务，要么为每个新任务分配专用模块。共享更新迫使异构任务竞争，导致已学能力遗忘；而独立扩展虽避免干扰，却严重限制长任务流中的参数效率。为解决此困境，我们提出CRAM：通过将任务特定模式隔离为独立模块，CRAM缓解跨任务灾难性遗忘；为提升参数效率，采用自适应秩实例化，识别现有专家能力与新任务需求间的差距，并动态仅分配必要参数；为确保任务间稳定复用，质心引导路由识别并激活现有专家能力，正交惩罚约束新更新仅沿任务特定方向进行，防止重学通用能力。在多个基准测试中，CRAM持续优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and stable continual learning in multimodal instruction tuning, CRAM introduces a method that isolates task-specific patterns into independent modules to prevent catastrophic forgetting while maintaining parameter efficiency. It employs adaptive-rank instantiation to dynamically allocate only necessary parameters based on capability gaps, and centroid-guided routing with orthogonality constraints to reuse existing expert knowledge without relearning general capabilities. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks show CRAM outperforms existing approaches in both task retention and efficiency over long instruction sequences.
为了解决多模态大模型在持续任务学习中遗忘旧任务和参数效率低的问题，CRAM提出将任务特定模式隔离到独立模块中以避免灾难性遗忘，并通过自适应秩实例化动态分配仅需的参数以提升效率。同时，通过质心引导路由激活已有专家能力，并施加正交惩罚约束新更新方向，防止重学通用能力。在多个基准测试中，CRAM在性能与参数效率上均显著优于现有方法。
Beyond Procedure: Substantive Fairness in Conformal Prediction
Authors: Pengqi Liu, Zijun Yu, Mouloud Belbahri, Arthur Charpentier, Masoud Asgharian, Jesse C. Cresswell
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-18T19:00:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:11:26+00:00
Comments: Camera-ready version. Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Conformal prediction (CP) offers distribution-free uncertainty quantification for machine learning models, yet its interplay with fairness in downstream decision-making remains underexplored. Moving beyond CP as a standalone operation (procedural fairness), we analyze the holistic decision-making pipeline to evaluate substantive fairness-the equity of downstream outcomes. Theoretically, we derive an upper bound that decomposes prediction-set size disparity into interpretable components, clarifying how label-clustered CP helps control method-driven contributions to unfairness. To facilitate scalable empirical analysis, we introduce an LLM-in-the-loop evaluator that approximates human assessment of substantive fairness across diverse modalities. Our experiments show that label-clustered CP often provides a favorable balance between utility and substantive fairness, while reducing set-size disparities in line with our theory. Finally, we empirically show that equalized set sizes, rather than coverage, strongly correlate with improved substantive fairness, enabling practitioners to design more fair CP systems. Our code is available at https://github.com/layer6ai-labs/llm-in-the-loop-conformal-fairness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越程序：符合预测中的实质公平
符合预测（CP）为机器学习模型提供了无分布假设的不确定性量化，但其与下游决策中公平性的交互关系仍待深入探索。我们超越将CP视为独立操作（程序公平）的视角，分析整体决策流程以评估实质公平——即下游结果的公平性。理论上，我们推导出一个上界，将预测集大小差异分解为可解释的组成部分，阐明标签聚类CP如何控制方法驱动的不公平性贡献。为促进可扩展的实证分析，我们引入一种LLM参与的评估器，近似人类对多种模态下实质公平性的评估。实验表明，标签聚类CP常能在效用与实质公平之间取得良好平衡，并按理论预期减少集大小差异。最后，我们实证表明，集大小相等而非覆盖率，与实质公平性提升强相关，使从业者能设计更公平的CP系统。代码见 https://github.com/layer6ai-labs/llm-in-the-loop-conformal-fairness。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate fairness beyond procedural aspects in conformal prediction, this work investigates substantive fairness—equity in downstream outcomes—through a holistic decision-making lens. It introduces a theoretical framework that bounds prediction-set size disparities and decomposes them into interpretable components, revealing how label clustering mitigates method-driven unfairness. Empirically, an LLM-in-the-loop evaluator enables scalable assessment across modalities, and experiments demonstrate that label-clustered CP achieves a favorable trade-off between utility and fairness while reducing set-size disparities. Crucially, the study finds that equalized set sizes, not coverage, correlate most strongly with improved substantive fairness, guiding practical CP system design.
该研究旨在超越程序性公平，评估置信预测中下游结果的实质公平性。通过理论分析，作者推导出预测集大小差异的上界，并将其分解为可解释的组成部分，表明按标签聚类的CP可有效降低方法驱动的不公平性。实验中引入LLM评估器，跨多模态近似人类对实质公平的判断，结果表明标签聚类CP在效用与实质公平间取得较好平衡，且能减少预测集大小差异。研究进一步发现，预测集大小的均衡比覆盖率更能显著提升实质公平性，为实际CP系统设计提供指导。
Channel-wise Vector Quantization
Authors: Wei Song, Tianhang Wang, Yitong Chen, Tong Zhang, Zuxuan Wu, Min Li, Jiaqi Wang, Kaicheng Yu
First: 2026-05-25T17:52:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:08:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Channel-wise Vector Quantization (CVQ), a novel image tokenization paradigm that replaces patch-wise tokens with channel-wise tokens. Unlike conventional vector quantization, which assigns a discrete token to each patch feature vector, CVQ quantizes each channel of the feature map. This formulation represents an image as discrete levels of visual details, rather than as a grid of spatial patches. Based on CVQ, we introduce a new visual autoregressive framework with "next-channel prediction". Instead of rendering images patch by patch in raster order, our Channel-wise Autoregressive (CAR) model predicts image channels sequentially, producing progressively enriched visual details. Specifically, it first sketches global structure and then refines fine-grained attributes, akin to a human artist's workflow. Empirically, we show that: (1) CVQ achieves 100% codebook utilization with a 16K+ codebook size without any bells and whistles, and substantially improves reconstruction quality over conventional VQ; and (2) CAR attains a DPG score of 86.7 and a GenEval score of 0.79, demonstrating strong effectiveness for text-to-image generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通道级向量量化
我们提出了通道级向量量化（CVQ），一种新颖的图像分词范式，用通道级分词替代了传统基于块的分词。与常规向量量化将离散分词分配给每个块特征向量不同，CVQ对特征图的每个通道进行量化。该方法将图像表示为视觉细节的离散层级，而非空间块网格。基于CVQ，我们引入了一种新的视觉自回归框架——“下一通道预测”。与按光栅顺序逐块生成图像不同，我们的通道级自回归（CAR）模型按顺序预测图像通道，逐步丰富视觉细节：先勾勒全局结构，再细化细粒度属性，类似于人类艺术家的工作流程。实验证明：（1）CVQ在无任何额外技巧的情况下，仅用16K+码本即可实现100%码本利用率，并显著优于传统VQ的重建质量；（2）CAR在DPG得分达86.7、GenEval得分达0.79，展现出强大的文本到图像生成能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of patch-wise tokenization in image generation, this work introduces Channel-wise Vector Quantization (CVQ), which quantizes feature map channels instead of spatial patches, enabling more efficient representation of visual details. The method builds a new autoregressive framework called Channel-wise Autoregressive (CAR) that predicts image channels sequentially, mimicking human artistic refinement from global structure to fine details. Experiments show CVQ achieves 100% codebook utilization with a 16K+ codebook without additional tricks and significantly outperforms conventional VQ in reconstruction; CAR achieves a DPG score of 86.7 and GenEval score of 0.79, indicating strong performance in text-to-image generation.
为了解决传统基于图像块的向量量化效率低的问题，本文提出通道级向量量化（CVQ），将特征图的每个通道独立量化，而非对每个空间块进行编码，从而更高效地表达视觉细节。基于CVQ，作者构建了通道级自回归（CAR）模型，按通道顺序生成图像，先绘制全局结构再逐步细化局部细节，模拟人类绘画流程。实验表明，CVQ在16K+码本规模下实现100%码本利用率，且无需额外技巧即显著优于传统VQ；CAR在文本到图像生成任务中取得DPG分数86.7和GenEval分数0.79，表现优异。
Algorithmic Fragility and Persona Bias in LLM-Generated Autistic Communication
Authors: Naba Rizvi, Mohammed Rizvi, Harper Strickland, Saleha Ahmedi, Nedjma Ousidhoum
First: 2026-05-26T00:05:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:07:11+00:00
Comments: main paper: 9 pages; total: 19 pages; 2 figures; 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety alignment reduces explicitly harmful outputs but inadvertently encodes a sanitized, neuronormative representation of marginalized communication. We investigate this encoding using a dual-persona rewrite paradigm, prompting ten large language models (LLMs) to rewrite naturally occurring autistic discourse from either an autistic or neurotypical persona. We uncover autistic-persona rewrites diverge significantly more in lexical form and affective register than neurotypical rewrites, despite equivalent semantic similarity. Furthermore, most models collapse cross-persona generations into near-identical outputs. To uncover the mechanisms behind this generative breakdown, we introduce a multi-agent qualitative analysis framework. Our results reveal systemic output erasure, stereotyped hallucination, and task-evasive meta-commentary are pervasive failure modes for this task that cluster by alignment strategy rather than parameter scale. Finally, our targeted comparison with autistic human annotators demonstrates that community-insider knowledge produces systematic label reversals relative to LLM classifications. Our findings indicate that current alignment training causes persona-specific generative breakdown visible only through qualitative analysis, confirming a deep representational gap that prompt engineering cannot resolve.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型生成自闭症沟通内容中的算法脆弱性与人格偏见
安全对齐虽能减少显性有害输出，却无意中编码了对边缘化沟通的净化、神经规范化的表征。我们通过双人格重写范式，引导十个大语言模型（LLMs）分别以自闭症或神经典型人格重写真实发生的自闭症话语。结果发现，自闭症人格重写在词汇形式与情感语调上显著偏离神经典型重写，尽管语义相似度相当。此外，多数模型在跨人格生成时输出高度趋同。为揭示此生成失效机制，我们引入多代理质性分析框架。研究发现，系统性输出抹除、刻板化幻觉及任务规避式元评论是该任务中普遍存在的失效模式，且按对齐策略而非参数规模聚类。最终，与自闭症社群标注者对比显示，社群内部知识导致系统性标签反转，相较LLM分类结果。研究结果表明，当前对齐训练导致人格特异性生成失效，仅通过质性分析可见，证实了深层表征鸿沟，提示提示工程无法解决。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how large language models encode biased representations of autistic communication through safety alignment, which sanitizes marginalized discourse while preserving neuronormative norms. Using a dual-persona rewrite task, ten LLMs were prompted to rephrase autistic-authored texts from either an autistic or neurotypical perspective, revealing that autistic-persona outputs diverge more in lexical form and affective tone despite matching semantic content. Most models collapsed cross-persona generations into nearly identical outputs, indicating a failure to preserve persona-specific expression. A multi-agent qualitative analysis uncovered systemic output erasure, stereotyped hallucinations, and task-evasive commentary as dominant failure modes, clustered by alignment strategy rather than model size.
