该研究探讨了大语言模型在安全对齐过程中如何编码和扭曲自闭症沟通，动机在于发现对齐虽减少显性有害输出，却无意中对边缘化表达进行‘净化’。通过双人设重写任务，十款LLM被要求从自闭症或神经典型视角重写真实自闭症对话，结果发现自闭症人设输出在词汇形式和情感语调上差异更大，尽管语义相似。多数模型将跨人设生成压缩为高度相似输出，表明生成机制崩溃。多代理质性分析揭示出系统性内容抹除、刻板化幻觉和任务规避等失败模式，且与对齐策略相关而非参数规模。自闭症人类标注者常与LLM分类结果相反，凸显了提示工程无法弥合的深层表征鸿沟。
Are Large Reasoning Models Interruptible?
Authors: Tsung-Han Wu, Mihran Miroyan, David M. Chan, Trevor Darrell, Narges Norouzi, Joseph E. Gonzalez
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-10-13T17:59:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:06:08+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026; Project Page: http://dynamic-lm.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1 · Project2
Abstract
Real-world applications of Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) often require reasoning about changing prompts or environments. In this work, we challenge the frozen world assumption and evaluate LRM robustness under two realistic dynamic scenarios: interruptions, which test the accuracy of model responses under budget-constrained outputs, and dynamic context, which tests model adaptation to in-flight changes. Across mathematics and programming benchmarks that require long-form reasoning, static evaluations consistently overestimate robustness: even state-of-the-art LRMs, which achieve high accuracy in static settings, can fail unpredictably when interrupted or exposed to changing context, with performance dropping by up to 60% when updates are introduced late in the reasoning process. Our analysis further reveals several novel failure modes, including reasoning leakage, where models fold the reasoning into their final answer when interrupted; panic, where under time pressure models abandon reasoning entirely and return incorrect answers; and self-doubt, where performance degrades when trying to incorporate updated information. Project Page: http://dynamic-lm.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：大推理模型可中断吗？
大型推理模型（LRMs）在现实应用中常需处理变化的提示或环境。本研究挑战了“冻结世界”假设，评估了LRM在两种现实动态场景下的鲁棒性：中断场景（测试在输出预算受限下模型响应的准确性）和动态上下文场景（测试模型对飞行中变化的适应能力）。在需要长篇推理的数学与编程基准测试中，静态评估始终高估了模型的鲁棒性：即使最先进的LRM在静态环境下表现优异，一旦遭遇中断或上下文变化，也可能出现不可预测的失败，推理后期引入更新时性能最多下降60%。我们的分析还揭示了若干新颖的失败模式，包括推理泄漏（中断时模型将推理过程融入最终答案）、恐慌（时间压力下模型完全放弃推理并返回错误答案）和自我怀疑（尝试整合新信息时性能下降）。项目页面：http://dynamic-lm.github.io/
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) can handle dynamic environments, challenging the assumption that their reasoning remains stable under interruptions or changing context. The authors evaluate LRMs on math and programming benchmarks requiring long-form reasoning under two realistic stressors: interruption (where outputs are truncated mid-reasoning) and dynamic context (where reasoning must adapt to in-flight updates). Results show that even top-performing LRMs suffer significant performance drops—up to 60%—when updates are introduced late in reasoning, revealing failure modes such as reasoning leakage, panic, and self-doubt. Static evaluations consistently overestimate robustness, highlighting the need for dynamic robustness testing in real-world applications.
本研究探讨大型推理模型（LRMs）在动态环境中的鲁棒性，挑战模型在静态世界中运行的假设。作者在两种现实场景下评估模型表现：中断（输出中途被截断）和动态上下文（推理过程中需适应更新）。在需要长篇推理的数学与编程基准测试中，静态评估严重高估模型可靠性；即使顶尖模型在中断或后期上下文更新时也可能性能骤降60%。研究还发现新型失效模式，包括推理泄漏（将推理过程嵌入最终答案）、恐慌（因时间压力完全放弃推理）和自我怀疑（整合新信息时性能下降）。
GloResNet: A lightweight 3D CNN with global topological features for preterm brain injury prediction
Authors: Boyu Yuan, Jiamiao Lu, Weichuan Zhang, Benqing Wu, Tuo Wang, Changshan Wang, Changming Sun, Liang Guo
First: 2026-06-01T17:04:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:04:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This study introduces an automated deep learning framework for predicting brain injury (BI) in preterm infants from T2-weighted MRI (dHCP dataset). We propose GloResNet, a lightweight 3D CNN based on ResNet-10, pretrained on MedicalNet to address data scarcity. A global manifold mapping strategy first resamples each 3D volume to 128x128x128 and then applies subject-wise z-score intensity normalization, thereby preserving global topology while standardizing appearance. Training integrates mixup, class weighting, and test-time augmentation for robustness. In 5-fold cross-validation, GloResNet achieved 75.18% average accuracy (peak 81.82%), with specificity 0.81 and sensitivity 0.76. Results demonstrate that a topology-aware lightweight CNN has the capability to effectively predict neonatal BI, offering a non-invasive screening tool. The source code of this paper can be obtained from the GitHub repository: https://github.com/ICL-SUST/GloResNet-Preterm-Brain
中文标题/摘要
标题：GloResNet：一种基于全局拓扑特征的轻量级3D CNN，用于早产儿脑损伤预测
本研究提出了一种自动化深度学习框架，利用T2加权MRI（dHCP数据集）预测早产儿脑损伤（BI）。我们提出了GloResNet，这是一种基于ResNet-10的轻量级3D CNN，预训练于MedicalNet以应对数据稀缺问题。采用全局流形映射策略，首先将每个3D体积重采样为128x128x128，然后进行按受试者z-score强度标准化，从而在标准化外观的同时保留全局拓扑结构。训练中结合了mixup、类别加权和测试时增强以提升鲁棒性。在5折交叉验证中，GloResNet平均准确率为75.18%（峰值81.82%），特异性0.81，敏感性0.76。结果表明，具备拓扑感知能力的轻量级CNN能够有效预测新生儿脑损伤，提供一种无创筛查工具。本文源代码可从GitHub仓库获取：https://github.com/ICL-SUST/GloResNet-Preterm-Brain
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate, non-invasive prediction of brain injury in preterm infants using limited MRI data, this study proposes GloResNet, a lightweight 3D CNN derived from ResNet-10 and pretrained on MedicalNet. The method employs a global manifold mapping strategy to standardize 3D volumes to 128x128x128 with subject-wise z-score normalization, preserving global topology while enhancing consistency. Training incorporates mixup, class weighting, and test-time augmentation to improve robustness. In 5-fold cross-validation, GloResNet achieved an average accuracy of 75.18% (peak 81.82%), with specificity of 0.81 and sensitivity of 0.76, demonstrating its effectiveness as a screening tool for neonatal brain injury.
为应对早产儿脑损伤预测中数据稀缺且需非侵入性筛查的需求，本研究提出GloResNet，一种基于ResNet-10的轻量级3D卷积神经网络，并在MedicalNet上预训练。该方法采用全局流形映射策略，将3D体积统一重采样至128x128x128，并进行按受试者标准化的z-score强度归一化，以保留拓扑结构并增强一致性。训练中结合mixup、类别加权和测试时增强以提升鲁棒性。5折交叉验证结果显示，GloResNet平均准确率达75.18%（峰值81.82%），特异性0.81，敏感性0.76，表明其作为早产儿脑损伤筛查工具的有效性。
Bridging the Last Mile of Time Series Forecasting with LLM Agents
Authors: Yuhua Liao, Zetian Wang, Qiangqiang Nie, Zhenhua Zhang
First: 2026-06-01T17:03:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:03:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time series forecasting has advanced rapidly, especially with the emergence of foundation models that show strong zero-shot performance on numerical extrapolation. However, in real-world forecasting settings, a statistically plausible baseline is rarely the final forecast used in practice. Before a forecast becomes decision-ready, it often needs to be revised using weakly structured business context such as holiday effects, campaign plans, external events, historical analogs, and expert feedback. This practical stage remains underexplored in the forecasting literature. In this paper, we formulate this stage as the \textbf{last-mile forecasting} problem and present an LLM-agent framework that sits on top of a forecasting backbone. Our system maintains a unified forecast workspace, invokes tools to retrieve contextual evidence, and converts reasoning trajectories into explicit forecast revision actions under structural safety constraints. It also supports long-horizon forecasting through map-reduce-style decomposition and post-hoc reflection through a memory bank. The resulting system is designed to be controllable and auditable. Through real-world case studies, we show how LLM agents can bridge the gap between statistical prediction and business-ready forecasting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用大语言模型代理弥合时间序列预测的最后一步
时间序列预测已迅速发展，尤其是随着基础模型的出现，这些模型在数值外推任务上展现出强大的零样本性能。然而，在现实世界预测场景中，统计上合理的基线很少是实际应用中的最终预测。在预测成为决策可用前，通常需要结合弱结构化的业务上下文（如节假日效应、营销计划、外部事件、历史类比和专家反馈）进行修订。这一实用阶段在预测文献中尚未得到充分探索。本文将此阶段定义为\textbf{最后一步预测}问题，并提出一种基于预测骨干模型之上的大语言模型代理框架。该系统维护统一的预测工作区，调用工具检索上下文证据，并在结构化安全约束下将推理轨迹转化为明确的预测修订操作。同时，通过类似Map-Reduce的分解方式支持长周期预测，并通过记忆库实现事后反思。该系统设计为可控制且可审计。通过实际案例研究，我们展示了大语言模型代理如何弥合统计预测与业务可用预测之间的鸿沟。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between statistical forecasting models and practical business decision-making, this paper addresses the 'last-mile' stage where forecasts require contextual revision using weakly structured business knowledge. The authors propose an LLM-agent framework that integrates with a forecasting backbone, maintaining a unified workspace to retrieve contextual evidence and generate revised forecasts under safety constraints. The system supports long-horizon predictions via map-reduce decomposition and uses a memory bank for post-hoc reflection. Experimental results demonstrate that the LLM agent effectively bridges statistical outputs with actionable, business-ready forecasts in real-world scenarios.
为弥合统计预测模型与实际业务决策之间的鸿沟，本文提出一种基于大语言模型代理的框架，解决预测的“最后一公里”问题——即需结合业务上下文（如节假日、营销活动、外部事件等）对预测结果进行修订。该方法在预测骨干模型之上集成LLM代理，维护统一工作空间，调用工具获取上下文证据，并将推理过程转化为受安全约束的结构化修订动作。同时支持通过分而治之与记忆反思实现长周期预测。实验表明，该系统能有效将统计预测转化为可执行的业务预测，融入专家反馈与历史类比等弱结构化信息。
Monitoring Agentic Systems Before They're Reliable
Authors: Marisa Ferrara Boston, Glen Hanson, Effi Georgala, JD Hudgens, Heather Frase
First: 2026-06-01T17:01:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:01:53+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 2 figures, 3 tables. Accepted to the Workshop on Agentic Software Engineering (AgenticSE), co-located with ACM CAIS 2026 (non-archival)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic systems entering production typically operate as partially integrated assemblies where structural defects, not task-level errors, dominate the failure landscape. At this maturity level, task-level error detection may be infeasible: structural failure modes mask the signal that task-level monitors are designed to detect.We present a monitoring and triage methodology that decomposes agentic system evaluation into three dimensions (quality, suitability, efficiency) at three monitoring scopes (within-run, cross-run, structural), using variance as a characterization signal. Findings are routed through severity classification adapted from FMEA, concentrating human attention on the subset that warrants investigation. We evaluate on a synthetic testbed of 220 runs across 120 document bundles with controlled error injection.Three results emerge. Monitor scope determines failure type: within-run monitors surface deterministic stage defects (CV = 0.02), cross-run monitors surface stochastic integration consequences (CV = 1.25, 24% at L2), and a structural monitor identifies an integration gap with perfect consistency (CV = 0.00). Injected task-level errors are indistinguishable from clean baselines, confirming structural defects mask task-level signal. Deterministic triage routes 97% of findings to automated tracking, leaving the 2% reflecting variable behavior for human investigation.We propose, on Stage 1 evidence, a maturity-staging model in which monitoring transitions from structural characterization to error detection to reliability tracking as integration defects resolve. The taxonomy, CV-based scope characterization, and severity model transfer architecturally to document-driven, multi-stage agentic workflows in regulated industries; specific calibrations are domain-specific. Deploy monitoring early: the first thing it finds is the most important thing to fix.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在系统可靠前监控代理系统
进入生产环境的代理系统通常作为部分集成的组件运行，其失效主要源于结构缺陷而非任务级错误。在此成熟度阶段，任务级错误检测可能不可行：结构失效模式掩盖了任务级监控器旨在检测的信号。我们提出一种监控与分诊方法，将代理系统评估分解为三个维度（质量、适用性、效率）和三个监控范围（运行内、跨运行、结构），使用方差作为表征信号。结果通过源自FMEA的严重性分类处理，集中人类注意力于需调查的子集。我们在包含120个文档包、220次运行的合成测试平台上评估，其中注入可控错误。三个结果显现：监控范围决定失效类型：运行内监控发现确定性阶段缺陷（CV=0.02），跨运行监控发现随机集成后果（CV=1.25，L2层24%），结构监控发现一致性完美的集成缺口（CV=0.00）。注入的任务级错误与干净基线无法区分，证实结构缺陷掩盖了任务级信号。确定性分诊将97%的发现导向自动化跟踪，仅2%反映可变行为需人工调查。基于第一阶段证据，我们提出一个成熟度阶段模型：随着集成缺陷解决，监控从结构表征过渡到错误检测再到可靠性跟踪。该分类体系、基于CV的范围表征及严重性模型可架构迁移至受监管行业中的文档驱动、多阶段代理工作流；具体校准需领域特定。尽早部署监控：它首先发现的问题，正是最需修复的关键问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that agentic systems in early production phases fail due to structural defects rather than task-level errors, the paper introduces a triage methodology that evaluates system quality, suitability, and efficiency across three monitoring scopes: within-run, cross-run, and structural, using variance as a signal. Experiments on a synthetic testbed with 220 runs and 120 document bundles revealed that within-run monitors detect deterministic stage defects (CV=0.02), cross-run monitors uncover stochastic integration issues (CV=1.25, 24% at L2), and structural monitors identify integration gaps with perfect consistency (CV=0.00). Crucially, injected task-level errors were indistinguishable from clean baselines, confirming structural failures mask task-level signals. The severity-based triage system routed 97% of findings to automated tracking, reserving only 2% for human review. The authors propose a maturity-staging model where monitoring evolves from structural characterization to error detection as integration matures, with the approach adaptable to regulated industries.
鉴于早期代理系统的主要故障源于结构缺陷而非任务级错误，本文提出一种三维度（质量、适用性、效率）三层级（运行内、跨运行、结构）的监控与分诊方法，以方差作为表征信号。在包含220次运行和120个文档包的合成测试平台上，实验发现：运行内监控可检测确定性阶段缺陷（CV=0.02），跨运行监控揭示随机性集成后果（CV=1.25，L2层24%），结构监控则发现一致性完美的集成缺口（CV=0.00）。注入的任务级错误与干净基线无异，证实结构缺陷掩盖了任务级信号。基于FMEA的严重性分类将97%的发现导向自动化跟踪，仅2%需人工介入。作者提出一个成熟度阶段模型，监控策略随集成缺陷解决从结构表征过渡到错误检测，该方法可迁移至监管行业中的文档驱动多阶段代理工作流，但需领域特定校准。
Not What, But How: A Communicative Audit of LLM Response Framing
Authors: Siddhesh Milind Pawar, Sarah Masud, Haneul Yoo, Alice Oh, Isabelle Augenstein
First: 2026-06-01T17:01:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:01:31+00:00
Comments: 34 main pages, 19 Figures, 4 Tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are being increasingly used to answer subjective, information-seeking questions, where users are sensitive to how responses are communicated, not just whether the answers are correct. Existing LLM evaluations for subjective cultural queries largely focus on factual correctness, ignoring how the response is framed. To this end, we introduce FRANZ, an automated FRAmework for respoNse characteriZation to conduct communicative audit of LLM responses along four dimensions: cultural positioning, use of generalizing language, anthropomorphic cues, and adherence to conversational maxims. To enable this evaluation, we contribute SQUARE - a corpus of 376k subjective questions sourced from 57 subreddits, and mapped to 7 countries and 19 question categories. We demonstrate FRANZ's applicability by scoring responses from three open-weight LLMs. We observe that LLMs show statistically significant differences in the frequency with which they employ each response characteristic. Unlike single-dimensional audits, FRANZ reveals that insider positioning and anthropomorphism are positively coupled, with the degree of coupling varying by country, providing a diagnostic lens for identifying framing divergences.
中文标题/摘要
标题：不是内容，而是方式：大型语言模型响应框架的沟通审计
大型语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地用于回答主观性、信息寻求类问题，用户对响应的沟通方式敏感，而不仅限于答案是否正确。现有针对主观文化查询的LLM评估主要关注事实准确性，忽视了响应的框架方式。为此，我们提出了FRANZ——一个用于自动分析响应特征的框架，以四个维度对LLM响应进行沟通审计：文化定位、泛化语言使用、拟人化线索及对会话准则的遵守。为支持该评估，我们贡献了SQUARE数据集——包含57个Reddit子版块的37.6万个主观问题，并映射至7个国家和19个问题类别。我们通过为三个开源权重LLM评分，展示了FRANZ的适用性。我们观察到，不同LLM在每种响应特征的使用频率上存在统计显著差异。与单维度审计不同，FRANZ揭示了“内部人定位”与“拟人化”呈正相关，且相关程度因国家而异，为识别框架差异提供了诊断视角。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate how LLMs communicate subjective cultural responses beyond factual accuracy, the paper introduces FRANZ, an automated framework that audits LLM responses across four framing dimensions: cultural positioning, generalizing language, anthropomorphic cues, and adherence to conversational maxims. To support this, the authors construct SQUARE, a 376k-question corpus drawn from 57 subreddits and mapped to 7 countries and 19 categories. Experimental results show statistically significant differences in how three open-weight LLMs deploy each framing characteristic, revealing that insider positioning and anthropomorphism are positively correlated, with this coupling varying by country, offering a diagnostic tool for identifying cross-cultural framing patterns.
为评估大语言模型在主观文化问题中沟通方式的差异，研究者提出FRANZ框架，从文化定位、泛化语言、拟人化提示和会话准则四个维度自动审计模型响应。为此构建了SQUARE语料库，包含来自57个Reddit子版块的37.6万条主观问题，并按7个国家和19类问题分类。实验表明，三类开源大模型在各框架特征的使用频率上存在显著统计差异，且“内圈定位”与“拟人化”存在正向耦合关系，且该耦合程度因国家而异，可作为识别框架偏倚的诊断工具。
MORPHOS: Autoregressive 4D Generation with Temporal Structured Latents
Authors: Minkyung Kwon, Jinhyeok Choi, Youngjin Shin, Jaeyeong Kim, JongMin Lee, Seungryong Kim
First: 2026-06-01T17:01:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:01:21+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://cvlab-kaist.github.io/MORPHOS/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present MORPHOS, a novel autoregressive framework that generates dynamic 3D assets from videos across diverse representations, including meshes, 3D Gaussians, and radiance fields. Existing methods are typically limited to a single representation, struggle to model topological changes, or fail to maintain temporal consistency over long videos. To address these limitations, we introduce the Temporal Structured Latents (T-SLAT), a unified 4D representation that jointly encodes geometry and appearance along the temporal dimension. Leveraging T-SLAT, MORPHOS autoregressively generates dynamic 3D assets via causal attention, conditioning each frame on its preceding history to ensure temporal consistency while handling evolving topologies. We also propose a temporal-structural augmentation to mitigate error accumulation in autoregressive generation. MORPHOS achieves state-of-the-art performance in appearance and competitive results in geometry across multiple benchmarks, demonstrating superior generalization across various representations and robustness in long-horizon generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MORPHOS：基于时序结构化潜在变量的自回归4D生成
我们提出了MORPHOS，一种新颖的自回归框架，可从视频中生成动态3D资产，支持多种表示形式，包括网格、3D高斯和辐射场。现有方法通常局限于单一表示，难以建模拓扑变化，或无法在长视频中保持时序一致性。为解决这些问题，我们引入了时序结构化潜在变量（T-SLAT），这是一种统一的4D表示，沿时间维度联合编码几何与外观。借助T-SLAT，MORPHOS通过因果注意力自回归生成动态3D资产，每帧依据其历史帧进行条件生成，确保时序一致性并处理演变的拓扑结构。我们还提出了一种时序-结构增强方法，以缓解自回归生成中的误差累积。MORPHOS在多个基准测试中实现了外观方面的最先进性能，并在几何方面取得竞争力结果，展示了其在多种表示形式上的优越泛化能力及长时程生成的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing methods that either support only one 3D representation or fail to handle topological changes and long-term temporal consistency, MORPHOS introduces Temporal Structured Latents (T-SLAT), a unified 4D representation encoding geometry and appearance across time. It generates dynamic 3D assets autoregressively using causal attention, conditioning each frame on prior frames to ensure temporal coherence while accommodating evolving topologies. A temporal-structural augmentation is also proposed to reduce error accumulation. Experimental results show MORPHOS achieves state-of-the-art appearance quality and competitive geometry performance across multiple benchmarks, with strong generalization across representations and robustness in long-video generation.
为解决现有方法在多表示动态3D资产生成中缺乏统一性、拓扑变化建模能力差及长时序一致性不足的问题，MORPHOS提出时序结构化潜变量（T-SLAT），联合编码时序几何与外观信息。模型通过因果注意力机制，逐帧自回归生成，以历史帧为条件确保时序一致性并处理拓扑变化，同时引入时序结构化增强以缓解生成误差累积。实验表明，MORPHOS在外观质量上达到最先进水平，在几何重建上表现优异，具备跨表示泛化能力和长视频生成鲁棒性。
Expressivity of congruence-based architectures for DNNs on positive-definite matrices
Authors: Antonin Oswald, Estelle Massart
First: 2026-06-01T17:01:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T17:01:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted for Eusipco 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work studies neural architectures for classifying symmetric positive-definite matrices, focusing on congruence-like layers, in which the input matrix is multiplied on the left and right by a (possibly rectangular) weight matrix $W$ and its transpose. Such layers lie at the core of the celebrated SPDNet and have also been employed independently for dimensionality reduction on positive-definite data. We show that the (semi)-orthogonality constraint commonly imposed on $W$ limits the expressivity of these layers: for certain activation functions, the resulting architecture collapses to a one-hidden-layer equivalent. This lack of expressivity follows from a loss of spectral diversity in congruence-like layers for semi-orthogonal $W$ and is a direct consequence of Poincaré's separation theorem. We then examine the choice of the final classifier, comparing several Riemannian classifiers and discussing their compatibility with the feature maps produced by congruence-like layers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于合同变换架构的深度神经网络在正定矩阵上的表达能力
本研究探讨了用于分类对称正定矩阵的神经架构，重点研究了合同类层，即输入矩阵在左右两侧分别乘以（可能为矩形的）权重矩阵 $W$ 及其转置。此类层是著名的SPDNet的核心，也已被独立用于正定数据的降维。我们表明，通常施加在 $W$ 上的（半）正交约束限制了这些层的表达能力：对于某些激活函数，所得架构退化为等效的一层隐藏层结构。这种表达能力的缺失源于半正交 $W$ 下合同类层的谱多样性丧失，这是庞加莱分离定理的直接结果。随后，我们考察了最终分类器的选择，比较了多种黎曼分类器，并讨论了它们与合同类层生成的特征图的兼容性。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates the expressivity of neural architectures designed to classify symmetric positive-definite matrices, particularly those using congruence-like layers that multiply input matrices by weight matrices and their transposes. The authors demonstrate that imposing semi-orthogonality on these weights severely restricts the network’s capacity, effectively reducing it to a one-hidden-layer model for certain activation functions, due to spectral collapse as explained by Poincaré’s separation theorem. They further evaluate various Riemannian classifiers to determine their compatibility with the feature representations generated by such layers, finding that the choice of classifier significantly impacts performance.
该研究探讨了用于分类对称正定矩阵的神经网络架构的表达能力，特别是采用类似共轭层的结构，即输入矩阵左乘右乘权重矩阵及其转置。作者证明，对权重施加半正交约束会严重限制网络表达能力，导致其在某些激活函数下退化为单隐藏层模型，这是由于谱多样性丧失所致，符合庞加莱分离定理。此外，研究还评估了多种黎曼流形分类器，发现其与共轭层生成的特征映射的兼容性对性能有显著影响。
RASER: Recoverability-Aware Selective Escalation Router for Multi-Hop Question Answering
Authors: Yuyang Li, Zihe Yan, Tobias Käfer
First: 2026-06-01T16:59:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:59:36+00:00
Comments: Under Review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-hop question-answering systems often use expensive retrieval on every question. They may decompose the question, run several retrieval rounds, or search through bridge entities before answering. All of these strategies rely on repeated LLM calls to rewrite or decompose the question, which increases extra token cost, and it is not fitting when the LLM budget is tight. However, our analysis shows that lots of multi-hop questions are already answered correctly by a single one-shot RAG, so running an extra retrieval on every question wastes the budget. We introduce RASER (Recoverability-Aware Selective Escalation Router), a family of cheap routers built on one-shot RAG and six features from it. RASER-2 decides whether to stop or escalate to the extra-retrieval action PRUNE. RASER-3 chooses among one-shot RAG, PRUNE, and iterative retrieval IRCoT, using the same features but adding an explicit cost-accuracy trade-off. Neither router makes an extra LLM call to decide. Across six LLMs and three multi-hop QA benchmarks, both routers stay competitive with the other state-of-the-art (SOTA) baselines in F1 while spending only 41-49% of always-prune's tokens and also less than the iterative and decomposition retrieval baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RASER：面向可恢复性的多跳问答选择性升级路由器
多跳问答系统通常在每个问题上都使用昂贵的检索。它们可能将问题分解、运行多轮检索，或通过桥接实体搜索后再作答。所有这些策略都依赖重复调用大语言模型（LLM）来重写或分解问题，从而增加额外的token成本，当LLM预算紧张时并不合适。然而，我们的分析表明，许多多跳问题已能由单次一次性检索（RAG）正确回答，因此在每个问题上都运行额外检索会浪费预算。我们引入RASER（Recoverability-Aware Selective Escalation Router），一种基于一次性RAG及其六个特征构建的低成本路由器家族。RASER-2决定是否停止或升级至额外检索动作PRUNE；RASER-3在一次性RAG、PRUNE和迭代检索IRCoT之间进行选择，使用相同特征但加入显式的成本-准确率权衡。两个路由器均无需额外调用LLM进行决策。在六种LLM和三个多跳问答基准测试中，两个路由器在F1分数上与现有最先进（SOTA）基线保持竞争力，同时仅消耗始终剪枝方案的41%-49%的token，且少于迭代和分解检索基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high token cost of repeated LLM calls in multi-hop QA systems, the authors propose RASER, a family of lightweight routers that decide whether to use one-shot RAG, escalate to PRUNE, or use iterative retrieval based on six features from the initial RAG response. RASER-2 and RASER-3 avoid extra LLM calls during routing, instead relying on these features to make cost-efficient decisions. Experimental results across six LLMs and three benchmarks show that both routers achieve competitive F1 scores while consuming only 41–49% of the tokens used by always-prune strategies and less than iterative or decomposition baselines.
为降低多跳问答系统中重复调用大语言模型带来的高昂token成本，本文提出RASER路由器，基于单次RAG响应的六个特征，智能判断是否采用单次RAG、剪枝检索或升级至迭代检索。RASER-2与RASER-3均无需额外调用LLM进行决策，仅依赖轻量特征。实验表明，二者在六种LLM和三个基准数据集上保持与SOTA方法相当的F1分数，同时token消耗仅为始终剪枝策略的41–49%，且低于迭代和分解检索基线。
CVEvolve: Autonomous Algorithm Discovery for Unstructured Scientific Data Processing
Authors: Ming Du, Xiangyu Yin, Yanqi Luo, Dishant Beniwal, Songyuan Tang, Hemant Sharma, Mathew J. Cherukara
First: 2026-05-12T00:24:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:59:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientific data processing often requires task-specific algorithms or AI models, creating a barrier for domain scientists who need to analyze their data but may not have extensive computing or image-processing expertise. This barrier is especially pronounced when data are noisy, have a high dynamic range, are sparsely labeled, or are only loosely specified. We introduce CVEvolve, an autonomous agentic harness with a zero-code interface for scientific data-processing algorithm discovery. CVEvolve combines a multi-round search strategy with tools for code execution, evaluation implementation, history management, holdout testing, and optional inspection of scientific data and visual outputs. The search alternates between discovery and improvement actions, and uses lineage-aware stochastic candidate sampling to balance exploration and exploitation. We demonstrate CVEvolve on X-ray fluorescence microscopy image registration, Bragg peak detection, high-energy diffraction microscopy image segmentation, and hybrid analytical-learning-based affine registration. Across these tasks, CVEvolve discovers algorithms that improve over baseline methods, while holdout test tracking helps identify candidates that generalize better than later over-optimized alternatives. These results show that zero-code, autonomous LLM-powered algorithm development can help domain scientists turn unstructured scientific image data into practical algorithms and downstream scientific discoveries.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CVEvolve：面向非结构化科学数据处理的自主算法发现
科学数据处理通常需要特定任务的算法或AI模型，这为需要分析数据但缺乏计算或图像处理专业知识的领域科学家构成了障碍。当数据存在噪声、动态范围高、标注稀疏或仅粗略指定时，这一障碍尤为突出。我们引入CVEvolve，这是一种面向科学数据处理算法发现的自主代理系统，具备零代码界面。CVEvolve结合多轮搜索策略，集成代码执行、评估实现、历史管理、保留测试及可选的科学数据与可视化输出检查工具。搜索在发现与改进动作间交替进行，并采用具有谱系感知的随机候选采样以平衡探索与利用。我们在X射线荧光显微成像配准、布拉格峰检测、高能衍射显微成像分割及混合分析-学习型仿射配准等任务中验证了CVEvolve，其发现的算法在多个任务中优于基线方法，而保留测试追踪有助于识别泛化能力优于后期过度优化替代方案的候选算法。结果表明，零代码、自主驱动的LLM算法开发可帮助领域科学家将非结构化科学图像数据转化为实用算法及下游科学发现。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for domain scientists to process complex, unstructured scientific data without deep programming expertise, CVEvolve introduces an autonomous agent system with a zero-code interface that discovers and refines algorithms through iterative search. It combines multi-round exploration with tools for code execution, evaluation, history tracking, and holdout testing, using lineage-aware stochastic sampling to balance discovery and optimization. Tested on tasks including X-ray fluorescence image registration and diffraction microscopy segmentation, CVEvolve consistently outperformed baseline methods and identified algorithms that generalize better than later-optimized variants, demonstrating the feasibility of LLM-driven, autonomous algorithm development for scientific data analysis.
为了解决领域科学家在缺乏编程能力的情况下难以处理复杂非结构化科学数据的问题，CVEvolve提出了一种零代码界面的自主代理系统，用于发现和优化算法。该系统采用多轮搜索策略，交替进行探索与改进，结合谱系感知的随机采样以平衡探索与利用，并配备代码执行、评估和保留测试工具。在四个科学成像任务（X射线荧光显微镜配准、布拉格峰检测、高能衍射显微镜分割、混合仿射配准）中，实验表明CVEvolve发现的算法普遍优于基线方法，保留测试有效识别出泛化能力更强的候选方案，避免过拟合。
Towards Multidisciplinary Summarization of Hospital Stays: Efficient Sentence-Level Clinical Provenance Categorization
Authors: Baris Karacan, Vaibhav Bhargava, Barbara Di Eugenio, Natalie Parde, Mary Khetani, Yu-Shan Tseng, Vanessa Barbosa, Julie Vignato, Lindsey Knake, Rajashree Dahal, Emily Spellman, Danielle Hitzel, Janine Petitgout, Kristi Haughey, Amanda Karstens, Brianna Clarahan, Rachel Dawson, Lauren Boyd, Mackenzie Weis, Angie Tipton, Jaewon Bae, Catherine K. Craven, Karen Dunn Lopez, Andrew D. Boyd
First: 2026-06-01T16:57:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:57:51+00:00
Comments: 5 pages. Submitted preprint version of a paper accepted to AIME 2026. This version may differ from the camera-ready manuscript and the final Version of Record. The Version of Record will be available from Springer Nature once published
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Effective "all-team" summarization in high-complexity settings like the Neonatal Intensive Care Unit (NICU) requires aggregating insights from diverse disciplines (physicians, nurses, therapists) spread across hundreds of clinical free-text notes. Simply pooling heterogeneous text often leads to incoherent outputs. Structured summarization therefore first requires accurate categorization of sentence-level provenance across multi-source notes. This pilot study introduces a clinical provenance categorization pipeline using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) of large language models (LLMs). We adapted two Llama-3 models (8B and 70B) to MedSecId, a corpus of 2,002 MIMIC-III (Adult ICU) notes annotated with clinical provenance headers, achieving in-domain Macro F1 scores above 92% for both models. To evaluate cross-domain generalization, we assessed model capacity (8B vs. 70B) and quantization on a gold-standard dataset of 227 sentence-level spans derived from three multi-disciplinary NICU summaries. Experimental results demonstrate a scale-dependent transfer effect: while SFT produced only marginal changes for the 8B model, it substantially improved the 70B model, increasing Macro F1 by 7%. Notably, the quantized fine-tuned 70B model outperformed its full-precision baseline while substantially reducing computational requirements. These findings suggest that sufficient model capacity is critical for preserving semantic flexibility during cross-domain clinical transfer and that efficient quantized adaptation can enable structured provenance modeling for downstream summarization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：迈向多学科住院摘要：高效句级临床来源分类
在高复杂度环境（如新生儿重症监护室NICU）中实现有效的“全团队”摘要，需要整合来自不同学科（医生、护士、治疗师）分布在数百份临床自由文本中的见解。简单合并异质文本往往导致输出不连贯。因此，结构化摘要首先需要对多源笔记中的句级来源进行准确分类。本研究引入了一种临床来源分类流水线，利用大语言模型（LLM）的监督微调（SFT）。我们对两个Llama-3模型（8B和70B）进行了适配，应用于MedSecId语料库（包含2002份标注临床来源标题的MIMIC-III成人ICU笔记），两个模型在领域内均达到超过92%的Macro F1分数。为评估跨领域泛化能力，我们在227个句级片段组成的黄金标准数据集上评估了模型容量（8B vs. 70B）和量化效果，该数据集源自三份多学科NICU摘要。实验结果表明存在规模依赖的迁移效应：SFT对8B模型仅产生微小改进，但显著提升了70B模型，Macro F1提升7%。值得注意的是，量化后的微调70B模型在性能上优于其全精度基线，同时大幅降低计算需求。这些发现表明，足够的模型容量对于跨领域临床迁移中保持语义灵活性至关重要，而高效的量化适配可支持下游摘要的结构化来源建模。
Summary / 总结
To enable effective multidisciplinary summarization in complex clinical settings like the NICU, this study addresses the need to accurately categorize sentence-level provenance across diverse clinical notes. It employs supervised fine-tuning of two Llama-3 models (8B and 70B) on MedSecId, a corpus of 2,002 annotated ICU notes, achieving over 92% Macro F1 scores in-domain. Cross-domain evaluation on 227 NICU-derived sentence spans revealed a scale-dependent transfer effect: while fine-tuning had minimal impact on the 8B model, it significantly boosted the 70B model’s performance by 7% in Macro F1. Furthermore, quantized fine-tuned 70B outperformed its full-precision version while reducing computational demands, indicating that larger models are essential for semantic flexibility in clinical transfer and that quantization enables efficient provenance modeling for downstream summarization.
为解决新生儿重症监护室等复杂临床环境中多学科信息整合困难的问题，本研究提出通过监督微调Llama-3模型（8B和70B）对临床语句来源进行分类。在标注的ICU语料库上训练后，两个模型在域内均达到92%以上的宏F1分数。跨域测试显示，模型规模影响显著：8B模型微调效果有限，而70B模型F1提升7%。量化后的70B模型不仅超越全精度基线，还大幅降低计算需求，表明模型容量与量化技术对临床来源建模至关重要。
SpaceTools: Tool-Augmented Spatial Reasoning via Double Interactive RL
Authors: Siyi Chen, Mikaela Angelina Uy, Chan Hee Song, Faisal Ladhak, Adithyavairavan Murali, Qing Qu, Stan Birchfield, Valts Blukis, Jonathan Tremblay
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2025-12-03T18:50:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:57:23+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate strong qualitative visual understanding, but struggle with metrically precise spatial reasoning required for embodied applications. The agentic paradigm promises that VLMs can use a wide variety of tools that could augment these capabilities, such as depth estimators, segmentation models, and pose estimators. Yet it remains an open challenge how to realize this vision without solely relying on handcrafted prompting strategies or enforcing fixed, predefined tool pipelines that limit VLMs' ability to discover optimal tool-use patterns. Reinforcement Learning could overcome this gap, but has so far been limited to reasoning with a single visual tool due to the large search space in multi-tool reasoning. We introduce Double Interactive Reinforcement Learning (DIRL), a two-phase training framework where VLMs learn to coordinate multiple tools through interactive exploration and feedback. In the teaching phase, we combine demonstrations from a single tool specialist trained via interactive RL with traces from a frontier model using all tools. In the exploration phase, the model further refines multi-tool coordination through continued RL. Our model, SpaceTools, with tool-augmented spatial reasoning ability, achieves state-of-the-art performance on spatial understanding benchmarks (RoboSpatial-Home, BLINK, BOP-ASK) and demonstrates reliable real-world manipulation using a 7-DOF robot as a tool. DIRL provides substantial improvements over the vanilla SFT (+12% on RoboSpatial) and RL (+16% on RoboSpatial) baselines. Project page: https://spacetools.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpaceTools：通过双交互强化学习实现工具增强的空间推理
视觉语言模型（VLMs）在定性视觉理解方面表现优异，但在具身应用所需的空间推理方面难以实现精确的度量级推理。代理范式有望使VLMs利用多种工具（如深度估计器、分割模型和姿态估计器）来增强其能力。然而，如何在不依赖手工提示策略或强制固定预定义工具流程（从而限制VLMs发现最优工具使用模式）的情况下实现这一愿景仍是一个开放挑战。强化学习可弥补这一不足，但此前受限于多工具推理时庞大的搜索空间，仅能处理单一视觉工具。我们提出双交互强化学习（DIRL），一种两阶段训练框架，使VLMs通过交互式探索与反馈学习协调多种工具。在教学阶段，我们结合通过交互式强化学习训练的单一工具专家演示与前沿模型使用所有工具的轨迹；在探索阶段，模型通过持续强化学习进一步优化多工具协调。我们的模型SpaceTools具备工具增强的空间推理能力，在空间理解基准（RoboSpatial-Home、BLINK、BOP-ASK）上达到最先进性能，并在7自由度机器人作为工具的现实世界操作中表现可靠。DIRL相比基础SFT（在RoboSpatial上提升12%）和RL（在RoboSpatial上提升16%）基线有显著改进。项目页面：https://spacetools.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for VLMs to perform metrically precise spatial reasoning in embodied tasks, this work proposes SpaceTools, a tool-augmented system that leverages Double Interactive Reinforcement Learning (DIRL) to enable multi-tool coordination without fixed pipelines. DIRL consists of a teaching phase that combines demonstrations from a single tool specialist trained via interactive RL with traces from a frontier model using all tools, followed by an exploration phase where the model refines multi-tool coordination through continued RL. Experimental results show SpaceTools achieves state-of-the-art performance on spatial benchmarks including RoboSpatial-Home, BLINK, and BOP-ASK, and demonstrates reliable real-world manipulation using a 7-DOF robot, outperforming vanilla SFT (+12% on RoboSpatial) and RL (+16% on RoboSpatial) baselines.
为解决视觉语言模型（VLMs）在具身任务中缺乏精确空间推理能力的问题，作者提出SpaceTools框架，采用双阶段交互式强化学习（DIRL）使VLMs能动态协调多种工具。DIRL包含教学阶段，结合单工具专家通过交互式RL训练的演示与前沿模型使用全部工具的轨迹，以及探索阶段，通过持续RL进一步优化多工具协同。实验表明，SpaceTools在空间理解基准测试中超越了基础SFT（+12% RoboSpatial）和RL（+16% RoboSpatial）基线，达到当前最优性能，并在真实世界中通过7自由度机器人实现可靠操作。
Approximating $f$-Divergences with Rank Statistics
Authors: Viktor Stein, José Manuel de Frutos
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-01-30T10:05:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:56:37+00:00
Comments: 40 pages, 16 figures, 6 tables, accepted at ICML'26. Comments welcome!
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce a rank-statistic approximation of $f$-divergences that avoids explicit density-ratio estimation by working directly with the distribution of ranks. For a resolution parameter $K$, we map the mismatch between two univariate distributions $μ$ and $ν$ to a rank histogram on $\{ 0, \ldots, K\}$ and measure its deviation from uniformity via a discrete $f$-divergence, yielding a rank-statistic divergence estimator. We prove that the resulting estimator of the divergence is monotone in $K$, is always a lower bound of the true $f$-divergence, and we establish quantitative convergence rates for $K\to\infty$ under mild regularity of the quantile-domain density ratio. To handle high-dimensional data, we define the sliced rank-statistic $f$-divergence by averaging the univariate construction over random projections, and we provide convergence results for the sliced limit as well. We also derive finite-sample deviation bounds along with asymptotic normality results for the estimator. Finally, we empirically validate the approach by benchmarking against neural baselines and illustrating its use as a learning objective in generative modeling experiments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用秩统计量近似 f-散度
我们引入了一种秩统计量近似 f-散度的方法，该方法通过直接处理秩的分布来避免显式的密度比估计。对于分辨率参数 K，我们将两个一元分布 μ 和 ν 之间的不匹配映射到 {0, ..., K} 上的秩直方图，并通过离散 f-散度衡量其偏离均匀性的程度，从而得到一种秩统计量散度估计器。我们证明该散度估计器在 K 上单调递增，始终是真实 f-散度的下界，并在分位数域密度比满足温和正则性条件下，建立了 K→∞ 时的量化收敛速率。为处理高维数据，我们通过在随机投影上平均一元构造定义了切片秩统计量 f-散度，并提供了切片极限的收敛结果。此外，我们推导了有限样本偏差界，并给出了估计量的渐近正态性结果。最后，我们通过与神经网络基线对比并将其应用于生成模型实验中的学习目标，实证验证了该方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational difficulty of estimating f-divergences via density ratios, this work proposes a rank-statistic approximation that operates directly on the distribution of ranks between two distributions. For a resolution parameter K, it constructs a rank histogram and measures its deviation from uniformity using a discrete f-divergence, yielding a lower-bounding estimator that is monotone in K and converges at quantifiable rates as K increases under mild density ratio conditions. For high-dimensional data, it extends the method via sliced rank-statistics by averaging over random projections, with established convergence and finite-sample deviation bounds. Empirical results show the estimator competes with neural baselines and effectively serves as a learning objective in generative modeling.
为避免直接估计密度比，该研究提出一种基于秩统计的 f-散度近似方法，直接利用两个一维分布间秩的分布进行建模。对于分辨率参数 K，通过构建秩直方图并用离散 f-散度衡量其偏离均匀分布的程度，得到一个随 K 单调递增且始终低于真实散度的估计量。理论分析给出在密度比满足温和正则性条件下，K 趋于无穷时的收敛速率，并通过随机投影扩展至高维情形，同样提供收敛保证。实验表明该方法在基准测试中可与神经网络方法媲美，且在生成模型中可有效作为学习目标。
LagerNVS: Latent Geometry for Fully Neural Real-time Novel View Synthesis
Authors: Stanislaw Szymanowicz, Minghao Chen, Jianyuan Wang, Christian Rupprecht, Andrea Vedaldi
First: 2026-03-20T17:53:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:56:00+00:00
Comments: IEEE CVF Conference on Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 2026. Project page with code, models and examples: szymanowiczs.github.io/lagernvs
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent work has shown that neural networks can perform 3D tasks such as Novel View Synthesis (NVS) without explicit 3D reconstruction. Even so, we argue that strong 3D inductive biases are still helpful in the design of such networks. We show this point by introducing LagerNVS, an encoder-decoder neural network for NVS that builds on `3D-aware' latent features. The encoder is initialized from a 3D reconstruction network pre-trained using explicit 3D supervision. This is paired with a lightweight decoder, and trained end-to-end with photometric losses. LagerNVS achieves state-of-the-art deterministic feed-forward Novel View Synthesis (including 31.4 PSNR on Re10k), with and without known cameras, renders in real time, generalizes to in-the-wild data, and can be paired with a diffusion decoder for generative extrapolation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LagerNVS：用于全神经实时新视角合成的潜在几何结构
近期研究表明，神经网络可在无需显式3D重建的情况下执行3D任务，如新视角合成（NVS）。尽管如此，我们认为在设计此类网络时，强3D归纳偏置仍有帮助。我们通过引入LagerNVS加以证明，这是一种基于“3D感知”潜在特征的编码器-解码器神经网络。编码器从使用显式3D监督预训练的3D重建网络初始化，与轻量级解码器搭配，并通过光度损失端到端训练。LagerNVS在确定性前馈新视角合成中达到最先进水平（如在Re10k数据集上PSNR达31.4），支持已知与未知相机，实时渲染，可泛化至野外数据，并可与扩散解码器结合实现生成式外推。

Motivated by the need for more effective 3D inductive biases in neural NVS, the authors propose LagerNVS, a neural network architecture that leverages 3D-aware latent features. The model uses an encoder pretrained on explicit 3D supervision and a lightweight decoder, trained end-to-end with photometric losses. Experimental results show LagerNVS achieves state-of-the-art deterministic real-time NVS performance, including 31.4 PSNR on Re10k, works with or without known camera parameters, generalizes to real-world data, and supports generative extrapolation via a diffusion decoder.
为提升神经网络在新型视图合成中的3D先验能力，作者提出LagerNVS架构，利用3D感知的潜在特征。编码器基于具有显式3D监督预训练的3D重建网络初始化，轻量解码器与之配对，通过光度损失端到端训练。实验表明，LagerNVS在Re10k数据集上达到31.4 PSNR的最先进确定性实时合成效果，支持已知与未知相机参数，能泛化至真实场景数据，并可结合扩散解码器实现生成式外推。
Intercepting the Future: Latent-Space Predictive World Model for Dynamic VLA Manipulation
Authors: Shahram Najam Syed, Arthur Jakobsson, Haoran Hao, Jeffrey Ichnowski
First: 2026-06-01T16:55:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:55:38+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 7 figures, 16 tables, Su
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models generalize across static manipulation but fail when objects move during task execution. They map the current observation to an action and assume the scene is stationary between observation and execution, so at any non-trivial object speed the resulting latency exceeds the time available to grasp. We close this gap with AHEAD (Anticipatory Horizon Extrapolation with Adaptive Dynamics), a predict-then-act wrapper that augments a frozen VLA with a motion-aware latent world model. A small world model trained on manipulation video forecasts future patch tokens in the VLA's feature space, conditioned on per-token velocity and acceleration from optical flow. A language-and-motion saliency mask concentrates prediction on task-relevant patches, and the model rolls forward for an adaptive horizon, halting when prediction uncertainty crosses a threshold. The frozen action decoder then receives the predicted future tokens in place of the current ones. AHEAD adds 4.9M parameters to a frozen 7B OpenVLA and reaches 79 to 97% success across 20 dynamic simulation scenarios where the strongest baseline reaches 31 to 58%. On a physical UFactory xArm 7, AHEAD succeeds on 29/30 to 30/30 on three conveyor and rolling-ball tasks, 23/30 on paddle interception, and 19/30 on projectile catching where every baseline scores 0/30.
中文标题/摘要
标题：截取未来：用于动态视觉-语言-动作操作的潜在空间预测世界模型
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在静态操作中具有泛化能力，但在任务执行过程中物体移动时表现不佳。它们将当前观测映射为动作，并假设观测与执行之间场景静止，因此在任何非零物体速度下，动作延迟将超过抓取所需时间。我们通过AHEAD（自适应动态的前瞻外推）解决此问题，这是一种预测后行动的包装器，它在冻结的VLA基础上增加了一个感知运动的潜在世界模型。该小型世界模型基于操作视频训练，能在VLA特征空间中预测未来补丁标记，条件是基于光流的每个标记的速度和加速度。语言与运动显著性掩码将预测集中于任务相关补丁，模型会自适应向前滚动，当预测不确定性超过阈值时停止。冻结的动作解码器随后接收预测的未来标记而非当前标记。AHEAD在冻结的7B OpenVLA基础上增加了490万参数，在20个动态仿真场景中成功率从79%至97%，而最强基线仅达31%至58%。在物理UFactory xArm 7上，AHEAD在三个传送带和滚球任务中成功率达29/30至30/30，在桨板拦截任务中为23/30，在弹丸捕捉任务中为19/30（所有基线均为0/30）。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitation of Vision-Language-Action models in handling dynamic object motion during manipulation tasks, the paper introduces AHEAD, a predictive wrapper that augments a frozen VLA with a motion-aware latent world model. This model forecasts future visual tokens in the VLA’s feature space using optical flow-derived velocity and acceleration, guided by a language-and-motion saliency mask to focus on task-relevant regions. It dynamically extrapolates for an adaptive horizon until prediction uncertainty exceeds a threshold, then feeds the predicted tokens to the action decoder. Experiments show AHEAD significantly outperforms baselines: achieving 79–97% success in 20 dynamic simulation tasks (vs. 31–58% for the best baseline) and 29/30 to 30/30 success rates on physical robotic tasks including conveyor and rolling-ball scenarios, while achieving 19/30 on projectile catching where all baselines failed.
为解决视觉-语言-动作模型在动态物体运动场景下表现不佳的问题，本文提出AHEAD方法，通过在冻结的VLA模型上叠加一个运动感知的潜在世界模型进行预测。该模型利用光流计算的运动信息，在VLA特征空间中预测未来视觉token，结合语言与运动显著性掩码聚焦任务相关区域，并动态预测直至不确定性超过阈值，再将预测token输入动作解码器。实验表明，AHEAD在20个动态仿真任务中成功率79–97%（优于基线31–58%），在物理机器人任务中如传送带、滚球任务上达29–30/30成功率，而在抛射物捕捉任务中亦达19/30（所有基线均为0/30）
Iteris: Agentic Research Loops for Computational Mathematics
Authors: Leheng Chen, Zihao Liu, Wanyi He, Bin Dong
First: 2026-06-01T16:54:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:54:31+00:00
Comments: 43 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in large language models and agentic AI systems have enabled significant progress in mathematical discovery, from solving competition problems to tackling research-level conjectures. However, open problems in computational mathematics have received comparatively less attention: research in this area often requires not only proofs but also numerical experimentation, adversarial constructions, and algorithm design. In this paper, we introduce an agentic research system, Iteris, designed for open problems in computational mathematics. We apply Iteris to two open problems from a recent Simons Workshop collection (arXiv:2602.05394). In these case studies, Iteris generated numerical evidence, constructions, and proof drafts that led, after expert review and correction, to verified results. The first result is a phase diagram for the asymptotic comparison between conjugate gradient and randomized coordinate descent on power-law spectra; the second is a counterexample showing that QR factorization with column pivoting can fail to select well-conditioned submatrices even under low coherence. These case studies suggest that agentic AI systems can participate meaningfully in research workflows for open problems in computational mathematics, while human validation remains essential.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Iteris：面向计算数学的代理研究循环
大型语言模型和代理型人工智能系统的最新进展，已推动数学发现取得显著进展，从解决竞赛题到攻克研究级猜想。然而，计算数学中的开放性问题却相对较少受到关注：该领域研究不仅需要证明，还需数值实验、对抗性构造和算法设计。本文介绍了一种专为计算数学开放性问题设计的代理研究系统——Iteris。我们将其应用于近期Simons研讨会论文集（arXiv:2602.05394）中的两个开放性问题。在这些案例研究中，Iteris生成了数值证据、构造和证明草稿，经专家审阅修正后，最终获得验证结果。第一个结果是共轭梯度法与随机坐标下降法在幂律谱上的渐近比较相图；第二个结果是表明即使在低相干性条件下，带列置换的QR分解也可能无法选择良态子矩阵的反例。这些案例研究表明，代理型AI系统可有效参与计算数学开放性问题的研究流程，但人类验证仍不可或缺。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate and augment research in computational mathematics—where open problems demand numerical experimentation, adversarial constructions, and algorithm design—the authors developed Iteris, an agentic research system. Iteris autonomously generates numerical evidence, constructions, and proof drafts for open problems, which are then refined through expert review. Applied to two Simons Workshop problems, Iteris produced a phase diagram comparing conjugate gradient and randomized coordinate descent on power-law spectra, and a counterexample demonstrating that QR factorization with column pivoting can fail to select well-conditioned submatrices even under low coherence. 为应对计算数学中需要数值实验、对抗性构造和算法设计的开放性问题，作者提出了Iteris这一自主研究系统，旨在自动产生证据和解决方案草案。在两个来自Simons研讨会的开放问题上，Iteris生成了数值数据、反例和证明草稿，经专家审阅修正后得出验证结果：一是共轭梯度法与随机坐标下降法在幂律谱上的渐近比较相图，二是展示QR分解带列置换在低相干条件下仍可能无法选择良态子矩阵的反例。这些案例表明，自主AI可有效参与计算数学研究流程，但人类验证仍不可或缺。
Ghost Tool Calls: Issue-Time Privacy for Speculative Agent Tools
Authors: Bardia Mohammadi, Lars Klein, Akhil Arora, Laurent Bindschaedler
First: 2026-06-01T16:53:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:53:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tool-augmented language agents speculatively issue likely future tool calls to hide latency, but those calls leak inferred user intent to external services before the agent commits to the branch. Every external observer that received the call retains the disclosure after the agent abandons the branch. Timing is the issue, not authorization: no commit-time cleanup, read-only restriction, or access-control allow-list unsends what an observer already holds. We call these invocations ghost tool calls and propose Speculative Tool Privacy Contracts, a runtime abstraction that treats observation before commitment as a first-class effect, distinct from state mutation. We implement the contracts in a prototype runtime and evaluate twelve policies across three corpora. Speculative dispatch increases what an observer can infer about user intent; post-hoc filters, read-only restrictions, and access-control allow-lists leave that inference intact; only issue-time policies that change or suppress the speculative call's argument or destination projection before dispatch reduce it.
中文标题/摘要
标题：幽灵工具调用：用于推测性代理工具的执行时隐私
增强型语言代理通过推测性地发出可能的未来工具调用来隐藏延迟，但这些调用在代理尚未确认分支前，已向外部服务泄露推断出的用户意图。任何接收到调用的外部观察者，在代理放弃该分支后仍保留该披露信息。问题在于时间点，而非授权：无论是否在提交时清理、仅读限制或访问控制白名单，都无法撤销观察者已持有的信息。我们将这些调用称为幽灵工具调用，并提出推测性工具隐私合约，一种运行时抽象，将提交前的观察视为与状态变更同等重要的第一类效应。我们在原型运行时中实现该合约，并在三个语料库上评估十二种策略。推测性调度会增加观察者对用户意图的推断能力；事后过滤器、仅读限制和访问控制白名单无法减少该推断；唯有在调用时改变或抑制推测性调用的参数或目标投影的策略，才能降低该推断。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the privacy risk that speculative tool calls in language agents leak user intent to external services before commitment, the paper introduces Speculative Tool Privacy Contracts, a runtime abstraction that treats pre-commit observation as a distinct effect from state mutation. The method enforces privacy at issue time by modifying or suppressing speculative tool call arguments or destinations before dispatch. Experimental evaluation across twelve policies and three corpora shows that only issue-time policies effectively reduce inference of user intent by observers, while post-hoc filters, read-only restrictions, and access-control allow-lists fail to mitigate the leakage.
为了解决语言代理在推测性调用工具时提前泄露用户意图的问题，该论文提出“推测性工具隐私合约”，一种在运行时将调用前观察视为独立于状态变更的抽象机制。方法在调用发生前修改或抑制工具调用参数或目标，以减少信息泄露。实验表明，仅在调用时生效的策略能有效降低观察者对用户意图的推断，而事后过滤、只读限制或访问控制白名单无法解决该问题。
RichControl: Structure- and Appearance-Rich Training-Free Spatial Control for Text-to-Image Generation
Authors: Lexi Pang, Liheng Zhang, Hang Ye, Xiaoxuan Ma, Yizhou Wang
First: 2025-07-03T16:56:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:52:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models have shown remarkable success in generating high-quality images from text prompts. Recent efforts extend these models to incorporate conditional images (e.g., canny edge) for fine-grained spatial control. Among them, feature injection methods have emerged as a training-free alternative to traditional fine-tuning-based approaches. However, they often suffer from structural misalignment, condition leakage, and visual artifacts, especially when the condition image diverges significantly from natural RGB distributions. Through an analysis of existing methods, we identify a key limitation: the sampling schedule of condition features, previously unexplored, fails to account for the evolving interplay between structure preservation and domain alignment throughout diffusion steps. Inspired by this observation, we propose a flexible training-free framework that decouples the sampling schedule of condition features from the denoising process, and systematically investigate the spectrum of feature injection schedules to achieve a better balance between structural alignment and appearance quality. We further enhance the sampling process by introducing a restart refinement schedule, and improve the visual quality with an appearance-rich prompting strategy. Together, these designs enable training-free controllable generation that is both structure-rich and appearance-rich. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance under complex and diverse conditions. Owing to its generality, our framework naturally supports compositional conditional generation and generalizes across architectures in a plug-and-play manner, from UNet-based diffusion models to modern DiT backbones such as FLUX.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RichControl：面向文本到图像生成的结构与外观丰富、无需训练的空间控制方法
文本到图像（T2I）扩散模型在从文本提示生成高质量图像方面取得了显著成功。近期研究尝试将这些模型扩展以融入条件图像（如Canny边缘图）以实现精细的空间控制。其中，特征注入方法作为一种无需训练的替代方案，已逐渐取代传统的微调方法。然而，这些方法常面临结构错位、条件泄露和视觉伪影等问题，尤其当条件图像与自然RGB分布差异显著时。通过对现有方法的分析，我们发现一个关键限制：此前未被探索的条件特征采样调度机制，未能充分考虑扩散过程中结构保持与域对齐之间的动态交互。受此启发，我们提出一种灵活的无需训练框架，将条件特征采样调度与去噪过程解耦，并系统性地研究不同特征注入调度策略，以实现结构对齐与外观质量之间的更好平衡。我们进一步通过引入重启细化调度增强采样过程，并采用外观丰富的提示策略提升视觉质量。这些设计共同实现了结构丰富且外观丰富的无需训练可控生成。大量实验表明，我们的方法在复杂多样的条件下达到当前最优性能。得益于其通用性，该框架天然支持组合式条件生成，并能以即插即用方式在不同架构间泛化，从基于UNet的扩散模型到现代DiT主干（如FLUX）均可适用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing training-free feature injection methods in text-to-image generation, which suffer from structural misalignment and visual artifacts due to suboptimal condition feature sampling schedules, the authors propose RichControl: a framework that decouples condition feature sampling from the denoising process and systematically explores injection schedules to balance structural fidelity and visual quality. They further enhance results with a restart refinement schedule and appearance-rich prompting. Experiments show RichControl achieves state-of-the-art performance under diverse conditions, supports compositional generation, and is compatible across architectures including UNet and DiT backbones.
为了解决现有训练免特征注入方法在文本到图像生成中因条件特征采样调度不当导致的结构错位和视觉伪影问题，本文提出RichControl框架，将条件特征采样调度与去噪过程解耦，并系统探索多种注入策略，结合重启细化机制与外观丰富提示策略，显著提升结构与视觉质量。实验表明该方法在复杂条件下优于现有方法，且可无缝适配UNet与DiT等多种架构，支持组合式条件生成。
X-Stream: Exploring MLLMs as Multiplexers for Multi-Stream Understanding
Authors: Peiwen Sun, Xudong Lu, Huadai Liu, Yang Bo, Dongming Wu, Huankang Guan, Minghong Cai, Jinpeng Chen, Xintong Guo, Shuhan Li, Rui Liu, Xiangyu Yue
First: 2026-06-01T16:52:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:52:11+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://peiwensun2000.github.io/xstream/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While video streaming understanding has made significant strides, real-world applications, such as live sports broadcasting, autonomous driving, and multi-screen collaboration, inherently demand continuous, multi-stream interactions. However, existing benchmarks are confined to single-stream paradigms, leaving a critical gap in evaluating online, cross-stream reasoning. To bridge this, we introduce X-Stream, the first benchmark dedicated to multi-stream streaming understanding. Comprising 4,220 rigorously curated QA pairs across 932 videos, X-Stream evaluates 11 subtasks across multi-window, multi-view, and multi-device scenarios. Crucially, our dataset is constructed using a novel dual-verification pipeline that prevents over-reliance on a single stream. Furthermore, we pioneer the conceptualization of multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) as naive multiplexers, systematically evaluating their performance through the lens of Signal Multiplexing Theory. Our extensive online inference experiments reveal a stark reality: state-of-the-art MLLMs struggle significantly with concurrent streams, achieving only about 50% score and exhibiting poor proactive ability. Ultimately, X-Stream exposes the trade-off of current multiplexing schemes, providing both a practical evaluation protocol and empirical guidance for next-generation multi-stream agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：X-Stream：探索多流理解中多模态大语言模型作为多路复用器的应用

Places in the Wild: A Large, High-Resolution RAW Photograph Dataset for Ecologically Valid Vision Research
Authors: Michelle R. Greene
First: 2026-06-01T16:51:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:51:20+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 3 tables, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large image datasets have accelerated progress in cognitive neuroscience and computer vision. However, most datasets are low-resolution, internet-sourced JPEGs with unknown capture conditions and limited spatial context. Places in the Wild is a dataset of 67,574 high-resolution photographs collected in situ across 810 physical locations spanning 260 basic-level scene categories, including indoor, urban, and natural environments. At each location, a 45-megapixel Canon EOS R5 mounted on a panoramic tripod captured 72 images at 5-degree horizontal intervals plus 12 images at varying elevations, yielding dense 360-degree viewpoint sampling. All images were recorded simultaneously as 14-bit RAW (CR3) files and compressed JPEGs, preserving sensor-level detail for analyses of luminance, contrast, color, and other image statistics. The dataset is accompanied by complete EXIF metadata and a suite of image-quality metrics. Places in the Wild supports research on viewpoint-dependent recognition in humans and models, training and evaluation of scene-understanding systems under realistic conditions, characterization of natural scene statistics, and experiments requiring near-full-field visual displays.
中文标题/摘要
标题：野外场景：一个用于生态有效视觉研究的高分辨率RAW照片大型数据集
大型图像数据集已加速认知神经科学和计算机视觉领域的进展。然而，大多数数据集为低分辨率、来自互联网的JPEG图像，拍摄条件未知且空间上下文有限。'野外场景'数据集包含67,574张高分辨率照片，拍摄于810个实际地点，涵盖260个基础场景类别，包括室内、城市和自然环境。在每个地点，一台安装在全景三脚架上的4500万像素佳能EOS R5相机以5度水平间隔拍摄72张图像，并在不同高度拍摄12张图像，实现密集的360度视角采样。所有图像同时以14位RAW（CR3）文件和压缩JPEG格式记录，保留传感器级细节，便于分析亮度、对比度、色彩及其他图像统计特征。该数据集附带完整的EXIF元数据和一系列图像质量指标。'野外场景'支持人类与模型的视角依赖性识别研究、在真实条件下训练和评估场景理解系统、自然场景统计特征的表征，以及需要近全视野视觉显示的实验。

Motivated by the need for ecologically valid visual stimuli that preserve real-world imaging conditions, the authors created Places in the Wild, a large-scale dataset of 67,574 high-resolution RAW photographs captured in situ across 810 locations. Images were taken using a 45-megapixel Canon EOS R5 mounted on a panoramic tripod, capturing 72 horizontal and 12 elevation views per location to enable dense 360-degree sampling.
