The dataset includes complete EXIF metadata and image-quality metrics, enabling research on viewpoint-dependent recognition, scene understanding under realistic conditions, and natural scene statistics.
为推动认知神经科学和计算机视觉研究，本研究构建了Places in the Wild数据集，包含67,574张在真实场景中拍摄的高分辨率RAW照片，覆盖810个物理地点，涉及260类基础场景。每处地点使用4500万像素佳能EOS R5相机在全景三脚架上拍摄72张水平视角和12张高低视角图像，实现密集360度采样。所有图像同时以14位RAW格式和压缩JPEG格式记录，保留传感器级细节，便于分析亮度、对比度和色彩等图像统计特征。数据集附带完整EXIF元数据和图像质量指标，支持视点依赖识别、真实环境下的场景理解及自然场景统计研究。
A Monosemantic Attribution Framework for Stable Interpretability in Clinical Neuroscience Transformer-Based Language Models
Authors: Michail Mamalakis, Tiago Azevedo, Cristian Cosentino, Chiara D'Ercoli, Subati Abulikemu, Zhongtian Sun, Richard Bethlehem, Pietro Lio
First: 2026-01-25T19:03:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:50:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Interpretability remains a key challenge for deploying language models (LM) in clinical settings such as progression diagnosis of Alzheimer disease, where early and trustworthy predictions are essential. Existing attribution methods exhibit high inter-method variability and unstable explanations due to the polysemantic nature of Transformer-Based LM and LLM representations, while mechanistic interpretability approaches lack direct alignment with model inputs and outputs and do not provide explicit importance scores. We introduce a unified interpretability framework that integrates attributional and mechanistic perspectives through monosemantic feature extraction. By constructing a monosemantic embedding space at the level of an transformer-based LM layer and optimizing the framework to explicitly reduce inter-method variability, our approach produces stable input-level importance scores and highlights salient features via a decompressed representation of the layer of interest, advancing the safe and trustworthy application of LMs in cognitive health and neurodegenerative disease.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向临床神经科学Transformer语言模型的单义性归因框架以实现稳定可解释性
可解释性是语言模型在阿尔茨海默病等临床场景中部署的关键挑战，这些场景需要早期且可靠的预测。现有归因方法因Transformer语言模型和大语言模型表示的多义性而表现出高方法间差异和不稳定解释，而机制性可解释方法缺乏与模型输入输出的直接对齐，且不提供明确的重要性评分。我们提出一种统一的可解释性框架，通过单义性特征提取整合归因与机制性视角。通过在Transformer语言模型层构建单义性嵌入空间，并优化框架以显式降低方法间差异，我们的方法生成稳定的输入级重要性评分，并通过目标层的解压缩表示突出显著特征，推动语言模型在认知健康和神经退行性疾病中的安全可信应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for stable and trustworthy interpretability in clinical applications like Alzheimer’s disease diagnosis, this work addresses the instability of existing attribution methods caused by the polysemantic nature of Transformer-based language models. The authors propose a monosemantic attribution framework that constructs a unified embedding space at the transformer layer level and optimizes it to minimize inter-method variability, thereby producing consistent input-level importance scores. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach yields more stable and salient feature attributions compared to baseline methods, enhancing the reliability of model explanations in clinical neuroscience contexts.
为了解决临床应用（如阿尔茨海默病诊断）中语言模型解释性不稳定的问题，本文提出了一种单义性归因框架，通过在Transformer层构建统一的单义嵌入空间并优化以降低方法间差异，从而生成稳定可靠的输入重要性评分。实验表明，该方法相比基线方法能提供更稳定、更清晰的特征归因，有助于在认知健康和神经退行性疾病领域实现更安全可信的模型应用。
Retrieve What's Missing: Coverage-Maximizing Retrieval for Consistent Long Video Generation
Authors: Minseok Joo, Dogyun Park, Taehoon Lee, Kyujin Lee, Hyunwoo J. Kim
First: 2026-06-01T16:49:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:49:58+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 10 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Maintaining long-term geometric consistency remains challenging for long-horizon autoregressive video generation. Memory-augmented generative models address this by retrieving historical frames, but their effectiveness depends on two key design choices: what 3D-geometric evidence should represent past observations, and how memory frames should be selected from this evidence. Existing methods often rely on camera poses or field-of-view overlap, which are lightweight but too coarse to reason about pixel-wise visibility, or use explicit 3D reconstruction, which provides fine-grained evidence but is costly to maintain over long rollouts. We propose Coverage-Maximizing Retrieval-Augmented Generation (COVRAG), a depth-based memory retrieval framework that uses pretrained 3D priors to construct a target-view coverage map as lightweight 3D memory evidence. For frame selection, COVRAG maximizes residual coverage gain, iteratively retrieving frames that explain target-view regions not covered by the current context or previously selected memories. To improve scalability in long-video generation, we introduce sliding-window depth caching for efficient geometry estimation. Experiments on RealEstate10K and DL3DV10K show that COVRAG improves long-horizon geometric consistency while maintaining low latency compared to baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：检索缺失内容：面向一致长视频生成的覆盖最大化检索
长时程自回归视频生成中保持长期几何一致性仍具挑战。记忆增强生成模型通过检索历史帧解决此问题，但其效果取决于两个关键设计选择：何种3D几何证据代表过往观测，以及如何从该证据中选择记忆帧。现有方法常依赖相机位姿或视场重叠，虽轻量但过于粗略，难以处理像素级可见性推理；或采用显式3D重建，虽提供细粒度证据，但长期生成中维护成本高昂。本文提出覆盖最大化检索增强生成（COVRAG），一种基于深度的记忆检索框架，利用预训练3D先验构建目标视角覆盖图作为轻量3D记忆证据。帧选择方面，COVRAG最大化残余覆盖增益，迭代检索能解释当前上下文或先前记忆未覆盖目标视角区域的帧。为提升长视频生成的可扩展性，引入滑动窗口深度缓存以高效估计几何信息。在RealEstate10K和DL3DV10K上的实验表明，COVRAG在保持低延迟的同时，较基线方法显著提升长时程几何一致性。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of maintaining geometric consistency over long video sequences, the paper proposes COVRAG, a depth-based memory retrieval framework that leverages pretrained 3D priors to generate lightweight coverage maps representing past observations. Instead of relying on coarse camera pose or expensive 3D reconstruction, COVRAG selects memory frames by maximizing residual coverage gain—iteratively choosing frames that cover previously unobserved regions in the target view. To scale efficiently for long videos, it employs sliding-window depth caching. Experiments on RealEstate10K and DL3DV10K demonstrate that COVRAG significantly improves geometric consistency while keeping latency low compared to existing methods.
为解决长视频生成中长期几何一致性难题，本文提出COVRAG框架，利用预训练3D先验构建目标视角覆盖图作为轻量级几何记忆证据。其通过迭代最大化残余覆盖增益选择记忆帧，优先覆盖当前上下文或已选记忆未覆盖的区域。为提升长视频生成效率，引入滑动窗口深度缓存机制优化几何估计。在RealEstate10K和DL3DV10K数据集上的实验表明，COVRAG显著提升几何一致性，同时保持低延迟，优于现有方法。
Probabilistic Data-Driven Modelling of Astrophysical Transients: The Neural Process Family for Ultrafast and Class-Agnostic Light Curve Reconstruction with NightLANP
Authors: Siddharth Chaini, Federica B. Bianco, Ashish Mahabal
First: 2026-05-26T18:01:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:48:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Astrophysical observations from Earth are subject to weather, environmental, and scientific constraints that lead to sparse, irregular light curves. On the eve of the Vera C. Rubin Observatory Legacy Survey of Space and Time, its dataset offers unprecedented opportunities for transient science. Yet a key challenge remains its cadence, sparse and irregular across six bands, limiting inference. Interpolation helps mitigate this, with Gaussian Processes the standard, but they struggle with cross-band correlations, require a priori kernel specification, and must be fit to each light curve individually, hence scaling poorly. Here, we introduce the neural process family for light curve reconstruction, combining the probabilistic framework of Gaussian Processes with the scalability of deep learning. By meta-learning on diverse simulated transients, Attentive Neural Processes shift the bulk of computation to training, enabling rapid, amortized inference with a class-agnostic model. Evaluated on realistic Rubin cadences across 15 transient classes, we show that even an unoptimized, out-of-the-box Attentive Neural Process consistently outperforms all benchmarks -- a suite of Gaussian Processes and neural networks -- on every tested metric, spanning regression quality, astrophysical feature recovery, and probabilistic calibration. Our model interpolates all bands simultaneously in microseconds, over four orders of magnitude faster than the next-best neural benchmark and five faster than Gaussian Processes, demonstrating the potential of neural processes for the nightly Rubin alert stream. Attentive Neural Processes avoid the overconfidence of standard neural networks and the underconfidence of Gaussian Processes, delivering sharp, well-calibrated uncertainties. This work establishes the neural process family as a scalable, probabilistic foundation for real-time transient science in the Rubin era.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于概率数据驱动的天文瞬变体建模：用于超快速且无类别依赖的光变曲线重建的神经过程家族与NightLANP
从地球观测的天文现象受天气、环境和科学限制，导致光变曲线稀疏且不规则。随着薇拉·C·鲁宾天文台“时空遗产巡天”即将启动，其数据为瞬变体科学提供了前所未有的机遇。然而，其观测周期在六个波段上稀疏且不规则，限制了推断能力。插值有助于缓解这一问题，高斯过程是标准方法，但其难以处理跨波段相关性、需预先指定核函数，且必须为每个光变曲线单独拟合，因此扩展性差。本文引入神经过程家族用于光变曲线重建，结合高斯过程的概率框架与深度学习的可扩展性。通过在多样化的模拟瞬变体上元学习，注意力神经过程将大部分计算移至训练阶段，实现快速、可并行的无类别依赖模型推断。在15类瞬变体的现实鲁宾观测周期上评估，即使未经优化的开箱即用的注意力神经过程，在所有测试指标上（包括回归质量、天文特征恢复和概率校准）均一致优于所有基准方法（包括高斯过程和神经网络）。我们的模型可在微秒级内同时插值所有波段，速度比次优神经网络基准快四个数量级，比高斯过程快五倍，展示了神经过程在鲁宾每日警报流中的应用潜力。注意力神经过程避免了标准神经网络的过度自信和高斯过程的不足自信，提供锐利且校准良好的不确定性。本工作确立了神经过程家族作为鲁宾时代实时瞬变体科学可扩展的概率基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle sparse and irregular light curves from the Rubin Observatory’s upcoming survey, this work introduces Attentive Neural Processes—a probabilistic, deep learning-based method that combines Gaussian Process modeling with meta-learning to enable fast, class-agnostic light curve interpolation across multiple bands. Trained on simulated transients, the model shifts computational burden to training, allowing rapid inference at microsecond scale. Evaluated across 15 transient classes using realistic Rubin cadences, it consistently outperforms Gaussian Processes and neural networks on regression accuracy, feature recovery, and uncertainty calibration, while being orders of magnitude faster than alternatives and producing well-calibrated, non-overconfident predictions.
为应对鲁宾天文台即将开展的巡天项目中观测数据稀疏、不规则的问题，该研究提出了一种名为“注意力神经过程”的概率模型，结合高斯过程的统计特性与深度学习的可扩展性。该模型通过在模拟瞬变天体数据上进行元学习训练，将大部分计算负担转移至训练阶段，从而实现快速、可复用的推理。在15类瞬变天体、真实鲁宾观测时序下测试，该模型在回归精度、天体特征恢复和不确定性校准等指标上全面优于高斯过程和神经网络基准方法，同时能在微秒级内完成多波段插值——比次优神经模型快四数量级，比高斯过程快五倍，并避免了神经网络的过自信和高斯过程的欠自信，提供精准校准的不确定性估计，为鲁宾时代实时瞬变天体研究奠定基础。
Physics-Informed Residuals for Adaptive Mesh Refinement in Finite-Difference PDE Solvers
Authors: Henry Kasumba, Ronald Katende
First: 2026-06-01T16:47:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:47:21+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 5 tables, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Classical finite-difference solvers remain reliable tools for partial differential equations, but their efficiency depends on where mesh resolution is placed. Uniform refinement can waste degrees of freedom when solution difficulty is localised near sharp gradients, fronts, oscillations, or constraint-sensitive regions. This paper studies a hybrid strategy in which a physics-informed neural network (PINN) is used not as the final solver, but as an off-grid residual probe for adaptive mesh refinement. The PINN residual is sampled over the domain, converted into cellwise indicators, and used to guide refinement before the final approximation is computed by a finite-difference solver. The method is evaluated on three benchmarks. The main full-solver validation uses the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation with a nonuniform finite-difference solve on the adapted meshes. PINN-threshold refinement attains final relative $L^2$ error $0.021067$ with $60$ degrees of freedom, compared with $0.022617$ for uniform refinement with $192$ degrees of freedom. At matched mesh size, PINN-threshold reduces the error by about $67.5\%$. PINN-D"orfler refinement gives similar performance, with error $0.021264$ using $58$ degrees of freedom. A gradient indicator remains slightly more accurate, so the result supports usefulness rather than universal superiority. Manufactured 2D and 3D proxy tests, based on a nonlinear Schr"odinger equation and an incompressible Navier--Stokes system, show that PINN residuals can organise structured refinement and improve over random refinement, although they do not consistently outperform gradient or uniform baselines. The results support PINN-guided AMR as a residual-indicator strategy for transferring physics-informed diagnostic information into finite-difference mesh adaptation while preserving the classical solver as the final approximation engine.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于物理信息的残差用于有限差分PDE求解器中的自适应网格细化
经典的有限差分求解器仍是偏微分方程的可靠工具，但其效率取决于网格分辨率的布置位置。当解的复杂性集中在尖锐梯度、前沿、振荡或约束敏感区域时，均匀细化会浪费自由度。本文研究了一种混合策略：将物理信息神经网络（PINN）用作离网格的残差探测器，指导自适应网格细化，而非作为最终求解器。PINN残差在域内采样，转换为单元级指示器，并在最终有限差分求解前引导细化。 方法在三个基准上评估。主要完整求解器验证使用一维粘性Burgers方程，在自适应网格上进行非均匀有限差分求解。PINN阈值细化在60个自由度下达到最终相对L²误差0.021067，而均匀细化在192个自由度下为0.022617。在相同网格规模下，PINN阈值将误差降低约67.5%。PINN-D"orfler细化表现类似，58个自由度下误差为0.021264。梯度指示器仍略为准确，因此结果支持其有用性而非普适优越性。基于非线性薛定谔方程和不可压缩Navier--Stokes系统的制造2D和3D代理测试显示，PINN残差可组织结构化细化并优于随机细化，但未始终优于梯度或均匀基线。结果支持PINN引导的AMR作为残差指示策略，将物理信息诊断信息传递至有限差分网格自适应，同时保留经典求解器作为最终近似引擎。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of uniform mesh refinement in finite-difference PDE solvers, this work proposes using a physics-informed neural network (PINN) as a residual probe to guide adaptive mesh refinement. The PINN computes residuals across the domain, which are then converted into cellwise indicators to determine where to refine the mesh before the final finite-difference solution is computed. Experiments on 1D viscous Burgers, 2D nonlinear Schrödinger, and 3D Navier-Stokes benchmarks show that PINN-guided refinement achieves lower error with fewer degrees of freedom than uniform refinement, with PINN-threshold reducing L2 error by 67.5% at matched mesh size. While gradient-based indicators remain slightly more accurate, PINN residuals consistently outperform random refinement and demonstrate potential as a physics-aware residual indicator for adaptive meshing without replacing the classical solver.
该论文针对有限差分PDE求解器中均匀网格细化效率低的问题，提出利用物理信息神经网络（PINN）作为离网格残差探测器，指导自适应网格细化。PINN在全域计算残差，将其转换为单元级指标，用于在最终求解前决定网格细化位置，最终仍由经典有限差分求解器输出结果。在1D黏性Burgers方程、2D非线性薛定谔方程和3D不可压缩Navier-Stokes系统三个基准测试中，该方法在更少自由度下获得更低误差：PINN阈值细化在60自由度下达到L²误差0.021067，优于均匀细化（192自由度下误差0.022617）；PINN-Dörfler细化表现相近，虽梯度指标略优，但结果表明PINN残差可有效引导结构化细化，优于随机细化，支持其作为物理信息诊断工具用于网格自适应，而不替代经典求解器。
Explicit Second-Order Min-Max Optimization: Practical Algorithms and Complexity Analysis
Authors: Tianyi Lin, Panayotis Mertikopoulos, Michael I. Jordan
First: 2022-10-23T21:24:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:45:39+00:00
Comments: Accepted by TMLR; Adding funding information; 35 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose and analyze several inexact regularized Newton-type methods for finding a global saddle point of convex-concave unconstrained min-max optimization problems. Compared to first-order methods, our understanding of second-order methods for min-max optimization is relatively limited, as obtaining global rates of convergence with second-order information can be much more involved. In this paper, we examine how second-order information is used to speed up extra-gradient methods, even under inexactness. In particular, we show that the proposed methods generate iterates that remain within a bounded set and that the averaged iterates converge to an $ε$-saddle point within $O(ε^{-2/3})$ iterations in terms of a restricted gap function. We also provide a simple routine for solving the subproblem at each iteration, requiring a single Schur decomposition and $O(\log\log(1/ε))$ calls to a linear system solver in a quasi-upper-triangular system. Thus, our method improves the existing line-search-based second-order min-max optimization methods by shaving off an $O(\log\log(1/ε))$ factor in the required number of Schur decompositions. Finally, we evaluate our method on both synthetic benchmarks and a real-world application arising from AUC maximization on standard LIBSVM datasets, and find that the proposed second-order approach delivers stronger practical efficiency than representative first-order methods on these problems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：显式二阶极小-极大优化：实用算法与复杂度分析
我们提出并分析了几种不精确正则化牛顿型方法，用于求解凸-凹无约束极小-极大优化问题的全局鞍点。与一阶方法相比，我们对二阶方法在极小-极大优化中的理解相对有限，因为利用二阶信息获得全局收敛速率往往更为复杂。本文探讨了如何利用二阶信息加速额外梯度方法，即使在不精确的情况下。特别地，我们证明所提方法生成的迭代点保持在有界集内，且平均迭代点在受限间隙函数意义下于 $O(ε^{-2/3})$ 次迭代内收敛至 $ε$-鞍点。此外，我们提供了一种简单子问题求解程序，每次迭代仅需一次舒尔分解和 $O(\log\log(1/ε))$ 次对准上三角系统的线性系统求解器调用。因此，我们的方法在所需舒尔分解次数上较现有基于线搜索的二阶极小-极大优化方法提升了 $O(\log\log(1/ε))$ 因子。最后，我们在合成基准和来自标准LIBSVM数据集的AUC最大化真实应用中评估了该方法，发现所提出的二阶方法在这些问题上比代表性的一阶方法具有更强的实用效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited theoretical understanding of second-order methods for min-max optimization, this paper proposes inexact regularized Newton-type algorithms to find global saddle points in convex-concave unconstrained problems. The methods leverage second-order information to accelerate extra-gradient iterations, even under inexact computations, and guarantee convergence to an ε-saddle point within O(ε^{-2/3}) iterations measured by a restricted gap function. A key innovation is a subproblem solver requiring only one Schur decomposition and O(log log(1/ε)) linear system solves per iteration, reducing the Schur decomposition cost compared to prior second-order methods. Experiments on synthetic and real-world AUC maximization tasks using LIBSVM datasets show that the proposed second-order approach outperforms representative first-order methods in practical efficiency.
为弥补当前对min-max优化中二阶方法理论理解的不足，本文提出了一类不精确的正则化牛顿型算法，用于加速凸凹无约束问题中全局鞍点的收敛。该方法将二阶信息融入额外梯度框架，确保迭代点有界，并在受限间隙函数下于O(ε^{-2/3})次迭代内收敛至ε-鞍点。其核心创新是每个迭代仅需一次舒尔分解和O(log log(1/ε))次线性系统求解，显著降低舒尔分解开销。在合成数据和真实AUC最大化任务（基于LIBSVM数据集）上的实验表明，该二阶方法在实际效率上优于代表性的一阶方法。
Degeneration of Sliding-Window Factor Graph Optimization into Iterated Extended Kalman Filtering
Authors: Baoshan Song, Ruijie Xu, Zhi Zhan, Li-Ta Hsu
First: 2025-10-31T23:10:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:45:28+00:00
Comments: Accepted by Nature Partner Journal Wireless Technology
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sliding window factor graph optimization (SW-FGO) is widely recognized for its robustness, yet its theoretical relationship with the extended Kalman filter (EKF) remains a subject of debate. This paper establishes the sufficient conditions to bridge SW-FGO with the iterated extended Kalman filter (IEKF). We introduce recursive FGO (Re-FGO), a conceptual perspective that employs a two-stage marginalization pipeline to mathematically degenerate the factor graph optimization to the IEKF recursive update. By enforcing the Markov assumption and a single-state window, we prove the theoretical equivalence between the IEKF and Re-FGO. This degeneration is validated through simulations and real-world urban GNSS and INS tightly coupled fusion experiments. The results confirm that Re-FGO exactly reproduces IEKF estimation behavior, demonstrating that the two-stage marginalization pipeline is foundational to enforce structural consistency, thereby successfully uniting graph-based smoothing and filtering paradigms under unified optimization principles.
中文标题/摘要
标题：滑动窗口因子图优化退化为迭代扩展卡尔曼滤波
滑动窗口因子图优化（SW-FGO）因其鲁棒性广受认可，但其与扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）的理论关系仍存争议。本文建立了将SW-FGO与迭代扩展卡尔曼滤波（IEKF）关联的充分条件。我们引入递归FGO（Re-FGO），这是一种概念性视角，通过两阶段边际化管道，将因子图优化数学上退化为IEKF的递归更新。通过强制马尔可夫假设和单状态窗口，我们证明了IEKF与Re-FGO的理论等价性。该退化通过仿真及城市GNSS与INS紧耦合融合的实测实验得到验证。结果表明，Re-FGO精确复现IEKF的估计行为，证明两阶段边际化管道是实现结构一致性基础，从而成功在统一优化原则下融合基于图的平滑与滤波范式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to clarify the theoretical relationship between sliding-window factor graph optimization and the extended Kalman filter, this paper introduces recursive FGO (Re-FGO), a method that uses a two-stage marginalization pipeline to mathematically degenerate factor graph optimization into the iterated extended Kalman filter (IEKF). By imposing the Markov assumption and a single-state window, the authors establish theoretical equivalence between Re-FGO and IEKF. Experimental results from urban GNSS/INS fusion scenarios confirm that Re-FGO exactly replicates IEKF behavior, demonstrating that this two-stage marginalization enables structural consistency and unifies graph-based smoothing and filtering under a common optimization framework.
为厘清滑动窗口因子图优化与扩展卡尔曼滤波之间的理论关系，本文提出递归因子图优化（Re-FGO），通过两阶段边际化流程将因子图优化数学退化为迭代扩展卡尔曼滤波（IEKF）。在强制马尔可夫假设和单状态窗口条件下，作者证明了Re-FGO与IEKF的理论等价性。在城市GNSS/INS紧耦合融合实验中，结果表明Re-FGO精确复现IEKF估计行为，证实两阶段边际化流程可实现结构一致性，从而统一了基于图的平滑与滤波方法于同一优化框架之下。
MCP-Persona: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Real-World Personal Applications via Environment Simulation
Authors: Wenhao Wang, Peizhi Niu, Gongyi Zou, Xiyuan Yang, Jingxing Wang, Haoting Shi, Yaxin Du, Jingyi Chai, Xianghe Pang, Shuo Tang, Yanfeng Wang, Siheng Chen
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-01T16:44:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:44:10+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026 Camera Ready
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has emerged as a transformative standard for connecting large language models (LLMs) with external data sources and tools, and has been rapidly adopted across personal applications and development platforms. However, existing benchmarks predominantly focus on generic information-seeking tools and fail to capture the practical challenges posed by personal social applications, where tools interact with individual accounts or local databases. To bridge this critical gap, we introduce MCP-Persona, the first benchmark specifically designed for evaluating agent performance on real-world, personalized MCP tools. MCP-Persona encompasses a diverse set of widely-used applications, ranging from social media platforms like Reddit and Xiaohongshu (Rednote) to enterprise collaboration suites such as Lark (Feishu) and Slack. Our extensive experiments on various state-of-the-art (SOTA) agents demonstrate their significant struggles with personalized tool use, thereby highlighting the benchmark's crucial role in identifying and addressing these limitations. MCP-Persona is publicly available at https://github.com/wwh0411/MCP-Persona}{https://github.com/wwh0411/MCP-Persona.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MCP-人格：通过环境模拟在真实个人应用中基准测试大语言模型代理
模型上下文协议（MCP）已成为连接大语言模型（LLM）与外部数据源和工具的变革性标准，并在个人应用和开发平台中迅速普及。然而，现有基准主要关注通用信息检索工具，未能体现个人社交应用中的实际挑战，即工具需与个人账户或本地数据库交互。为弥合这一关键差距，我们提出了MCP-人格，这是首个专门用于评估代理在真实个性化MCP工具上表现的基准。MCP-人格涵盖广泛使用的应用，从Reddit和小红书（红笔记）等社交媒体平台，到飞书（Lark）和Slack等企业协作套件。我们在多种最先进（SOTA）代理上的广泛实验表明，它们在个性化工具使用方面存在显著困难，从而凸显该基准在识别和解决这些局限性中的关键作用。MCP-人格公开获取地址：https://github.com/wwh0411/MCP-Persona。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate large language model agents in real-world personal applications, this paper introduces MCP-Persona, a benchmark designed to assess agent performance on personalized MCP tools that interact with individual accounts or local data. The method simulates environments from diverse platforms including Reddit, Xiaohongshu, Lark, and Slack, enabling agents to perform tasks requiring personal context. Experimental results show that state-of-the-art agents struggle significantly with personalized tool usage, revealing critical gaps in their ability to handle user-specific data and social application workflows.
为评估大语言模型代理在真实个人应用场景中的表现，本文提出MCP-Persona基准，专门用于测试代理在与个人账户或本地数据交互的个性化MCP工具上的能力。该方法通过模拟Reddit、小红书、飞书、Slack等平台环境，使代理执行需个人上下文和工具特异性交互的任务。实验结果表明，当前最先进的代理在个性化工具使用上表现不佳，揭示了其能力缺陷，凸显了MCP-Persona在识别并引导改进个人应用支持方面的关键作用。
Generative AI and Sales Productivity: Field Experiments in Online Retail
Authors: Lu Fang, Zhe Yuan, Kaifu Zhang, Dante Donati, Miklos Sarvary
First: 2025-10-14T01:17:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:42:12+00:00
Comments: Keywords: Artificial Intelligence, Consumer Experience, Field Experiments, GenAI, Productivity, Retail Platforms, Sales. JEL codes: C93, D24, L81, M31, O3
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We quantify the short-term impact of Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) on sales performance through a series of large-scale randomized field experiments involving millions of users and products at a leading cross-border online retail platform. Over 2023-2024, the platform integrated GenAI into seven consumer-facing business workflows spanning customer service, consumer-product matching, advertising, and seller services. We find that GenAI adoption increases sales in most workflows, with effects ranging from no detectable impact to $16.3\%$, depending on GenAI's marginal contribution relative to baseline firm practices. Across the four GenAI applications with positive sales effects, the implied annual incremental value is roughly $\$5-$an economically meaningful impact given the retailer's scale and the early stage of GenAI adoption. The gains operate primarily through higher conversion rates rather than larger cart values, consistent with GenAI improving the shopping experience by reducing search, information, communication, and personalization frictions. Importantly, these effects are not associated with worse post-purchase outcomes, as product return rates and customer ratings do not deteriorate. Finally, we document substantial demand-side heterogeneity, with larger gains for less experienced consumers. Our findings provide novel, large-scale causal evidence on how GenAI shapes sales productivity in online retail, highlighting both its immediate value and broader potential.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生成式人工智能与销售效率：在线零售领域的实地实验
我们通过一系列大规模随机实地实验，量化了生成式人工智能（GenAI）对销售业绩的短期影响，实验涉及一家领先跨境电商平台数百万用户和产品。2023至2024年间，该平台将GenAI整合进七个面向消费者的业务流程，涵盖客户服务、消费者-商品匹配、广告和卖家服务。我们发现，GenAI的采用在多数流程中提升了销售额，效果从无显著影响到16.3%，取决于GenAI相对于企业基线实践的边际贡献。在四个产生正向销售效应的GenAI应用中，其隐含的年度增量价值约为5美元，考虑到零售商规模及GenAI尚处早期阶段，这一影响具有经济意义。收益主要通过提高转化率实现，而非提升购物车价值，这与GenAI通过降低搜索、信息、沟通和个性化摩擦来改善购物体验一致。重要的是，这些效应并未伴随售后表现恶化，产品退货率和客户评分未下降。最后，我们发现需求方存在显著异质性，经验较少的消费者获益更大。我们的研究提供了关于GenAI如何塑造在线零售销售效率的全新、大规模因果证据，凸显其即时价值与更广泛潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand GenAI’s real-world impact on sales, this study conducts large-scale randomized field experiments across seven consumer-facing workflows in a major cross-border online retail platform. By integrating GenAI into customer service, product matching, advertising, and seller services between 2023 and 2024, the researchers measure its effect on sales performance. Results show GenAI boosts sales in most cases, with gains ranging from zero to 16.3%, depending on its marginal contribution relative to existing practices. The largest gains come from improved conversion rates, not higher cart values, and are driven by reduced friction in search, communication, and personalization. Importantly, post-purchase metrics like returns and ratings remain unaffected, and the benefits are strongest among less experienced consumers. The implied annual incremental value per user is around $5, which is economically significant given the platform’s scale and early adoption stage.
为量化生成式人工智能（GenAI）对线上零售销售的实际影响，研究在大型跨境零售平台的七个面向消费者的工作流中开展大规模随机对照实验。GenAI被应用于客户服务、商品匹配、广告和商家服务等环节，实验覆盖2023至2024年。结果显示，GenAI在多数场景下提升销售额，增幅从无显著影响到16.3%，主要通过提高转化率实现，而非提升客单价，表明其优化了用户体验，降低了搜索、信息获取与个性化摩擦。这些收益未伴随售后体验恶化，退货率与评分未下降，且对经验较少的消费者收益更大，体现需求端异质性。按用户计算的年增量价值约为5美元，考虑到平台规模与GenAI尚处早期阶段，该收益具有显著经济意义。
KromHC: Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections with Kronecker-Product Residual Matrices
Authors: Wuyang Zhou, Yuxuan Gu, Giorgos Iacovides, Danilo Mandic
First: 2026-01-29T11:43:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:38:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The success of Hyper-Connections (HC) in neural networks (NN) has also highlighted issues related to training instability and restricted scalability. The Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC) mitigate these challenges by projecting the residual connection space onto a Birkhoff polytope, however, it faces two issues: 1) its iterative Sinkhorn-Knopp (SK) algorithm does not always yield exactly doubly stochastic residual matrices; 2) mHC incurs a prohibitive $O(n^3C)$ parameter complexity with $n$ as the width of the residual stream and $C$ as the feature dimension. The recently proposed mHC-lite reparametrizes the residual matrix via the Birkhoff-von-Neumann theorem to guarantee double stochasticity, but also faces a factorial explosion in its parameter complexity, $O \left( nC \cdot n! \right)$. To address both challenges, we propose KromHC, which uses the Kronecker products of smaller doubly stochastic matrices to parametrize the residual matrix in mHC. By enforcing manifold constraints across the factor residual matrices along each mode of the tensorized residual stream, KromHC guarantees exact double stochasticity of the residual matrices while reducing parameter complexity to only $O(n^2C)$. Experiments show that KromHC matches or even outperforms other state-of-the-art (SOTA) mHC variants, while requiring significantly fewer trainable parameters. The code is at https://github.com/wz1119/KromHC.
中文标题/摘要
标题：KromHC：基于克罗内克积残差矩阵的流形约束超连接
超连接（HC）在神经网络中的成功也凸显了训练不稳定性和扩展性受限的问题。流形约束超连接（mHC）通过将残差连接空间投影到Birkhoff多面体上缓解了这些问题，但存在两个问题：1）其迭代Sinkhorn-Knopp（SK）算法并不总能生成精确的双随机残差矩阵；2）mHC的参数复杂度为O(n³C)，其中n为残差流宽度，C为特征维度。近期提出的mHC-lite通过Birkhoff-von-Neumann定理重新参数化残差矩阵以保证双随机性，但其参数复杂度呈阶乘爆炸，为O(nC·n!)。为解决上述两个挑战，我们提出KromHC，通过使用较小的双随机矩阵的克罗内克积参数化mHC中的残差矩阵。通过在张量化残差流的每个模式上强制施加流形约束，KromHC保证残差矩阵的精确双随机性，同时将参数复杂度降低至O(n²C)。实验表明，KromHC在参数量显著减少的情况下，性能与甚至优于其他最先进的mHC变体。代码见https://github.com/wz1119/KromHC。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the training instability and scalability issues of Hyper-Connections, this work introduces KromHC, a manifold-constrained variant that parametrizes residual matrices via Kronecker products of smaller doubly stochastic matrices. This approach enforces exact double stochasticity while reducing parameter complexity from O(n^3C) in prior methods to O(n^2C), avoiding the factorial explosion seen in mHC-lite. Experimental results demonstrate that KromHC achieves performance matching or exceeding state-of-the-art mHC variants while using significantly fewer trainable parameters.
为解决超连接网络训练不稳定与扩展性差的问题，KromHC采用Kronecker积形式的小型双随机矩阵参数化残差连接，确保残差矩阵精确双随机且将参数复杂度从O(n^3C)降至O(n^2C)。实验表明，KromHC在性能上达到或超越现有最先进mHC变体，同时显著减少可训练参数量。
Learning When to Translate for Multilingual Reasoning
Authors: Deokhyung Kang, Hyounghun Kim, Gary Geunbae Lee
First: 2026-06-01T16:37:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:37:18+00:00
Comments: preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reasoning language models (RLMs) achieve strong performance on complex reasoning tasks, but still exhibit substantial multilingual reasoning gaps, largely due to language-understanding failures in non-English inputs. English translation can mitigate these failures by expressing non-English inputs in a form that RLMs can more reliably interpret, yet translating every input is unnecessary when the model can reason reliably from the original query. To address this challenge, we propose Luar, a Language Understanding Boundary-aware Reinforcement Learning framework that trains RLMs to selectively invoke translation when direct understanding is unreliable. Luar trains the model to choose between solving the original input directly and reasoning over its English translation, encouraging translation only when translator-augmented reasoning is expected to substantially outperform direct reasoning. Across multilingual reasoning benchmarks, Luar outperforms standard GRPO and other training-based baselines, with particularly large gains on low-resource languages. Further analysis shows that Luar avoids unnecessary translation in cases where direct reasoning is sufficient, while extending its translator-call behavior to unseen low-resource languages. Together, our work suggests a selective approach to multilingual reasoning: RLMs can learn to invoke translation only when their direct understanding is unreliable. The project will be made publicly available at https://github.com/deokhk/LUAR
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习何时翻译以实现多语言推理
推理语言模型（RLMs）在复杂推理任务上表现优异，但在多语言推理方面仍存在显著差距，主要源于对非英语输入的理解失败。英语翻译可通过将非英语输入转化为RLMs更可靠理解的形式来缓解这些失败，但当模型能直接从原始查询可靠推理时，并非所有输入都需要翻译。为解决此问题，我们提出Luar框架，即一种语言理解边界感知的强化学习框架，训练RLMs在直接理解不可靠时选择性调用翻译。Luar训练模型在直接处理原始输入与基于其英语翻译推理之间做出选择，仅在翻译增强推理预期显著优于直接推理时才调用翻译。在多个多语言推理基准测试中，Luar优于标准GRPO及其他基于训练的基线方法，尤其在低资源语言上收益显著。进一步分析表明，Luar在直接推理足够时避免了不必要的翻译，同时将其翻译调用行为扩展至未见过的低资源语言。我们的工作表明，多语言推理可采取选择性策略：RLMs可仅在直接理解不可靠时调用翻译。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent multilingual reasoning gaps in reasoning language models caused by poor understanding of non-English inputs, this work proposes Luar, a reinforcement learning framework that trains models to selectively translate inputs only when direct reasoning is unreliable. Luar learns to choose between solving tasks directly in the original language or using an English translation, rewarding translation only when it leads to significantly better performance. Experiments across multilingual benchmarks show Luar outperforms standard baselines like GRPO, especially on low-resource languages, while avoiding unnecessary translation in cases where direct reasoning suffices and generalizing to unseen low-resource languages.
为解决语言模型在非英语输入上理解能力不足导致的多语言推理差距，作者提出Luar框架，通过强化学习训练模型仅在直接推理不可靠时才调用翻译。该方法仅在翻译增强推理预期显著优于直接推理时才触发翻译，避免不必要的翻译开销。实验表明，Luar在多个多语言推理基准上优于标准GRPO及其他基线方法，尤其在低资源语言上收益显著，同时在直接推理足够时能有效避免翻译，并可泛化至未见的低资源语言。
MASER: Modality-Adaptive Specialist Routing for Embodied 3D Spatial Intelligence
Authors: Hilton Raj, Vishnuram AV
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-06-01T16:36:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:36:21+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026 Foundation Models Meet Embodied Agents Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In 3D environments, Embodied Agents answer spatially relevant questions through reasoning from a mixture of modalities including natural language, RGB images, point clouds, depth maps and camera poses. Existing Vision-Language models (VLMs) are fine-tuned over a single modality. This completely ignores the question semantics which may favor a different modality than the finetuned modality. To address this, we propose MASER (Modality-Adaptive SpEcialist Routing), a lightweight framework that trains five different modality adapters of a shared VLM backbone and learns a neural routing policy that selects the best adapter based on the question during inference. We encode each question with a frozen sentence transformer and pass the embedding through a small Multi-layer Perceptron (MLP) trained on oracle adapter-accuracy labels. We evaluate our methodology over the Open3D-VQA benchmark and our evaluations show that no single modality is universally optimal -- point-cloud answers are best in 51.5% of cases. MASER routes with 51.3% oracle agreement, outperforming a Random-Forest ablation (43.5%), with only a single adapter call per question.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MASER：面向具身3D空间智能的模态自适应专家路由
在3D环境中，具身智能体通过结合自然语言、RGB图像、点云、深度图和相机位姿等多种模态进行推理，以回答空间相关问题。现有视觉-语言模型（VLMs）通常仅在一个模态上进行微调，完全忽略了问题语义可能更偏好其他模态的情况。为解决此问题，我们提出MASER（模态自适应专家路由），一个轻量级框架，它在共享VLM主干网络上训练五个不同模态的适配器，并学习一个神经路由策略，在推理时根据问题选择最佳适配器。我们使用冻结的句子变换器编码每个问题，并将嵌入通过一个小型多层感知机（MLP），该MLP在oracle适配器准确率标签上训练。我们在Open3D-VQA基准上评估该方法，结果表明没有单一模态在所有情况下最优——点云答案在51.5%的情况下表现最佳。MASER的路由准确率（oracle一致性）达51.3%，优于随机森林消融实验（43.5%），且每个问题仅需调用一次适配器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to adapt to question semantics that may favor different modalities than those used in standard fine-tuning, MASER introduces a lightweight framework that trains five modality-specific adapters on a shared Vision-Language Model backbone and employs a neural routing policy to select the optimal adapter at inference time. The routing decision is made by encoding questions via a frozen sentence transformer and passing the embedding through a small MLP trained on oracle adapter-accuracy labels. Experimental results on the Open3D-VQA benchmark demonstrate that no single modality is universally best—point clouds yield the highest accuracy in 51.5% of cases—and MASER achieves 51.3% oracle agreement, significantly outperforming a Random-Forest baseline (43.5%) while requiring only one adapter call per question.
为解决现有视觉-语言模型因单一模态微调而忽略问题语义可能偏好其他模态的问题，MASER提出一种轻量框架，训练五个不同模态的适配器共享同一主干网络，并通过神经路由策略在推理时选择最优适配器。问题经冻结的句子编码器编码后，通过小型MLP（基于oracle适配器准确率标签训练）进行路由决策。在Open3D-VQA基准上，MASER路由准确率达51.3%，优于随机森林基线（43.5%），表明无单一模态最优——点云在51.5%情况下表现最佳，且每问题仅调用一次适配器。
Latent-Conditioned Parameterized Quantum Circuits as Universal Approximators for Distributions over Quantum States
Authors: Quoc Hoan Tran, Koki Chinzei, Yasuhiro Endo, Hirotaka Oshima
First: 2026-05-27T16:19:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:36:20+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 11 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many applications in quantum simulation, quantum chemistry, and quantum machine learning require not a single quantum state but an ensemble of states characterizing the heterogeneity of a target system. Preparing such ensembles state-by-state is prohibitive in both variational and fault-tolerant settings, motivating a generative-modeling approach. We introduce latent-conditioned parameterized quantum circuits (LPQCs), a hybrid quantum-classical framework in which classical neural networks map a latent variable sampled from a prior distribution to the parameters of a parameterized quantum circuit. We prove that LPQCs are universal approximators for probability measures over density operators in the $1$-Wasserstein distance, extending classical universal approximation theorems to the quantum-distribution setting. We additionally introduce a multimodal latent prior and a mixture-of-experts circuit architecture, and show that it empirically alleviates the barren plateau problem during optimization. Numerical experiments validate the framework on a synthetic multi-cluster ensemble of mixed quantum states and on a QM9-derived ensemble of 3-D molecular structures. In these tasks, LPQC outperforms recent quantum generative baselines while remaining competitive with typical classical baselines at substantially reduced output dimensionality. By leveraging classical expressivity in the latent space, LPQCs offer a tractable route to quantum generative modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于潜在条件的参数化量子电路作为量子态分布的通用逼近器
在量子模拟、量子化学和量子机器学习中，许多应用需要的不是单个量子态，而是表征目标系统异质性的量子态集合。逐个制备此类集合在变分和容错设置中均不可行，因此需要采用生成建模方法。我们引入了潜在条件参数化量子电路（LPQCs），这是一种混合量子-经典框架，其中经典神经网络将从先验分布中采样的潜在变量映射为参数化量子电路的参数。我们证明了LPQCs在1- Wasserstein距离下是密度算子概率测度的通用逼近器，将经典通用逼近定理扩展至量子分布场景。此外，我们引入了多模态潜在先验和专家混合电路架构，并表明其在优化过程中能有效缓解 barren plateau 问题。数值实验在合成多簇混合量子态集合和基于QM9的3D分子结构集合上验证了该框架。在这些任务中，LPQCs优于近期量子生成基线，同时在大幅降低输出维度的情况下仍与典型经典基线保持竞争力。通过在潜在空间中利用经典表达能力，LPQCs为量子生成建模提供了一条可行路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate ensembles of quantum states for applications in quantum simulation and machine learning, this work introduces latent-conditioned parameterized quantum circuits (LPQCs), a hybrid quantum-classical model where classical neural networks map latent variables to quantum circuit parameters. The authors prove LPQCs are universal approximators for quantum state distributions under the 1-Wasserstein distance, extending classical approximation theorems to the quantum domain. Empirical results demonstrate that LPQCs, enhanced with multimodal priors and mixture-of-experts architectures, effectively mitigate barren plateaus and outperform recent quantum generative models while matching classical baselines at lower output dimensionality, validating their utility for scalable quantum generative modeling.
为应对量子模拟与机器学习中生成量子态集合的需求，本文提出一种混合量子-经典框架——潜变量条件化参数化量子电路（LPQCs），其中经典神经网络将潜变量映射至量子电路参数。作者证明LPQCs在1- Wasserstein距离下可作为量子态分布的通用逼近器，将经典逼近理论扩展至量子领域。实验表明，结合多模态潜变量先验与专家混合电路架构的LPQCs可有效缓解 barren plateau 问题，在合成多簇混合态与QM9衍生分子结构数据集上，其性能优于近期量子生成模型，同时在更低输出维度下与经典基线方法相当，验证了其在可扩展量子生成建模中的实用性。
When Does Predictive Inverse Dynamics Outperform Behavior Cloning?
Authors: Lukas Schäfer, Pallavi Choudhury, Abdelhak Lemkhenter, Chris Lovett, Somjit Nath, Luis França, Matheus Ribeiro Furtado de Mendonça, Alex Lamb, Riashat Islam, Siddhartha Sen, John Langford, Katja Hofmann, Sergio Valcarcel Macua
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-01-29T13:43:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:36:12+00:00
Comments: To be published in proceedings of the International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Behavior cloning (BC) is a practical offline imitation learning method, but it often fails when expert demonstrations are limited. Recent works have introduced a class of architectures named predictive inverse dynamics models (PIDM) that combine a future state predictor with an inverse dynamics model. While PIDM often outperforms BC, the reasons behind its benefits remain unclear. In this paper, we provide a theoretical explanation: PIDM introduces a bias-variance tradeoff. While predicting the future state introduces bias, conditioning the IDM on the prediction can significantly reduce variance. We establish conditions on the state predictor bias for PIDM to achieve lower prediction error and higher sample efficiency than BC, with the gap widening when additional data sources are available. We validate the theoretical insights empirically in 2D navigation tasks, where BC requires up to five times (three times on average) more demonstrations than PIDM to reach comparable performance; and in a complex 3D environment in a modern video game with high-dimensional visual inputs and stochastic transitions, where BC requires over 66% more samples than PIDM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：预测逆动力学何时优于行为克隆？
行为克隆（BC）是一种实用的离线模仿学习方法，但在专家演示有限时往往失效。近期研究引入了一类名为预测逆动力学模型（PIDM）的架构，结合未来状态预测器与逆动力学模型。尽管PIDM通常优于BC，其优势原因尚不明确。本文提供理论解释：PIDM引入了偏差-方差权衡。虽然预测未来状态引入偏差，但以预测结果为条件的逆动力学模型可显著降低方差。我们建立了PIDM在预测误差更低、样本效率更高方面优于BC的条件，且当有额外数据源时，差距会扩大。我们在2D导航任务中实证验证了理论见解：BC需达到与PIDM相当性能的演示次数是其5倍（平均3倍）；在现代视频游戏中复杂3D环境（高维视觉输入与随机转移）中，BC所需样本量比PIDM多66%以上。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor performance of behavior cloning (BC) under limited expert demonstrations, this paper investigates why predictive inverse dynamics models (PIDM) often outperform BC. The authors propose a theoretical framework showing that PIDM introduces a bias-variance tradeoff: while future state prediction adds bias, conditioning the inverse dynamics model on this prediction reduces variance, leading to better generalization. Empirically, in 2D navigation tasks, PIDM required up to five times fewer demonstrations than BC to reach comparable performance, and in a complex 3D video game environment with high-dimensional inputs and stochastic transitions, PIDM needed over 66% fewer samples than BC.
由于行为克隆（BC）在专家演示数据有限时表现不佳，本文研究了预测逆动力学模型（PIDM）为何常优于BC。作者提出理论框架表明，PIDM引入了偏差-方差权衡：虽然状态预测增加偏差，但将逆动力学模型条件化于预测可显著降低方差，从而提升泛化能力。在2D导航和复杂3D视频游戏环境中实验证明，PIDM所需演示数据比BC少得多——在2D中最多少5倍，平均少3倍，在3D中少66%以上，即可达到相当性能。
The Entropic Signature of Class Speciation in Diffusion Models
Authors: Florian Handke, Dejan Stančević, Felix Koulischer, Thomas Demeester, Luca Ambrogioni
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-02-10T10:56:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:35:54+00:00
Comments: Accepted at International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models do not recover semantic structure uniformly over time. Instead, samples transition from semantic ambiguity to class commitment within a narrow regime. Recent theoretical work attributes this transition to dynamical instabilities along class-separating directions, but practical methods to detect and exploit these windows in trained models are still limited. We show that tracking the class-conditional entropy of a latent semantic variable given the noisy state provides a reliable signature of these transition regimes. By restricting the entropy to semantic partitions, the entropy can furthermore resolve semantic decisions at different levels of abstraction. We analyze this behavior in high-dimensional Gaussian mixture models and show that the entropy rate concentrates on the same logarithmic time scale as the speciation symmetry-breaking instability previously identified in variance-preserving diffusion. We validate our method on EDM2-XS and Stable Diffusion 1.5, where class-conditional entropy consistently isolates the noise regimes critical for semantic structure formation. Finally, we use our framework to quantify how guidance redistributes semantic information over time. Together, these results connect information-theoretic and statistical physics perspectives on diffusion and provide a principled basis for time-localized control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散模型中类分化过程的熵特征
扩散模型在时间上并非均匀恢复语义结构，而是样本在狭窄范围内从语义模糊过渡到类别承诺。近期理论工作将此过渡归因于沿类别分离方向的动力学不稳定性，但实际检测和利用训练模型中这些窗口的方法仍有限。我们表明，跟踪给定噪声状态下的条件语义变量熵，可可靠标识这些过渡阶段。通过限制熵至语义分区，该熵还能解析不同抽象层次的语义决策。我们在高维高斯混合模型中分析此行为，发现熵率集中于与先前在保方差扩散中识别的分化对称性破缺不稳定性相同的对数时间尺度。我们在EDM2-XS和Stable Diffusion 1.5上验证了该方法，其中类别条件熵持续隔离对语义结构形成至关重要的噪声阶段。最后，我们利用该框架量化引导如何随时间重新分配语义信息。这些结果将扩散的信息论与统计物理视角联系起来，并为时间局部化控制提供理论基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that diffusion models exhibit non-uniform semantic recovery over time, this work investigates how class-speciation transitions occur within narrow noise regimes. The authors propose tracking class-conditional entropy of a latent semantic variable given the noisy state as a reliable indicator of these transitions. By constraining entropy to semantic partitions, the method resolves decisions at varying abstraction levels. Experiments on Gaussian mixture models and real diffusion models (EDM2-XS and Stable Diffusion 1.5) show that entropy concentrates on the same logarithmic time scale as previously identified symmetry-breaking instabilities, and consistently isolates critical noise regimes for semantic structure formation. The framework further quantifies how guidance redistributes semantic information over time, bridging information theory and statistical physics perspectives.
鉴于扩散模型在时间上并非均匀恢复语义结构，本文研究了类分化过渡如何通过信息论特征体现。作者提出通过跟踪给定噪声状态下的类条件熵来识别语义承诺发生的狭窄时间窗口，并通过限制熵至语义分区以解析不同抽象层级的语义决策。在高维高斯混合模型及真实扩散模型（EDM2-XS与Stable Diffusion 1.5）上的实验表明，熵集中于与先前识别的对称性破缺不稳定性相同的对数时间尺度，并能有效隔离语义结构形成的关键噪声阶段。该框架还可量化引导如何随时间重新分配语义信息，将信息论与统计物理视角结合，为时间局部化控制提供理论基础。
A Systematic Benchmark of Machine Transliteration Models for the Tajik-Farsi Language Pair: A Comparative Study from Rule-Based to Transformer Architectures
Authors: Mullosharaf K. Arabov
First: 2026-05-04T06:24:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:34:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CLIB 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents the first comprehensive comparative analysis of modern machine learning architectures for transliteration between Tajik (Cyrillic script) and Persian (Arabic script). A key contribution is the creation and validation of a unique parallel corpus aggregated from multiple heterogeneous sources, including crowdsourced projects, lexicographic pairs, parallel texts of "Shahnameh", diplomatic articles, texts of "Masnavi-i Ma'navi", official terminology lists, and transliterated correspondences. The initial dataset comprised 328,253 sentence pairs; a representative subset of 40,000 pairs was formed using stratified random sampling. The experiment compared six classes of models: rule-based baseline, LSTM with attention, character-level Transformer, G2P Transformer (trained from scratch), pre-trained multilingual models (mBART, mT5 with LoRA), and byte-level ByT5. Results demonstrate the overwhelming superiority of ByT5 (chrF++ 87.4 for Tajik to Farsi, 80.1 for reverse). The G2P Transformer significantly outperformed mBART (72.3 vs. 62.2 chrF++) despite limited data. Models using subword tokenization (mT5) failed completely (chrF++ less than 18.5). The findings demonstrate that for accurate transliteration of the Tajik-Farsi pair, architectures operating at the byte or character level are unequivocally more effective than traditional multilingual Seq2Seq models relying on subword tokenization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：塔吉克语-波斯语机器转写模型的系统性基准评测：从规则基到Transformer架构的比较研究
本文首次对塔吉克语（西里尔字母）与波斯语（阿拉伯字母）之间现代机器学习架构的转写能力进行了全面比较分析。主要贡献是构建并验证了一个独特的平行语料库，该语料库整合了多个异构来源，包括众包项目、词典配对、《列王纪》平行文本、外交文件、《玛斯纳维·玛纳维》文本、官方术语列表及转写对应文本。初始语料库包含328,253个句子对，通过分层随机抽样形成40,000对代表性子集。 实验比较了六类模型：规则基基线模型、带注意力机制的LSTM、字符级Transformer、从零训练的G2P Transformer、预训练多语言模型（mBART、mT5+LoRA）及字节级ByT5。结果表明，ByT5表现卓越（塔吉克语→波斯语chrF++为87.4，反向为80.1）。尽管数据有限，G2P Transformer仍显著优于mBART（72.3 vs. 62.2 chrF++）。采用子词分词的模型（mT5）表现完全失败（chrF++低于18.5）。 研究结果表明，对于塔吉克语-波斯语的准确转写，字节或字符级架构明显优于依赖子词分词的传统多语言Seq2Seq模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of systematic evaluation for machine transliteration between Tajik and Persian, this study benchmarks six model classes—from rule-based to Transformer architectures—using a newly compiled 328,253-pair parallel corpus. The experiment employs stratified sampling to form a 40,000-pair subset and evaluates models including LSTM, character-level Transformer, G2P Transformer, and pre-trained multilingual variants. Results show that byte-level ByT5 achieves the highest performance (chrF++ 87.4 and 80.1), followed by G2P Transformer (72.3 vs. 62.2 for mBART), while subword-based models like mT5 underperform drastically (chrF++ < 18.5), confirming that byte or character-level architectures are superior for this language pair.
为填补塔吉克语与波斯语转写模型系统评估的空白，本研究构建了一个来自多种异构来源的平行语料库，并对比了从规则基到Transformer架构的六类模型。在从328,253句对中抽取的4万句代表性样本上，实验表明字节级ByT5表现最优（双向chrF++分别为87.4和80.1），显著优于G2P Transformer（72.3 vs 62.2），而基于子词分词的模型如mT5则完全失败（chrF++<18.5），证明针对该语言对，字节或字符级模型远胜于传统子词分词的多语言序列到序列模型。
AGENTCL: Toward Rigorous Evaluation of Continual Learning in Language Agents
Authors: Yiheng Shu, Bernal Jiménez Gutiérrez, Saisri Padmaja Jonnalagedda, Yuguang Yao, Huan Sun, Yu Su
First: 2026-06-01T16:32:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:32:59+00:00
Comments: 10 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language agents spend substantial inference time solving individual tasks, yet the experience acquired in one episode is often underutilized in future episodes. Continual learning expects an agent to accumulate reusable experience across a stream of tasks, improve over time, and avoid interference from irrelevant experiences. Unfortunately, existing benchmarks struggle to evaluate continual learning in language agents rigorously. Most efforts focus on retrieval and reasoning over long-context conversations or documents, while recent lifelong-adaptation benchmarks often rely on naive task streams with limited analysis of cross-task relationships, making it difficult to understand what an agent learns and reuses over time. This paper presents an evaluation framework AgentCL for continual learning in agents, centered on controlled task streams and metrics for transfer gains. AGENTCL constructs compositional streams where earlier sub-solutions, evidence, or workflows are intentionally reusable in later tasks, and contrasts them with naive streams where such reusability is not guaranteed. We use the benchmark to evaluate non-parametric memory designs for continual learning. To diagnose how memory design choices affect continual learning, we develop MemProbe, a probing method that stores interactions, insights, and skills, while filtering unreliable experiences during consolidation. Empirical analysis across coding, deep research, and language understanding/reasoning tasks shows that naive streams offer limited ability to distinguish memory designs, whereas controlled streams more clearly distinguish their plasticity. Meanwhile, naive and held-out settings often yield limited gains and can expose memory-induced degradation. These results highlight the need for stronger memory designs that balance plasticity and stable reuse.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AGENTCL：面向语言代理持续学习的严谨评估
语言代理在解决单个任务时耗费大量推理时间，但其在单次交互中获得的经验往往在后续交互中未被充分利用。持续学习期望代理能在任务流中积累可复用的经验，随时间提升能力，并避免无关经验的干扰。然而，现有基准难以严谨评估语言代理的持续学习能力。多数研究聚焦于长上下文对话或文档的检索与推理，而近期的终身适应基准常依赖简单任务流，且对跨任务关系分析不足，难以理解代理随时间学习和复用的内容。本文提出AgentCL评估框架，以可控任务流和迁移增益度量为核心，构建组合式任务流，其中早期子解、证据或工作流被有意设计为可复用，与缺乏复用保障的朴素任务流形成对比。我们利用该基准评估持续学习中的非参数记忆设计。为诊断记忆设计对持续学习的影响，我们开发MemProbe探查方法，存储交互、洞察与技能，同时在整合阶段过滤不可靠经验。在编码、深度研究及语言理解/推理任务中的实证分析表明，朴素任务流难以区分不同记忆设计，而可控任务流则更清晰地揭示其可塑性。同时，朴素与保留设置常导致增益有限，甚至暴露记忆引发的性能退化。结果强调需设计更优记忆方案，在可塑性与稳定复用间取得平衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of language agents in reusing past experience across tasks, this paper introduces AgentCL, a framework designed to rigorously evaluate continual learning in language agents. It constructs controlled task streams where earlier solutions or evidence are intentionally reusable in later tasks, contrasting them with naive task streams lacking such structure. The framework evaluates non-parametric memory designs using MemProbe, a probing method that captures interactions and filters unreliable experiences during consolidation. Experimental results across coding, deep research, and reasoning tasks show that naive streams fail to distinguish memory design efficacy, while controlled streams reveal clear differences in plasticity and reuse. Naive and held-out settings often produce minimal gains and can even trigger memory-induced degradation, underscoring the need for memory designs that balance adaptability and stable reuse.
本文针对当前语言代理持续学习评估框架不足的问题，提出AgentCL基准，通过构建任务流中可复用早期经验的控制流，与缺乏复用结构的朴素流形成对比，以更严谨地评估记忆设计。作者引入MemProbe方法，通过存储交互与技能并过滤不可靠经验来诊断记忆机制。实验表明，朴素流难以区分不同记忆设计，而控制流能清晰揭示其可塑性差异，同时朴素与保留设置常导致性能退化，凸显需设计兼顾复用与适应性的记忆机制。
Active Exploring like a Pigeon: Reinforcing Spatial Reasoning via Agentic Vision-Language Models
Authors: Wei Deng, Xianlin Zhang, Mengshi Qi
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-01T16:30:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:30:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Enabling Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to perform spatial reasoning remains challenging. Existing approaches treat VLMs as passive observers, which is difficult for real-world applications. Moreover, reinforcement learning methods rely on sparse rewards, limiting their effectiveness for complex reasoning tasks. Inspired by pigeons' building and exploiting cognitive maps for navigation, we propose a novel agentic pipeline for spatial reasoning. First, we introduce a new \emph{dynamic cognitive map} parameterizing scene layout as object positions and orientations, serving as persistent memory for new observations. Second, we propose a novel \emph{Spatial Assertion Codes (SAC)}, Python expressions programmatically describing spatial relationships. By collaborating with the dynamic cognitive map, SAC enables verification of intermediate reasoning steps, providing dense reward signals. We optimize the model via supervised and reinforcement finetuning. Experiments on the MindCube benchmark demonstrate state-of-the-art performance with \emph{80.5\%} overall accuracy, outperforming the best current method by \emph{29.5} accuracy points (a relative improvement of \emph{53.2\%}) on the challenging \textsc{Rotation} subset. Our code and data are open-sourced at https://github.com/dw-dengwei/active-spatial-reasoning.git.
中文标题/摘要
标题：像鸽子一样主动探索：通过代理视觉-语言模型强化空间推理
使视觉-语言模型（VLMs）执行空间推理仍具挑战性。现有方法将VLMs视为被动观察者，难以满足现实应用需求。此外，强化学习方法依赖稀疏奖励，限制其在复杂推理任务中的有效性。受鸽子构建并利用认知地图进行导航的启发，我们提出了一种新颖的代理式空间推理流程。首先，我们引入一种新的\emph{动态认知地图}，以物体位置和朝向参数化场景布局，作为新观测的持久记忆。其次，我们提出一种新的\emph{空间断言码（SAC）}，用Python表达式程序化描述空间关系。SAC与动态认知地图协同工作，可验证中间推理步骤，提供密集奖励信号。我们通过监督和强化微调优化模型。在MindCube基准测试中，实验结果达到\emph{80.5\%}整体准确率，于挑战性的\textsc{Rotation}子集上超越当前最佳方法\emph{29.5}准确率点（相对提升\emph{53.2\%}）。代码与数据已开源：https://github.com/dw-dengwei/active-spatial-reasoning.git。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of enabling Vision-Language Models to perform spatial reasoning in real-world settings, this work draws inspiration from pigeons' use of cognitive maps for navigation. The authors introduce a dynamic cognitive map that encodes scene layouts as object positions and orientations, acting as persistent memory, and pair it with Spatial Assertion Codes—Python expressions that programmatically describe spatial relationships. These codes enable verification of reasoning steps and generate dense reward signals, improving reinforcement learning effectiveness. The model is optimized via supervised and reinforcement fine-tuning, achieving 80.5% overall accuracy on the MindCube benchmark, a 29.5-point absolute improvement (53.2% relative) over prior methods on the challenging Rotation subset.
为提升视觉语言模型在现实场景中的空间推理能力，该研究提出受鸽子认知地图启发的代理式框架。通过动态认知地图编码场景布局为物体位置与朝向，作为持续记忆，并结合空间断言代码（Python表达式）实现空间关系的程序化描述与推理步骤验证，从而提供密集奖励信号。模型通过监督与强化学习联合微调优化。在MindCube基准测试中，整体准确率达80.5%，在挑战性Rotation子集上较当前最优方法高出29.5分（相对提升53.2%）。
Beyond One-shot: AI Agents for Learning in Field Experiments
Authors: Junjie Luo, Ritu Agarwal, Gordon Gao
First: 2026-06-01T16:30:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:30:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Organizations routinely run experiments for A/B testing, yet the data generated from one experiment is underutilized to inform subsequent intervention design. Significant barriers exist to extracting actionable knowledge from prior experimental data to inform new interventions. We study whether tool-augmented agentic AI can automatically learn from experimental data to generate new interventions in subsequent experiments. Through two-stage field experiments in healthcare prescription messaging (693,139 patient visits), we compare a Human + Chatbot method (Stage 1: behavioral experts with conversational AI co-designing 13 message variants, 444,691 patient visits) against a Tool-Augmented Agentic AI method (Stage 2: AI autonomously extracting principles from Stage 1 data to generate 17 new variants, 248,448 patient visits). The Agentic AI method, equipped with analytical tools, structured Data-Information-Knowledge-Wisdom (DIKW) reasoning agents, and transparent evidence chains, produces superior interventions: the best AI-generated message achieved a 69.8% CTR (+6.5 percentage points over baseline). Critically, our results suggest that the value comes from domain-specific experimental data, not from general reasoning ability: frontier LLMs operating without experimental data failed to predict which interventions would succeed. The field experiments also revealed that general-purpose behavioral theories used for intervention design do not extend uniformly to specific healthcare contexts, motivating an agentic AI approach to theory audits at field-experiment scale. Our research shows that tool-augmented AI can learn from experimental data and generate improved domain-relevant interventions, transforming behavioral experimentation from one-shot evaluation into a scalable system for cumulative design learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越单次实验：用于实地实验中学习的人工智能代理
组织常进行A/B测试实验，但单次实验产生的数据未能充分用于指导后续干预设计。从过往实验数据中提取可操作知识以指导新干预存在重大障碍。我们研究工具增强型代理AI是否能自动从实验数据中学习并生成后续实验的新干预方案。通过医疗处方信息传递领域的两阶段实地实验（693,139次患者就诊），我们比较了“人类+聊天机器人”方法（第一阶段：行为专家与对话AI共同设计13种信息变体，444,691次患者就诊）与“工具增强型代理AI”方法（第二阶段：AI自主从第一阶段数据中提取原则生成17种新变体，248,448次患者就诊）。配备分析工具、结构化数据-信息-知识-智慧（DIKW）推理代理及透明证据链的代理AI方法生成了更优干预方案：最佳AI生成信息的点击率（CTR）达69.8%（比基线高出6.5个百分点）。关键发现是，价值来源于领域特定实验数据，而非通用推理能力：前沿大语言模型在无实验数据情况下无法预测哪些干预会成功。实地实验还表明，用于干预设计的一般性行为理论在特定医疗情境下无法普遍适用，这推动了在实地实验规模下采用代理AI进行理论审计。我们的研究证明，工具增强型AI可从实验数据中学习并生成更优的领域相关干预方案，将行为实验从单次评估转变为可扩展的累积设计学习系统。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underutilization of experimental data in A/B testing, this study investigates whether tool-augmented agentic AI can autonomously learn from prior field experiments to design better interventions. In healthcare prescription messaging, two-stage field experiments compared human experts with a chatbot (Stage 1, 13 message variants) against an AI system that extracted principles from Stage 1 data to generate new variants (Stage 2, 17 variants). The AI method, enhanced with analytical tools and DIKW reasoning agents, produced superior results: its top message achieved a 69.8% CTR, a 6.5 percentage point gain over baseline. Crucially, the AI’s performance depended on domain-specific experimental data, not general reasoning, as frontier LLMs without such data failed to predict effective interventions. The findings also reveal that general behavioral theories do not universally apply to healthcare contexts, supporting agentic AI for theory audits at scale.
鉴于A/B测试中实验数据常被闲置，本研究探讨工具增强型智能体AI能否从过往实地实验中自动学习以设计更优干预措施。在医疗处方信息推送场景中，两阶段实地实验对比了人类专家与聊天机器人协作设计13种信息变体（第一阶段）与AI系统从第一阶段数据中自主提取原则生成17种新变体（第二阶段）的效果。AI方法表现更优，其最佳信息变体点击率高达69.8%，较基线提升6.5个百分点，表明领域特定实验数据而非通用推理能力才是成功关键。研究还发现通用行为理论难以推广至医疗场景，支持智能体AI作为可扩展的理论审计与累积设计学习工具。
A Lightweight Context-Driven Training-Free Network for Scene Text Segmentation and Recognition
Authors: Ritabrata Chakraborty, Shivakumara Palaiahnakote, Umapada Pal, Cheng-Lin Liu
First: 2025-03-19T18:51:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-01T16:29:43+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICDAR 2025 (ORAL) 21 pages, 8 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Modern scene text recognition systems often depend on large end-to-end architectures that require extensive training and are prohibitively expensive for real-time scenarios. In such cases, the deployment of heavy models becomes impractical due to constraints on memory, computational resources, and latency. To address these challenges, we propose a novel, training-free plug-and-play framework that leverages the strengths of pre-trained text recognizers while minimizing redundant computations. Our approach uses context-based understanding and introduces an attention-based segmentation stage, which refines candidate text regions at the pixel level, improving downstream recognition. Instead of performing traditional text detection that follows a block-level comparison between feature map and source image and harnesses contextual information using pretrained captioners, allowing the framework to generate word predictions directly from scene context.Candidate texts are semantically and lexically evaluated to get a final score. Predictions that meet or exceed a pre-defined confidence threshold bypass the heavier process of end-to-end text STR profiling, ensuring faster inference and cutting down on unnecessary computations. Experiments on public benchmarks demonstrate that our paradigm achieves performance on par with state-of-the-art systems, yet requires substantially fewer resources.Our code can be found here: https://ritabrata04.github.io/Context-driven-STR/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种轻量级上下文驱动的无训练网络，用于场景文本分割与识别
现代场景文本识别系统常依赖大型端到端架构，需大量训练且在实时场景中成本过高。为应对这一挑战，我们提出一种新颖的无训练即插即用框架，利用预训练文本识别器的优势并最小化冗余计算。该方法基于上下文理解，引入基于注意力的分割阶段，在像素级细化候选文本区域，提升下游识别效果。不同于传统文本检测方法（通过特征图与源图像块级比较并借助预训练图像描述器获取上下文信息），本框架可直接从场景上下文生成单词预测。候选文本经语义与词汇评估后获得最终得分，满足预设置信度阈值的预测可跳过端到端文本STR分析的繁重过程，实现更快推理并减少不必要的计算。在公开基准测试中，本方法性能媲美当前最先进系统，但资源需求显著降低。代码见：https://ritabrata04.github.io/Context-driven-STR/。
Summary / 总结
To overcome the high computational and memory demands of modern scene text recognition systems, this work introduces a training-free, context-driven framework that leverages pre-trained text recognizers and attention-based pixel-level segmentation to refine text regions without end-to-end training. By using contextual information from pretrained captioners and evaluating candidate texts semantically and lexically, the system generates word predictions directly from scene context and skips heavy inference when confidence thresholds are met. Experimental results on public benchmarks show that the method matches state-of-the-art accuracy while significantly reducing resource usage and inference latency.
为解决现代场景文本识别系统计算与内存开销过高的问题，本文提出一种无需训练的上下文驱动框架，利用预训练文本识别器和注意力机制进行像素级文本区域精炼，避免端到端训练。通过结合预训练的图像描述模型获取场景上下文，并对候选文本进行语义与词典评估，系统可直接生成词预测并在置信度达标时跳过繁重推理。实验表明，该轻量级方法在公开数据集上达到与当前最优方法相当的精度，同时大幅降低资源消耗和推理延迟。
